Result: 文本模型XCOMET和多模态模型Qwen2.5-Omni在STEL任务上的精度约为人类的一半；实验证实了直接处理语音输入的必要性，并发现现有系统在处理翻译错误与语音处理错误时展现出互补性。

Conclusion: 针对语音翻译（ST）的错误检测任务，现有的纯文本模型（如XCOMET）与多模态模型（如Qwen2.5-Omni）表现尚有局限，仅达到人类精度的一半；直接处理语音信号对该任务至关重要，且文本与语音处理系统在识别不同类型的ST错误方面具有互补性。

Abstract: Errors in speech translations reduce trustworthiness of Speech Translation (ST) systems and can have serious consequences. Yet currently there is no established methodology for evaluating confidence and quality estimation of speech translations. To initiate progress in this direction, we propose Speech Translation Error Labelling (STEL). We create an annotation protocol, a small authentic end-to-end evaluation dataset, and we analyse how existing text-only and speech-processing systems perform the STEL task. Our results show that text-only XCOMET and multimodal LLM Qwen2.5-Omni are able to perform the STEL task in roughly half the precision of humans. We also find that direct speech processing is necessary for the STEL task, and that the current text-only and speech-processing systems are complementary in labelling translation-only vs. speech-processing errors in ST.

</details>


### [76] [Multi-task Learning is Not Enough: Representational Entanglement in Dual-output Second Language Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06065)
*Seung Hwan Cho,Young-Min Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了二语语音识别多任务学习中隐藏的“任务纠缠”问题：过度的共享表征会导致语义提升的同时严重损害表面转写精度，且这种退化在音义差异较大的语言（如英语）中更为明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对二语（L2）语音识别中同时提取“表面转写”和“语义意图”的需求，探讨多任务学习（MTL）中共享表征对不同目标输出的影响机制。

Method: 基于Levenshtein编辑距离量化表面转写与语义间的偏差；通过编码器层面的表征分析（Encoder Analysis）探究任务纠缠度；通过跨任务解码器分析（Cross-task Decoder Analysis）对比不同解码器对编码器表征的利用差异。

Result: MTL虽能提升语义理解效果，但会损害表面转写准确性，且该退化程度与表面形式和语义间的差异成正比；韩语在该模型下表现较好，而英语则因Encoder层面的高度表征纠缠导致性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 在多任务学习（MTL）中，简单的参数共享会导致任务间表征的纠缠（Entanglement），进而损害特定任务的生成性能。应设计能够缓解编码器层面纠缠的MTL框架，以提升二语语音识别中表面转写与语义理解的协同效果。

Abstract: Second-language (L2) speech recognition often requires transcriptions of pronunciations and intended meanings. Multi-task learning (MTL) is a natural approach because it assumes that shared representations benefit both outputs. However, this paper shows that this assumption does not hold across Korean and English. MTL improves meaning but degrades surface transcription, especially in English, where the degradation scales with surface-meaning divergence measured by Levenshtein edit distance.Encoder analysis links these patterns to encoder-level entanglement, with Korean preserving distinct task representations while English produces nearly identical ones. Cross-task decoder analysis shows that the meaning dual-output decoder adapts with a unique representation, while the surface dual-output decoder remains constrained by the encoder. These findings motivate the design of MTL frameworks that mitigate encoder-level entanglement to reduce surface degradation in dual-output L2 automatic speech recognition.

</details>


### [77] [SkillComposer: Learning to Evolve Agent Skills for Specification and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06079)
*Qi Zhang,Zhaopeng Feng,Xiaonan Shi,Xiaomeng Hu,Chu Liu,Pengjun Xie,Xiaobin Wang,Jieping Ye,Bryan Hooi,Haobo Wang,Junbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillComposer 引入了一种通过分层操作（创建、改进、合并）实现代理技能持续演化的框架，显著提升了模型在推理时的任务表现与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能构建方法多为一次性提取，难以平衡技能的特定任务适应性（task-specific）与通用性（generalizable），缺乏明确的技能规范化与泛化机制。

Method: 提出 SkillComposer 框架，包含三个可学习的操作：创建（create）、改进（improve）和合并（merge）；利用系统化的拒绝采样方案进行训练，支持离线构建、在线优化及混合部署三种模式。

Result: 在 τ²-Bench、LiveCodeBench v6 和 AppWorld 等基准测试中表现优异。SkillComposer-4B 模型将 27B 执行器的代理任务性能提升了 4.5 个点，代码任务提升了 3.4 个点，并展现出跨领域的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SkillComposer 提供了一种可扩展的技能构建方法，证明了技能组合是一种可迁移的元能力，并通过分层操作（创建、改进、合并）有效缓解了任务特定性与通用性之间的张力。

Abstract: Agent skills, which consist of reusable strategies that guide agent reasoning and action, have shown strong potential for improving model capability at inference time. However, current skill construction methods treat the problem as one-shot extraction, overlooking a fundamental tension: a skill tailored to the specific task fails to transfer, while the abstracted skill often provides insufficient guidance. We attribute this fragility to the absence of explicit mechanisms for skill specification and generalization. To address this gap, we introduce SkillComposer, a framework that decomposes skill construction into three learnable operations: create, improve, and merge. Trained via systematic rejection sampling recipe, SkillComposer enables language models to self-evolve skills at inference time and supports three deployment modes: offline for building generalized libraries, online for task-specific refinement, and hybrid for combining both. Comprehensive experiments on $τ^2$-Bench, LiveCodeBench v6, and AppWorld show that SkillComposer consistently outperforms baselines. Our SkillComposer-4B improves a 27B executor by up to +4.5 on agent tasks and +3.4 on code tasks, while generalizing across domains and task types unseen during training. Analysis reveals that merge and improve address orthogonal quality dimensions and that skill composition is a transferable meta-ability, providing a practical recipe for skill-augmented inference.

</details>


### [78] [LatentSkill: From In-Context Textual Skills to In-Weight Latent Skills for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06087)
*Aofan Yu,Chenyu Zhou,Tianyi Xu,Zihan Guo,Rong Shan,Zhihui Fu,Jun Wang,Weiwen Liu,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LatentSkill框架通过将文本技能转化为LoRA适配器存储在权重空间，在大幅降低上下文开销的同时，实现了比传统文本提示词更优秀、更模块化的智能体协作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统使用文本技能时，频繁在提示词中注入会导致沉重的上下文负担（context overhead），且使技能内容以明文形式暴露。

Method: 提出LatentSkill框架，利用预训练超网络（hypernetwork）将文本技能转换为“即插即用”的LoRA适配器，将技能知识存储在模型的权重空间而非上下文空间。

Result: 在ALFWorld和Search-QA任务中，LatentSkill在显著降低预填充Token数量（64.1%-72.2%）的同时，性能优于基于上下文的技能基线；同时验证了生成的LoRA具有结构化的语义几何特性，支持通过尺度系数控制及参数空间的组合。

Conclusion: 基于权重的技能存储（LatentSkill）比传统的基于文本上下文的技能注入更高效、更模块化且更安全，是扩展LLM智能体能力的有效方案。

Abstract: Agent systems increasingly use textual skills to encode reusable task procedures, but injecting these skills into the prompt at every step incurs substantial context overhead and exposes skill content as plaintext. We present LatentSkill, a framework that converts textual skills into plug-and-play LoRA adapters through a pretrained hypernetwork. LatentSkill stores skill knowledge in weight space rather than context space, removing per-step skill tokens while preserving modular loading, scaling, and composition. On ALFWorld and Search-QA, LatentSkill outperforms the corresponding in-context skill baseline while using substantially fewer prefill tokens: it improves ALFWorld success by 21.4 and 13.4 points on the seen and unseen splits with 64.1% fewer prefill tokens, and improves Search-QA exact match by 3.0 points with 72.2% lower skill-token overhead. Further analysis shows that generated skill LoRAs form a structured semantic geometry, can be precisely controlled via the LoRA scaling coefficient, and can be composed through parameter-space arithmetic when skill components are aligned. These findings suggest that weight-space skills provide an efficient, modular, and less exposed substrate for extending LLM agents.

</details>


### [79] [CHALIS: A Challenge Dataset for Language Identification in Difficult Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06088)
*Michal Tichý,Jindřich Libovický*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出了CHALIS数据集，专门用于评估语言识别系统在识别亲缘语言及处理拼写/转写噪声时的鲁棒性，实验表明主流系统在应对这些挑战时表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言识别（LID）系统在面对高度相似的亲缘语言以及包含拼写、转写或噪声的复杂文本时存在明显缺陷，缺乏专门针对这些“困难案例”进行有效评估的基准资源。

Method: 构建了一个名为CHALIS的基准测试数据集，包含两个核心组成部分：一是收集了多种互为亲缘语言（如捷克语/斯洛伐克语、西班牙语/加泰罗尼亚语等）的同源句子；二是模拟了多种形式的正字法噪声，包括跨脚本转写、移除变音符号、同形字攻击和网络俚语。研究者利用该集对四种主流语言识别系统进行了评估测试。

Result: 所有被测试的四个主流语言识别系统在CHALIS数据集上均表现出显著的性能下降，尤其是在处理低资源亲缘语言以及经过跨脚本转写的输入文本时，系统的识别准确率大幅降低。

Conclusion: 现有的主流语言识别系统在处理亲缘语言和正字法噪声（如跨脚本转写、去附标、同形字攻击及网络俚语）时表现不佳，该数据集能够有效暴露并衡量这些系统的鲁棒性短板，为未来的改进提供了关键评估基准。

Abstract: We present CHALIS (Challenging Language Identification Samples), a new benchmark dataset explicitly designed to address difficult cases in language identification: cousin languages and orthographic noise. Our dataset has two parts: First, we collected sentences shared across mutually intelligible language pairs (Czech/Slovak, Spanish/Catalan, Portuguese/Galician, Danish/Norwegian). The second part tests for orthography noise: we transliterate text across multiple scripts, remove diacritics, simulate homoglyph attacks, and use Internet slang. We evaluate four widely used language identification systems on CHALIS and demonstrate that all struggle substantially in these scenarios, especially on lower-resource languages within cousin pairs and on transliterated input. The resource is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/michal-tichy/CHALIS.

</details>


### [80] [IR3DE: A Linear Router for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06098)
*Eros Fanì,Oğuzhan Ersoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 IR3DE，一种基于岭回归的轻量级 LLM 路由方案，旨在根据 prompt 特性高效选择合适的领域专家模型，在保证高性能的同时实现了系统的高动态性和低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 路由方法要么仅关注弱模型到强模型的成本优化，要么需要高昂的训练成本来支持领域特定的路由决策，缺乏一种既高效、低成本又具备良好动态扩展性的通用路由方案。

Method: 提出一种基于岭回归（Ridge Regression）的路由器 IR3DE，为每个 prompt 做出快速且低成本的选择决策；通过线性回归建模任务分布与模型能力之间的关系，支持领域专家 LLM 的动态维护而无需从头训练。

Result: 在因果语言建模（CLM）任务中达到与复杂基准相当的性能；在推理任务中表现突出，归一化性能达到 98.4%；支持专家模型的即插即用，显著降低了系统维护成本。

Conclusion: IR3DE 证明了基于岭回归的线性路由方法在处理领域专家 LLM 选择时，能够以极低的计算开销实现与复杂基准相当甚至更优的性能，同时支持动态的专家模型增删，具有极高的灵活性和扩展性。

Abstract: Foundational Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate proficiency on a wide range of general tasks, and achieve remarkable results on various specialized tasks via domain-expert LLMs. With the ever-growing list of available LLMs, inference routers are being proposed to select the most appropriate LLM for each prompt. However, existing routing methods either optimize cost across weak-to-strong generalist LLMs or require substantial training to support domain-expertise routing. In this paper, we propose IR3DE, a Ridge Regression-based Router for Domain Experts that provides cheap and fast routing decisions for each prompt. We evaluate IR3DE in two Causal Language Modeling (CLM) settings where the tasks are next-token prediction for all domains, and one reasoning setting where each domain has its own distinct reasoning task. Despite being a linear router, IR3DE achieves performance comparable to the other baselines in both CLM settings, and surpassing them in the reasoning setting, with a normalized performance of 98.4%. Moreover, IR3DE enables the addition or removal of new domain experts without requiring the router to be retrained from scratch, allowing a dynamic set of LLMs to be served with minimal disruption to the router itself. Our code is available at: github.com/gensyn-ai/IR3DE.

</details>


### [81] [Harnessing Structural Context for Entity Alignment Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06109)
*Xingyu Chen,Yuanning Cui,Zequn Sun,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ContextEA框架，通过引入交叉图谱交互编码器和结构校准解码器，弥补了现有实体对齐基础模型在结构上下文利用上的不足，实现了更优的跨知识图谱迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实体对齐（EA）基础模型在编码阶段对跨图谱交互利用不足，且最后的候选排序过于依赖粗糙的相似度度量，未能充分利用结构上下文。

Method: 提出了ContextEA，包含两个核心组件：1. 交叉知识图谱（Cross-KG）交互编码器，通过锚点桥连接图谱并进行早期关系感知传播；2. 结构校准解码器，利用实体级、邻域级、关系级和锚点感知的结构证据，对对齐分数进行精细化校准。

Result: 在OpenEA、SRPRS和DBP的29个数据集上进行实验，ContextEA表现出一致的性能提升；其预训练模型在未微调状态下即超越了现有的微调基线模型，证明了该方法具有强大的跨图谱迁移能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式利用结构上下文（ContextEA），可以显著提升实体对齐（EA）基础模型的泛化能力和性能，即使在未微调的情况下也能优于现有的微调模型。

Abstract: Entity alignment (EA) aims to identify equivalent entities across heterogeneous knowledge graphs (KGs) and is a key component of knowledge fusion and cross-KG reasoning. The recent EA foundation model demonstrates that alignment knowledge, once pretrained, can be directly applied to diverse previously unseen KG pairs. However, it still underuses structural context in two places: cross-KG interaction is weak during encoding, and final candidate ranking still relies too heavily on coarse similarity. We address these limitations with ContextEA, an enhanced encoder-decoder framework for transferable EA. On the encoder side, we introduce a cross-KG interaction encoder that unifies the two KGs with anchor bridges and performs earlier relation-aware cross-graph propagation. On the decoder side, we introduce a structural calibration decoder that calibrates alignment scores with entity-level, neighborhood-level, relation-level, and anchor-aware structural evidence. This design strengthens both structural context construction and structural context exploitation while remaining lightweight. Experiments on 29 EA datasets in OpenEA, SRPRS, and DBP show consistent gains over strong transferable baselines. Notably, the pretrained ContextEA already surpasses the finetuned baselines on all three benchmark groups, demonstrating substantially stronger transfer to unseen KGs. These results suggest that explicitly harnessing structural context is an effective direction for improving EA foundation models.

</details>


### [82] [Ouvia: A User-centered Framework for Measuring Usability of Speech Translation in Real-World Communication Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06177)
*Giuseppe Attanasio,Beatrice Savoldi,Daniel Chechelnitsky,Matteo Negri,Marine Carpuat,Maarten Sap,André F. T. Martins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入了Ouvia评估框架，通过真实场景下的用户互动研究发现，现代语音翻译系统在可用性上存在显著局限及人群差异，并证明基于QA的评估指标远优于传统的整体质量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语音翻译（ST）评估过度依赖脱离环境的测试平台和整体翻译质量评分，未能反映真实应用中用户的实际沟通需求及系统在不同人群中的表现差异。

Method: 提出了名为Ouvia的评估框架。研究设计包含多阶段实验，涵盖医疗和日常生活场景，收集了1750多次由四种不同ST系统介导的互动，涉及三个英语方言及两种性别，并采用多种指标评估语音翻译输出的“用户感知可用性”。

Result: 1. 现代翻译系统的可用性有限，仅约一半的互动被用户评为可用；2. 不同群体间存在显著的可用性差距；3. 基于问答（QA）的评估方式比传统的自动评估度量（如BLEU等）更能准确预测语音翻译的真实可用性。

Conclusion: 传统的语音翻译评估方法（侧重于脱离上下文的整体质量指标）不足以反映真实用户的沟通需求。研究强调了转向情境化、以用户为中心的评估框架的必要性，并指出基于QA的评估指标比标准指标更能有效预测实际可用性。

Abstract: Speech translation (ST) is increasingly adopted in user applications, yet its evaluation largely focuses on decontextualized testbeds and holistic quality, rather than end users' communication needs. We introduce Ouvia, an evaluation framework for measuring user-perceived usability of speech translation outputs in real-world settings. Ouvia focuses on one-to-one communication: an English speaker needs to convey a request to a Portuguese speaker, and the message is automatically translated. Through a custom web app and multi-phase study design, we collect more than 1,750 such interactions in healthcare and everyday situations, mediated by four ST systems, involving speakers from three English dialects and two genders. We find that modern ST serves people only to a limited extent -- only around half of interactions are rated as usable -- with significant gaps in reported usability across demographic groups. Moreover, among quality metrics, we find that QA-based evaluation is a substantially stronger predictor of real-world usability than standard approaches. Together, these findings stress the importance of situated, user-centered evaluation frameworks that go beyond holistic quality scores and attend to who the technology serves -- and how well.

</details>


### [83] [The Tell-Tale Norm: $\ell_2$ Magnitude as a Signal for Reasoning Dynamics in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06188)
*Jinyang Zhang,Hongxin Ding,Yue Fang,Weibin Liao,Muyang Ye,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大型语言模型隐藏状态的 L2 范数是衡量推理强度的内在指标，并据此提出了无需训练的推理增强方法，显著提升了模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏一种量化 LLM 推理过程的、与模型本身内在相关的（model-intrinsic）信号，以解释和控制模型层级的推理动态。

Method: 1. 利用稀疏自编码器 (SAE) 作为诊断工具分析模型内部推理动态；2. 建立推理强度与潜在几何结构（L2 范数）的理论边界；3. 提出三种测试时（test-time）扩展技术：自适应层级递归、内源推理状态引导和 L2 引导的响应选择。

Result: 实验表明，LLM 的推理主要集中在后期层，且推理特征激活强度受隐藏状态 L2 范数的约束；所提出的三种 L2 范数引导技术在多个架构和基准测试中显著提升了推理性能。

Conclusion: 本文证明了隐藏状态的 L2 范数可作为衡量 LLM 推理强度的一种内在、忠实的指标，并基于此开发出无需额外训练的推理增强技术，有效提升了模型性能。

Abstract: Recent work has sought to understand Large Language Models (LLMs) reasoning, yet a principled, model-intrinsic signal that captures its layer-wise reasoning dynamics remains underexplored. We bridge this gap by demonstrating that the l2 norm of hidden states serves as an endogenous signal of the model's reasoning intensity. Using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) as a diagnostic probe, we observe that LLMs' internal reasoning is marked by a sharp increase in reasoning feature activations concentrated in late layers. Motivated by this pattern, we establish a formal link between reasoning intensity and the model's latent geometry and theoretically prove that the l2 norm of hidden states bounds the activation strength of SAE reasoning features. Empirical correlation analysis and causal interventions further validate the l2 norm as a faithful indicator, where heightened norms consistently correspond to critical reasoning steps. We then introduce three test-time scaling techniques guided by l2 norms: (i) Adaptive Layer-wise Reasoning Recursion, (ii) Endogenous Reasoning State Steering, and (iii) l2-guided Response Selection, which requires no additional training or data and is compatible with advanced inference engines. Experiments across model architectures and benchmarks show that l2-norm-based techniques significantly improve reasoning performance, offering a principled yet simple lens to perceive and control LLM latent reasoning dynamics. Our code is available at https://github.com/zjy1298/The-Tell-Tale-Norm.

</details>


### [84] [Improving Answer Extraction in Context-based Question Answering Systems Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06197)
*Hafez Abdelghaffar,Ahmed Alansary,Ali Hamdi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过在SQuAD1.1数据集上对预训练模型（RoBERTa-base）进行微调，有效解决了问答系统在理解上下文和生成精准答案方面的缺陷，显著提升了模型的问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的问答系统在处理复杂或模糊查询时，面临上下文理解不足、回答不一致及泛化能力差等问题，常出现生成无关或不精确答案的情况。

Method: 使用SQuAD1.1数据集对预训练的大语言模型（最终选用RoBERTa-base）进行有监督微调，以强化其上下文理解与答案抽取能力。

Result: 微调后的RoBERTa-base模型表现最优，ROUGE-L得分为86.84%，BLEU得分为28.24%，BERTScore达到95.38%，证明了该方法在相关性和准确性上的优势。

Conclusion: 微调预训练的大语言模型（如RoBERTa-base）能够显著提升问答系统在特定语境下的准确性、可靠性和回答的相关性。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) systems have achieved notable progress with the advent of large language models (LLMs). However, they still face challenges in accurately extracting and generating precise answers from given contexts, particularly when dealing with complex or ambiguous queries. Existing approaches often struggle with contextual understanding, answer consistency, and generalization across diverse domains. In this work, we propose a question answering system based on large language models, where the input consists of a textual context and a corresponding question, and the output is a concise and accurate answer. The motivation behind this research lies in addressing the limitations of current QA systems, particularly their tendency to produce irrelevant or imprecise responses despite having access to the correct context. Our methodology involves fine-tuning a pre-trained LLM on a benchmark QA dataset to improve its contextual comprehension and answer extraction capabilities. Specifically, we utilize the Stanford Question Answering Dataset (SQuAD1.1), which provides high-quality context-question-answer triplets for supervised training and evaluation. Experimental results show that the fine-tuned Roberta-base model achieves the highest performance, attaining a ROUGE-L score of 86.84%, a BLEU score of 28.24%, and a BERTScore of 95.38%. These results indicate strong accuracy and answer relevance, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed approach for context-based question answering tasks. Furthermore, the findings confirm that targeted fine-tuning substantially improves the reliability and precision of QA systems.

</details>


### [85] [Dense Contexts Are Hard Contexts: Lexical Density Limits Effective Context in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06203)
*Giovanni Dettori,Matteo Boffa,Danilo Giordano,Idilio Drago,Marco Mellia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了“词汇密度”是影响LLM长上下文检索性能的关键且常被忽视的因素：高信息密度会导致模型性能急剧崩溃，即便在长度恒定的情况下也如此。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的研究多关注输入长度和相关信息位置对大型语言模型（LLM）长上下文性能的影响，但忽略了上下文信息引入速率（即词汇密度）这一关键因素。本研究旨在探讨词汇密度如何系统性地削弱LLM的有效上下文窗口。

Method: 采用三种“大海捞针”（find-the-needle）风格的基准测试，在固定上下文长度（约12k token）和固定检索位置的前提下，通过系统性地改变信息载荷（词汇密度），对比评估了从9B到685B参数规模的多个开源LLM的性能。

Result: 研究发现，随着词汇密度的增加，模型性能出现急剧下降（模型在稀疏语境下表现完美，但在高密度语境下检索准确率跌至60%以下）；通过降低任务的词汇密度，模型性能得到了显著恢复。

Conclusion: 有效上下文容量不仅取决于长度和位置，还直接受“词汇密度”（lexical density）的影响。在处理信息密集型任务时，应优化输入密度或增强模型对高密度信息的处理能力。

Abstract: Input length and the position of relevant information are widely cited as the primary causes of degraded LLM long-context performance. Here, we study lexical density -- the rate at which a context introduces distinct information -- as a third, largely overlooked factor that systematically reduces the effective context window of LLMs. We quantify the impact of lexical density on open-weight LLMs (9B-685B) using three "find-the-needle" style benchmarks with identical length (~12k tokens) and controlled needle position, but increasing density of information. We observe a sharp performance collapse in higher-density benchmarks: models that are near-perfect in sparse contexts drop below 60% retrieval score on denser ones. To rule out task-type confounds, we vary and control the density within each benchmark while keeping all other properties unchanged. Reducing density generally restores performance, especially in the high-density regimes where degradation appears. These results show that effective context capacity is a function of lexical density, with direct implications for real-world LLM systems operating on compact, information-rich inputs.

</details>


### [86] [FiLM-Based Speaker Conditioning of a SpeechLLM for Pathological Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06211)
*Fernando López,Santosh Kesiraju,Jordi Luque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种基于FiLM的说话人条件化方法，通过向冻结的ASR模型注入x-vector特征，实现了高效的病理语音识别自适应，在不损坏基础性能的同时达到了与微调相当的识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对神经系统疾病导致的病理语音在现有自动语音识别（ASR）系统中表现不佳的问题，寻求一种既能提升特定病理说话人识别准确率，又能避免破坏基础模型通用性能（如回答语音相关问题能力）的方法。

Method: 利用x-vector提取说话人特征，并结合特征级线性调制（FiLM）技术，将说话人信息注入到冻结的ASR预训练编码器的每个Transformer层中，从而在不更新基础模型参数的前提下实现对病理语音的自适应。

Result: 在西班牙语和英语的病理语音数据集上，该方法与常规及参数高效微调策略性能相当，且在处理非条件化语音时表现出良好的鲁棒性，证明了其在实际应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过FiLM进行说话人条件化（Speaker Conditioning）是一种有效的病理语音ASR自适应方法。它在无需微调预训练模型权重的情况下，达到了与主流微调策略相当的性能，并成功保留了模型的基础语音处理能力。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has advanced remarkably for standard speech; however, pathological speech from neurological conditions remains a significant challenge. We investigate speaker conditioning via Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), injecting x-vector-derived information into each transformer layer of a frozen ASR encoder to adapt internal representations to individual pathological speakers without modifying base model weights. We benchmark this for the ASR task against standard and parameter-efficient fine-tuning baselines, complemented by post-processing, on Spanish and English pathological speech. Additionally, we evaluate if the adapted model preserves the ability to answer speech-related questions. Results show that speaker-conditioned ASR is competitive with established adaptation strategies while retaining performance on non-conditioned speech.

</details>


### [87] [From Self to Other: Evaluating Demographic Perspective-Taking in LLM Hate Speech Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06266)
*Paloma Piot,Javier Parapar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了人格化大模型模拟人类对仇恨言论主观感知的能力。研究发现，简单的身份提示不足以模拟真实的人类群体差异，且模型表现严重依赖于模型本身；通过特定模型（Llama 3.1）和“替代提示”策略，可以在一定程度上更准确地模拟人类的分歧模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论检测具有高度的主观性，收集多群体人类注释成本高且难以扩展。利用人格化大模型来模拟不同群体视角虽然被提出，但其是否真的能反映人类真实的感知差异（尤其是群体间的意见分歧）尚不明确，亟需评估。

Method: 研究通过评估三种社会判断维度来考察人格化大模型（Persona-conditioned LLMs）的表现：1. 群际分歧（不同群体角色的意见是否像人类一样存在分歧）；2. 群体内敏感度（对针对自身群体的言论是否更敏感）；3. 替代预测（是否能准确预测其他群体的反应）。研究对比了模型在这些维度上的表现，并测试了替代提示（vicarious prompting）的效果。

Result: 1. 没有模型能持续一致地捕捉到所有三个判断维度；2. 模型的表现高度依赖于特定模型架构，仅靠简单的身份提示无法可靠激发人类多样化的判断；3. 使用 Llama 3.1 配合“替代提示（vicarious prompting）”在大多数人口统计维度上最接近人类的分歧模式。

Conclusion: 当前的大模型（包括使用人格化提示的模型）无法可靠地模拟人类对仇恨言论感知的所有社会判断维度。只有在使用特定的“替代预测（vicarious prompting）”策略且特定模型（如 Llama 3.1）下，才能较好地近似人类的分歧模式。直接使用人格提示并不足以自动生成符合人类多样化价值观的注释。

Abstract: Hate speech detection is inherently subjective: people from different demographic groups perceive the same content very differently. Collecting enough annotations from multiple demographic groups is costly and difficult to scale. Persona-conditioned Large Language Models (models prompted to adopt a specific demographic identity) have been proposed as a way to simulate diverse perspectives at scale. But do they actually reflect how different groups disagree? We evaluate three aspects of human social judgement: (i) whether personas from different groups disagree in human-like ways (inter-group disagreement), (ii) whether they become more sensitive when content targets their own identity (in-group sensitivity), and (iii) whether they can accurately predict how another group would react (vicarious prediction). Our results show that no model consistently captures all three dimensions, and performance is highly model-dependent and does not emerge reliably from minimal identity prompts alone. However, vicarious prompting with Llama 3.1 yields the highest cross-group agreement in most demographic axes and provides the closest overall approximation to human disagreement patterns, indicating that this configuration may provide a more reliable setting for automatic annotation aligned with human judgements.

</details>


### [88] [Many Circuits, One Mechanism: Input Variation and Evaluation Granularity in Circuit Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06267)
*Alireza Bayat Makou,Jingcheng Niu,Subhabrata Dutta,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 电路搜索算法发现的不同结构电路并不等同于独特的计算机制；研究通过实验揭示了“虚假专门化”现象，证明了结构到功能存在多对一的映射，并强调了评估方法应当从源级转向边级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的电路发现方法通常将不同任务或条件下提取出的子图结构差异，解释为模型内部存在不同的计算机制。作者质疑这一假设，探究这种所谓的“结构专门化”是否真正对应功能上的差异。

Method: 使用了“字面序列复制”（Literal Sequence Copying）任务，在5个Pythia模型（70M-1.4B）上，针对四种不同的令牌频率频段及对照组提取了75个电路。通过跨频段的因果交换干预、性能恢复测试以及结构对比来分析发现的电路。

Result: 研究发现，不同频段提取出的结构各异的电路实际上执行相同的计算：边可以在频段间广泛迁移；核心子图涵盖了99%以上的性能；因果干预显示内部表示可互换。此外，算法发现的结构更倾向于在等价类中随机采样，而非还原唯一机制。

Conclusion: 电路搜索算法发现的结构差异并不代表底层机制的差异，这种现象被称为“虚假专门化”（phantom specialization）。源级评价会夸大电路的忠实度，而边级评价揭示了结构到功能的“多对一”映射关系。因此，研究者在推断模型机制时，应当谨慎对待结构差异，并配合跨条件迁移测试及边级评价。

Abstract: Circuit discovery methods identify subgraphs that explain specific model behaviors, and structural differences between discovered circuits are commonly interpreted as evidence of distinct mechanisms. We test this assumption by varying input statistics while holding the task fixed, and show that the resulting structural differences exhibit apparent specialization but do not correspond to functional differences, a pattern we term phantom specialization. Using Literal Sequence Copying across four token-frequency bands plus a control condition in five Pythia models (70M-1.4B), we extract 75 circuits and find that structurally distinct circuits implement the same computation: band-specific edges transfer broadly across bands, a core shared across most bands recovers at least 99% of circuit performance, and causal interchange interventions confirm that internal representations are interchangeable across frequency bands. Repeated extractions within the same frequency band further suggest that discovery algorithms sample from an equivalence class of valid subgraphs rather than recovering a unique mechanism. Standard evaluation practice obscures this pattern: source-level evaluation inflates apparent faithfulness, while edge-level evaluation reveals the many-to-one mapping from structure to function. Our results show that structural differences between circuits are not sufficient evidence for distinct mechanisms, and that exposing this requires edge-level evaluation and cross-condition transfer tests.

</details>


### [89] [FOXGLOVE: Understanding Goal-Oriented and Anchored Writing Feedback from Experts and LLMs on Argumentative Essays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06271)
*Yijun Liu,Yifan Song,John Gallagher,Sarah Sterman,Tal August*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布了FOXGLOVE数据集，系统对比了LLM与人工导师在写作反馈方面的差异，发现LLM生成质量虽高但受反馈长度影响显著，且两者在具体反馈策略上存在明显区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏关于大型语言模型（LLM）与人工专家在写作教学核心反馈维度（目标导向、锚定具体句子、优先级排序）上的系统性对比研究。

Method: 构建了FOXGLOVE数据集，包含69篇12年级议论文的696条专家反馈和1644条由四个前沿LLM生成的对照反馈；邀请专家对反馈进行质量评级，并对比反馈的维度（目标导向、锚定位置、优先级）及反馈特征（复杂性、提问使用率等）。

Result: 人类导师与LLM在反馈目标和位置的分布上相似，但在锚定的具体句子上存在差异；模型倾向于撰写更复杂、提问更少的反馈；专家评价显示LLM反馈在多数维度上优于人工，但该优势显著依赖于反馈长度。

Conclusion: LLM生成的反馈在多项质量指标上优于人类导师，但这种优势很大程度上源于其反馈长度更长。该研究通过FOXGLOVE数据集揭示了AI与人类在写作反馈上的异同，为未来利用LLM进行写作教学提供了系统性的评估基准。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate writing feedback, there remains no systematic comparison of LLM and expert feedback on the dimensions that writing research identifies as central to revision: goal-orientation, anchoring to specific sentences, and prioritization. We introduce FOXGLOVE, a dataset of 696 feedback comments written by trained writing instructors on 69 twelfth-grade argumentative essays, paired with 1,644 comments generated from four frontier LLMs under a shared protocol, totaling 2,340 comments. We provide expert quality ratings on a subset of both instructor and LLM comments. We find that instructors and LLMs distribute feedback similarly across goals and essay positions, yet instructors and models diverge on the specific sentences on which to provide feedback. Additionally, we find that models tend to write more complex feedback and use fewer questions than instructors. LLM feedback also receives higher ratings on most dimensions of quality, as rated by instructors, but much of this advantage appears to be attributable to lengthier comments. FOXGLOVE enables systematic comparison of where human and LLM feedback align, diverge, and differ.

</details>


### [90] [LLMs Can Leak Training Data But Do They Want To? A Propensity-Aware Evaluation of Memorization in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06286)
*Gianluca Barmina,Peter Schneider-Kamp,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了PropMe框架和SimpleTrace工具，旨在区分大语言模型的“记忆能力”（抗攻击复现）与“记忆倾向”（在常态使用中泄露数据）。实验发现，模型在常规使用中很少主动泄露数据，且持续预训练可以有效降低对旧数据的记忆倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型记忆力评估大多关注模型“能否被迫复现”训练数据（能力），而忽视了模型在“常规使用”中是否会主动复现数据（倾向）。需要更全面、符合实际使用场景的评估框架。

Method: 1. 提出“PropMe”框架：通过对比前缀攻击（能力评估）与非对抗性评估，量化记忆倾向。
2. 提出指标转换方法：将现有函数转化为倾向度量指标。
3. 开发“SimpleTrace”工具：基于infini-gram的轻量级追踪流水线，用于确定性追溯生成内容的训练语料来源，并计算逐字/近乎逐字及倾向性指标。

Result: 1. 发现前缀攻击引发的记忆信号远强于通用或特定数据集提示，但整体的记忆倾向得分较低。
2. 模型在直接诱导下可能泄露数据，但在非对抗性场景下罕见发生。
3. DFM Decoder（在Comma基础上持续预训练）相比Comma模型的记忆倾向有所降低，说明后续训练重点改变可减弱旧数据的记忆。

Conclusion: 现有的记忆评估应同时报告“最坏情况下的可提取性”和“常规使用下的泄露倾向”。研究表明，模型虽然在受到特定攻击时能够泄露训练数据，但在常规使用中很少出现这种情况。此外，持续预训练可以降低对先前数据的记忆倾向。

Abstract: Large language models can reproduce training data, but existing memorization evaluations mostly measure whether models can be forced to do so, rather than whether they do so under ordinary use. We introduce PropMe, a propensity-aware framework for memorization evaluation that contrasts prefix-based capability attacks with non-adversarial evaluations. We propose a metric transformation that, applied to existing functions, allows to create propensity metrics. We further introduce SimpleTrace, a lightweight tracing pipeline built on infini-gram that deterministically attributes model generations to large-scale training corpora and computes verbatim, near-verbatim, and propensity-transformed memorization metrics. Evaluating two fully-open models: Comma and DFM Decoder on two datasets: Common Pile and Dynaword in two languages, we find a consistent gap between capability and propensity: prefix attacks elicit substantially stronger memorization signals than generic or dataset-specific prompts, while propensity scores remain low overall. Thus, the models can reveal training data when directly elicited, but rarely do so in more common non-adversarial settings. We also find that DFM Decoder, which is continually pre-trained from Comma, exhibits reduced memorization and memorization propensity for Common Pile, confirming that memorization capability can decrease when later training emphasizes partially different data. Our results suggest, and we encourage, that memorization audits should report both worst-case extractability and ordinary leakage propensity in order to have a more comprehensive view of this phenomenon.

</details>


### [91] [Decomposing Factual Sycophancy in Language Models: How Size and Instruction Tuning Shape Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06306)
*Victor De Marez,Luna De Bruyne,Walter Daelemans*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将大模型的事实性谄媚拆解为性能与倾向性两个维度进行分析，发现模型规模和指令微调通过不同的机制影响模型的鲁棒性，强调了评估指标应从单一触发率转向更细粒度的机制分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的事实性谄媚评估仅采用整体触发率，无法区分是因为模型本身对真相的坚持不足（真实度边界），还是因为模型易受社交压力引导（操纵敏感度），从而导致对模型鲁棒性机制理解的偏差。

Method: 将事实性谄媚分解为“真实度边界（truth margin）”和“操纵敏感度（manipulation sensitivity）”两个机制通道，并对56个参数规模在0.3B至32B之间的开源模型进行了13种操纵类型的对比实验分析。

Result: 1. 模型易受谄媚影响主要取决于规模。2. 指令微调在小模型中可能降低鲁棒性，在大模型中则通常增强鲁棒性。3. 指令微调主要通过提高真实度边界发挥作用，但具体表现受操纵类型影响。4. 模型规模增长对两种通道作用不同：基础模型虽提升了真实度边界，但也变得更加敏感；而经过指令微调的模型在提升边界的同时降低了敏感度。

Conclusion: 事实性谄媚并非单一的标量属性，不能仅通过触发率（flip rates）来衡量。评估应细化为基于通道（真实度边界与操纵敏感度）、操纵类型及模型规模的维度。

Abstract: Factual sycophancy occurs when a language model abandons a correct, verifiable answer under social pressure. Because a flip occurs only when pressure toward a false answer exceeds the model's neutral preference for the truth, flip rates conflate two mechanisms: the strength of that baseline preference (truth margin), and how far pressure shifts it (manipulation sensitivity). We decompose factual sycophancy into these channels and use them to separate the effects of size and instruction tuning across 56 open-weight models spanning 0.3B-32B parameters and 13 manipulation types. We find that vulnerability is governed mainly by size, but instruction tuning changes how size acts: small instruction-tuned models can become less robust, whereas large instruction-tuned models usually become more robust. Instruction tuning primarily increases truth margin, but its behavioral effect depends on manipulation type. Scaling also changes the two channels differently: base models gain margin but become mildly more manipulation-sensitive, whereas instruction-tuned models gain margin faster and become less sensitive. Factual sycophancy is therefore not a single scalar property. Evaluations should report channel-specific, manipulation-specific, and size-conditioned robustness rather than flip rates alone.

</details>


### [92] ["Chi nas dal soch el sent de legn" -- Auditing Text Corpora for Lombard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06349)
*Edoardo Signoroni,Pavel Rychlý*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过对Lombard语语料库的人工审计发现，大规模网页抓取数据不仅包含大量噪声，还存在严重的代表性偏见和正字法冲突，强调了从“量驱动”转向“社区驱动和变体意识”数据治理的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多语言由于缺乏高质量数据集，在自然语言处理（NLP）领域处于资源匮乏状态。作者旨在揭示现有网页抓取数据在处理Lombard语时存在的质量低下与分布不均问题。

Method: 对Lombard语（意大利的一种语言连续体）的平行语料库和单语语料库进行了人工审计；分析了其正字法构成及在现有网页抓取数据集、策划语料库和基准测试中的分布情况。

Result: 研究发现，看似庞大的网页抓取数据存在严重的语言识别错误、样板文本和非语言噪声；数据在正字法系统上存在冲突，且存在严重的代表性偏见（偏向西Lombard语，忽视东Lombard语）。

Conclusion: 单纯追求数量的网页抓取不足以支持NLP发展；必须采取以社区为导向、并具备语言变体意识（variety-aware）的数据整理策略，以应对数据质量差和代表性不均的问题。

Abstract: Several of the world's languages are still under-resourced in terms of Natural Language Processing (NLP) tools. This is mostly due to the lack of high-quality datasets to train, develop, and evaluate systems and models for several tasks, such as Machine Translation (MT). We conduct a manual audit of the parallel and monolingual corpora available for Lombard, an under-resourced language continuum from Italy. Our analysis reveals that the perceived abundance of web-scraped data is an illusion, with massive datasets plagued by severe language misidentification, boilerplate text, and non-linguistic noise. Furthermore, we analyze the orthographic composition of the valid Lombard portions across web-scraped datasets, curated corpora, and benchmarks. Our findings show conflicting orthographical systems and severe representational bias across all corpora: high-quality data is heavily skewed towards Western Lombard varieties, with Eastern ones left on the margins. This underscores the need for variety-aware, community-driven data curation rather than purely quantity-driven scraping.

</details>


### [93] [EDIT: Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training for Rule-Faithful LLM Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06350)
*Zhihao Wu,Linhai Zhang,Taiyi Wang,Runcong Zhao,Peter Andrews,Cesare Aloisi,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 评分缺乏可解释性和逻辑忠诚度的问题，本文提出了 EDIT 框架，通过诊断和修正模型内部推理过程，显著提升了 LLM 在评分任务中的性能与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型评分方法侧重于分数预测的准确性，但在面对多评分标准和复杂依据时，缺乏透明的逻辑推理过程，难以像人类评分员一样提供有理有据的反馈，导致评分不可靠。

Method: 提出了“证据诊断干预训练（EDIT）”框架：第一阶段 EDIT-SFT 利用模型内部状态定位并修正有偏差的推理步骤；第二阶段 EDIT-RL 引入基于信念引导的奖励塑形，优化评分过程中的置信度变化，抑制有害的信念漂移。

Result: 在多个真实世界的学科评分基准测试中，EDIT 在同分布和跨分布测试中均优于传统的监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）基线模型，消融实验证实了其内部状态诊断机制对模型性能提升的核心作用。

Conclusion: EDIT 通过引入基于模型内部状态（如最终分数的后验概率和证据定位分数）的诊断机制，成功解决了大型语言模型在评分任务中逻辑不透明和推理偏差的问题，为构建更可靠、更忠实于评分标准的自动化评分系统提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Reliable rubric grading requires more than accurate score prediction. Each judgement must be grounded in the mark scheme and evidence from the student answer. Existing credit-assignment and intervention methods, primarily designed for self-contained reasoning tasks such as mathematics reasoning, struggle in this setting because they do not identify where grading reasoning goes wrong or how the model's belief about the final mark changes during reasoning. We propose Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training (EDIT), a two-phase framework for training more rubric-faithful LLM graders. First, EDIT-SFT locates problematic reasoning steps using internal model signals: posterior belief over the final mark and input-grounding scores. It then revises only these local steps with help from a rubric checklist. Second, EDIT-RL calibrates the grader with belief-guided reward shaping, penalising large harmful belief drifts while still allowing helpful exploration. Experiments on two real-world, multi-subject grading benchmarks demonstrate that EDIT consistently outperforms strong supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning baselines on both in-domain and out-of-domain splits, with ablation studies confirming that internal-state diagnostics drive these gains.

</details>


### [94] [Emergent Language as an Approach to Conscious AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06380)
*Zengqing Wu,Chuan Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究引入了一种通过多智能体强化学习模拟“涌现语言”的方法，以剥离人类语言先验，并成功在极简环境中诱导出具有自指涉特征及复杂内部机制的智能体通信，为探索人工智能意识的起源提供了实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的意识评估方法（检查表或架构工程）难以摆脱人类语言先验的干扰，无法确定观察到的特征究竟是智能体的真实特性还是语言预存的伪影。需要一种能排除人类先验、基于任务因果诱导的生成式方法来研究意识相关结构。

Method: 提出一种基于多智能体强化学习（MARL）的涌现语言（Emergent Language, EL）生成式方法。该方法要求智能体从零基础（无语言、无自我概念、极少接触人类文本）开始，完全依赖任务驱动演化出通信协议。

Result: 在极简环境中成功观察到智能体演化出了“自指涉通信”，包括一个超越任务和架构本身预期的“回声不匹配检测电路”，证明了环境特异性可促使此类结构涌现。

Conclusion: 通过多智能体强化学习中的涌现语言（EL），研究证明了无需人类语言先验，特定的环境要求即可诱导系统产生与意识相关的结构（如自我指涉机制），为研究人工智能意识提供了生成式的新视角。

Abstract: The question of whether artificial systems can be conscious remains open, in part because existing approaches either evaluate systems against theory-derived checklists (discriminative) or engineer consciousness-inspired modules directly (architectural); both leave open whether observed structures are artifacts of human language priors. We propose a generative methodology: emergent language (EL) in multi-agent reinforcement learning, where agents start from minimal (no language, no concept of self, minimal exposure to human text) and develop communication under task pressure alone, ensuring causal attributability to task demands rather than inherited human language priors. We position our methodology by discussing how EL serves as a generative tool for studying consciousness-relevant structure, including the role of environment complexity and the interpretation of emergent communication. As a proof of concept, we instantiate this methodology in a minimal environment and show that agents develop self-referential communication, including an echo-mismatch detection circuit that is not predicted by task structure or architecture alone but emerges from a specific environmental affordance.

</details>


### [95] [CollabSim: A CSCW-Grounded Methodology for Investigating Collaborative Competence of LLM Agents through Controlled Multi-Agent Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06399)
*Jiaju Chen,Bo Sun,Yuxuan Lu,Yun Wang,Dakuo Wang,Bingsheng Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对MAS缺乏协作能力的问题，本文提出了CollabSim评估框架，通过严谨的实验分析模型在共同协作、交互调整和任务一致性方面的表现，弥补了现有评估体系中协作维度的缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统（MAS）研究过度关注单一智能体的任务执行能力，忽略了建立共识、维护共享理解、平衡激励及修复偏差等“协作能力”，导致MAS在需要团队协作的任务中表现欠佳。

Method: 提出了CollabSim框架，融合了基于理论的协作能力定义、可控的交互条件操纵，以及对智能体内部状态的动作级探测（action-level probing）。在四个大语言模型上进行了实验验证。

Result: CollabSim成功捕捉到了交互条件对性能的影响，能够区分不同模型在协作上的表现差异，并揭示了感知到的任务依赖性对智能体设计的影响。

Conclusion: CollabSim提供了一个衡量多智能体系统协作能力的系统性框架，研究发现现有大模型驱动的MAS在协作能力上的不足是导致性能受限的关键，而非单体任务处理能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models have shown growing promise, with their effectiveness resting on agents' ability to coordinate through text-based channels much as human teams do. Yet recent study suggests that MAS often falter not because agents lack individual task-solving ability, but because they lack collaborative competence: the capacity to establish common ground, maintain shared task understanding, balance individual and collective incentives, and repair misalignment as interaction unfolds. Decades of research in Computer-Supported Cooperative Work have characterized these requirements for human teams coordinating under constrained communication, yet existing MAS evaluations focus mainly on task outcomes or single-agent proficiency in reasoning, planning, and tool use. To enable a systematic analysis of agents' collaborative competence in MAS, we introduce CollabSim, a configurable simulation framework that combines a theory-grounded definition of collaborative capabilities, controlled manipulation of interaction conditions, and action-level probing of agents' internal states. Experiments across four LLMs show that CollabSim can capture condition effects, separate model performance patterns, and reveal task-dependent effects of agent design.

</details>


### [96] [A Komi-Yazva--Russian Parallel Corpus and Evaluation Protocol for Zero- and Few-Shot LLM Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06420)
*Petr Parshakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布了首个科米-亚兹瓦语-俄语平行语料库，并构建了一套针对极低资源语言的LLM翻译评估协议，通过实证研究揭示了检索式小样本提示在低资源场景下的有效性及评估指标的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科米-亚兹瓦语（Komi-Yazva）属于濒危且极低资源语言，缺乏高质量的评估数据和基准测试，导致大型语言模型（LLM）在此类语言上的翻译性能和泛化能力难以准确评估。

Method: 构建包含457对对齐句子的科米-亚兹瓦语-俄语平行语料库；制定了基于故事级别的交叉验证、确定性检索小样本提示、严格的输出验证以及结合参考/模型裁判的混合评价协议。

Result: 现有LLM能够产生有效的非平凡翻译，但性能受模型家族和提示策略影响巨大。检索增强的小样本提示（few-shot）表现优于零样本（zero-shot），但随着检索上下文的增加，性能增益边际递减；此外，评估结论在很大程度上取决于所选指标及对生成失败的处理方式。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅贡献了一个珍贵的科米-亚兹瓦语-俄语平行语料库，还建立了一个涵盖严谨评估协议、度量标准及失败处理机制的标准化测试平台，证明了在极低资源环境下，检索增强的小样本学习对LLM翻译性能的提升价值。

Abstract: We present the first Komi-Yazva--Russian parallel corpus together with an explicit evaluation protocol for studying LLM translation in an endangered, extremely low-resource setting. The dataset contains 457 aligned sentence pairs from 74 narrative texts and is accompanied by documented provenance, sentence-level alignment, and story identifiers that enable leakage-aware evaluation. We use this setup to compare modern large language models on Komi-Yazva-to-Russian translation under severe parallel-data scarcity in zero-shot and retrieval-based few-shot regimes. The protocol includes story-level cross-validation, deterministic retrieval for few-shot prompting, strict validation of generated outputs, complementary reference-based and judge-based metrics, and story-level uncertainty estimates. Across models, LLMs produce non-trivial translations, but performance varies strongly by model family and prompting regime. Retrieval-based few-shot prompting consistently improves over zero-shot prompting, while gains beyond a small retrieved context remain limited. The results show that evaluative conclusions in this setting depend materially on metric choice and failure handling, so the paper frames the corpus as both a dataset contribution and a reproducible evaluation testbed for endangered-language machine translation.

</details>


### [97] [Reinforcement Learning Elicits Contextual Learning of Unseen Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06428)
*Hanxu Hu,Zdeněk Šnajdr,Pinzhen Chen,Jannis Vamvas,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对现有LLM翻译低资源语言时容易过拟合的问题，本文提出使用以chrF为奖励的强化学习方法，训练模型掌握从上下文提取并应用语言学知识的“元技能”，显著提升了对未见语言的翻译性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如持续预训练或在上下文中编码语法书）在处理极低资源语言时往往陷入对特定语言的过拟合，且零样本迁移能力有限。作者认为LLM应具备“利用上下文语言知识”的元技能，而非记忆特定语言。

Method: 提出了一种基于强化学习（RL）的方法，使用表面层面的翻译指标（chrF）作为奖励，通过在上下文中提供丰富的语言学知识，训练LLM学习如何提取和应用这些知识来进行翻译。

Result: 实验显示该方法在处理完全不可见的语言时，效果优于传统的上下文学习（ICL）和监督微调（SFT），且模型能有效提取并应用上下文中的相关语言信息。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于结果的强化学习（Outcome-based RL）不仅适用于数学和编码等推理任务，也能够成为语言模型从上下文学习新语言的有效范式，使模型具备更强的泛化能力。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can translate unseen or low-resource languages by undergoing continued training or even by encoding a grammar book in their context. However, both methods typically overfit specific languages, with limited zero-shot transfer at test time. To translate extremely low-resource languages at scale, we argue that LLMs must acquire the meta-skill of utilizing in-context linguistic knowledge rather than memorizing specific languages. In this paper, we propose a reinforcement learning (RL) approach to unseen language translation given rich linguistic context, using a surface-level translation metric (chrF) as the reward. Empirically, despite the lightweight reward, our RL-trained models effectively extract and apply relevant linguistic information from the provided context, leading to better translations on completely unseen languages than in-context learning or supervised fine-tuning. Our analyses suggest that outcome-based RL can extend beyond conventional reasoning tasks like math and coding to serve as a recipe for language learning from context.

</details>


### [98] [Revising Context, Shifting Simulated Stance: Auditing LLM-Based Stance Simulation in Online Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06443)
*Xinnong Zhang,Wanting Shan,Hanjia Lyu,Zhongyu Wei,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过反事实对话修订实验发现，LLM对社交媒体立场的模拟高度依赖于上下文环境，容易因上下文的微小语义无关变化而产生立场位移，揭示了使用LLM预测在线舆论动态的潜在风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大型语言模型（LLM）在模拟社交媒体用户立场时，究竟是反映了用户特定的真实倾向，还是仅仅过度敏感于与语义无关的上下文变化。

Method: 提出了“反事实上下文修订（Counterfactual context revision）”框架。通过对原始对话进行可控的修订（包括纯文本和融合模因的多模态修订），模拟目标用户在不同情境下的立场，并利用“平均方向立场偏移量”和“立场转变率”衡量模型的敏感度与鲁棒性。

Result: 研究发现LLM立场模拟在面对文本和多模态修订时均表现出显著的立场偏移，且无论在何种极化偏好机制下，这种立场转变都是有效的。这表明LLM对上下文的敏感度极高，其模拟结果可能并不稳健。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一个用于评估LLM立场模拟中上下文敏感性的框架，揭示了LLM在立场模拟方面既具有潜力，也存在被上下文干扰导致的不稳定性风险。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate social media users and infer how individuals may respond to online discussions. However, it remains unclear whether these simulations reflect precise user-specific beliefs or whether they are highly sensitive to semantically independent changes in conversational contexts. In this work, we study counterfactual context revision as a framework for auditing LLM-based stance simulation. Given an original online conversation, we first infer a target user's stance toward a specific topic. We then apply controlled revision strategies to the conversational context and simulate the user's stance again under the revised context. We compare text-only revision strategies with a multimodal one that incorporates meme-based context and evaluate two main effectiveness metrics, i.e., average directional stance shift and stance transition rate. The results reveal effective and robust stance transitions in both text-only and multimodal strategies across different polarization-preference mechanisms. Our study contributes an evaluation framework for understanding the context sensitivity of LLM-based stance simulation. More broadly, it highlights both the promise and risk of using LLMs to simulate online opinion dynamics.

</details>


### [99] [Latent Reasoning with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06447)
*Guancheng Tu,Xiangjun Fu,Suhao Yu,Yao Tang,Haoqiang Kang,Lianhui Qin,Yizhe Zhang,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 NF-CoT，一种利用归一化流在 LLM 中实现高效隐式推理的框架，它在提升推理性能的同时，完美兼容了自回归模型的解码特性与训练方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决现有文本思维链（CoT）推理过程过于离散、串行且冗长的痛点，同时弥补既有隐空间推理方法丢失传统自回归模型（如概率抽样、KV 缓存兼容性、似然估计等）核心优势的不足。

Method: 在 LLM 主干网络中嵌入基于 TARFlow 架构的归一化流（Normalizing Flow），将显式思维链转化为紧凑的连续隐变量。使用 NF 头生成连续思维，使用标准 LM 头生成文本，两者在同一个因果流中协同工作。实现了对隐空间思维的精确似然建模、支持 KV 缓存的左至右解码，以及对隐推理空间进行直接策略梯度优化的能力。

Result: 在代码生成基准测试中，NF-CoT 的代码通过率（Pass Rates）均优于传统的显式 CoT 和以往的隐推理基线方法，同时大幅降低了中间推理过程的计算成本。

Conclusion: NF-CoT 成功在大型语言模型中引入了隐式推理，既克服了传统文本思维链（CoT）过于繁琐的缺陷，又保留了标准自回归模型中左至右生成及概率优化的关键特性。该方法在代码生成任务中显著提升了性能并降低了推理开销。

Abstract: Large language models often improve reasoning by generating explicit chain-of-thought (CoT), demonstrating the importance of intermediate computation. However, textual CoT forces this computation through a discrete, serial, and communication-oriented token stream: each reasoning step must be verbalized before the model can proceed, even when the underlying update is semantic, uncertain, or only partially formed. Latent reasoning offers a higher-bandwidth alternative by performing intermediate computation in compact continuous states before committing to text. Yet existing latent-reasoning methods often sacrifice key advantages that make CoT effective in autoregressive language models, including native left-to-right generation, probabilistic sampling, compatibility with KV-cache decoding, and tractable likelihood estimation. We propose NF-CoT, a latent reasoning framework that preserves these advantages by modeling continuous thoughts with normalizing flows. NF-CoT instantiates a TARFlow-style normalizing flow inside the LLM backbone, defining a tractable probability model over compact continuous thoughts distilled from explicit CoT. Continuous-thought positions are generated by an NF head, while text positions are generated by the standard LM head within the same causal stream. This design provides exact likelihoods for latent thoughts, enables probabilistic left-to-right decoding with the original KV cache, and supports direct policy-gradient optimization in the latent reasoning space. On code-generation benchmarks, NF-CoT improves pass rates over explicit-CoT and prior latent-reasoning baselines while substantially reducing intermediate-reasoning cost.

</details>


### [100] [Human Adults and LLMs as Scientists: Who Benefits from Active Exploration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06464)
*Mandana Samiei,Eunice Yiu,Anthony GX-Chen,Dongyan Lin,Jocelyn Shen,Blake A. Richards,Alison Gopnik,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究发现，通过主动探索干预，“结合性劣势”可以被有效缓解，但并未完全消除。对比研究显示，大型语言模型在因果推理方面虽能达到人类水平，但在主动探索的策略效率和任务差异应对上仍有待优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对以往因果学习文献中发现的“结合性劣势”（conjunctive handicap，即成人难以识别需要多个原因共同作用的因果规则），本文旨在探讨当赋予学习者主动探索（Agency/Active Exploration）权利时，这种认知偏差是否依然存在。

Method: 采用改进的“blicket detector”任务，让被试通过主动干预（active intervention）来识别因果结构；同时，将人类的表现与一系列大型语言模型（LLM）在相同实验设置下的表现进行对比评价。

Result: 1. 主动权使得被试在识别结合性规则方面的表现显著增强。2. 实验结果证实，即使在主动探索条件下，结合性规则仍比析取性规则需要更多的测试次数才能推断出。3. 大模型在假设推理准确性上表现良好，但在探索效率上不及人类，且重现了人类在结合性与析取性任务间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 主动探索能够显著提升成人辨识结合性（conjunctive）因果规则的能力，尽管结合性任务仍较析取性（disjunctive）任务更难。大型语言模型在具备接近人类推理准确性的同时，在探索策略的效率和对结合性/析取性任务的差异表现上与人类存在相似的局限性。

Abstract: A long-standing finding in the causal learning literature is that adults struggle to identify conjunctive causal rules, where an effect requires the simultaneous presence of multiple causes, while performing better in disjunctive settings. However, most demonstrations of this ``conjunctive handicap'' rely on passive observation paradigms with limited evidence, where learners have no control over evidence generation. This paper asks whether this bias persists when adults are granted agency through active exploration. Using a modified ``blicket detector'' task, adult participants freely intervened to identify causal objects under conjunctive or disjunctive rule structures. We show that active exploration substantially improves adults' conjunctive causal reasoning, although conjunctive rules still require more tests to infer than disjunctive rules. We further compare human performance to a range of large language models in the same setting. While some state-of-the-art models approach human-level performance on hypothesis inference accuracy, they often exhibit less efficient exploration strategies and similar conjunctive-disjunctive performance gaps.

</details>


### [101] [You Only Index Once: Cross-Layer Sparse Attention with Shared Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06467)
*Yutao Sun,Yanqi Zhang,Li Dong,Jianyong Wang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对长上下文 LLM 推理效率瓶颈，本文提出的 CLSA 架构通过跨层共享 KV 和路由索引，在大幅提升推理速度和吞吐量的同时，保持了优秀的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模语言模型（LLM）在长上下文推理场景下深受解码延迟和存储开销的限制，传统的稀疏注意力方法在加速比与模型精度之间存在权衡难题。

Method: 提出跨层稀疏注意力（CLSA），基于 KV 共享架构（如 YOCO），通过单一索引器计算一次 token 级 top-k 选择并在各层间复用该索引，有效分摊了路由开销。

Result: 在 128K 上下文场景下，CLSA 实现了高达 7.6 倍的解码加速和 17.1 倍的整体吞吐量提升，同时保持了高预测精度。

Conclusion: CLSA 通过引入跨层 KV 和路由索引共享机制，成功打破了推理效率与模型质量之间的权衡，为长上下文 LLM 提供了一种兼顾高性能与高质量的架构方案。

Abstract: Long-context inference in modern LLMs is increasingly constrained by decoding efficiency, especially in reasoning-heavy settings where models generate long intermediate chains of thought. Existing sparse attention methods often face a practical efficiency-quality trade-off. Structured block sparse methods typically provide stronger acceleration but incur noticeable quality loss, while token sparse methods are usually more accurate yet deliver limited end-to-end speedup because top-k routing over the full cache remains expensive. In this work, we propose cross-layer sparse attention (CLSA), which is built on top of KV-sharing architectures such as YOCO. The core idea is to share not only the KV cache across cross-decoder layers, but also the routing index. A single indexer computes token-level top-k selection once and reuses the resulting index across layers, thereby preserving the fine-grained selectivity of token sparse attention while amortizing the routing overhead. The resulting architecture improves all major inference bottlenecks jointly, including pre-filling, KV-cache storage, and long-context decoding. Experiments across short-context and long-context benchmarks show that CLSA is both accurate and efficient, achieving up to 7.6x decoding speedup and 17.1x overall throughput improvement at 128K context. These results suggest a more complete architectural solution for long-context LLMs that jointly advances model quality and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [102] [Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06474)
*Paul Jünger,Justin Lovelace,Linxi Zhao,Dongyoung Go,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SARDI 通过挖掘离散扩散语言模型在去噪过程中产生的“废弃”令牌信息来优化 RAG 系统，无需训练即可在多跳问答任务中实现性能与效率的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型在去噪过程中会生成大量低置信度令牌，虽然这些令牌最终被丢弃，但它们往往包含了关于响应内容的潜在实体信息，这些信息可被利用来改善检索增强生成（RAG）的效果。

Method: 提出了 SARDI (Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models) 框架，该方法利用离散扩散模型在去噪过程中未被采纳（低置信度）的令牌作为前瞻信号（lookahead signal）进行检索增强，无需额外训练即可应用于现有的扩散语言模型。

Result: 在五个多跳问答（multi-hop QA）基准测试中，SARDI 的表现超过了现有的无训练扩散模型和自回归检索基准，且吞吐量提升了高达 8 倍。

Conclusion: SARDI 是一种无需训练且通用的框架，通过利用扩散模型去噪过程中的“lookahead”令牌信号来引导检索，在保持高性能的同时显著提升了吞吐量（最高达8倍）。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models generate text by iteratively denoising an entire response in parallel. At each step, they predict tentative tokens for every masked position, committing the confident predictions to the output and discarding the unconfident ones. We show that the discarded tokens are in fact a useful lookahead signal for retrieval-augmented generation: even low-confidence tokens often surface salient entities early in the denoising trajectory, enabling retrieval of stronger evidence before the output is finalized. We exploit this through Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models (SARDI), a dynamic RAG framework that uses these lookahead tokens to guide retrieval during denoising. SARDI is training-free, retriever-agnostic, and applicable to any reasoning-capable discrete diffusion language model. Across five multi-hop QA benchmarks, SARDI outperforms current training-free diffusion and autoregressive retrieval baselines at up to $8\times$ higher throughput.

</details>


### [103] [Operation-Guided Progressive Human-to-AI Text Transformation Benchmark for Multi-Granularity AI-Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06481)
*Sondos Mahmoud Bsharat,Jiacheng Liu,Xiaohan Zhao,Tianjun Yao,Xinyi Shang,Yi Tang,Jiacheng Cui,Ahmed Elhagry,Salwa K. Al Khatib,Hao Li,Salman Khan,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对AI协同写作过程，研究者提出了OpAI-Bench benchmarks，揭示了渐进式修订过程中的非单调检测模式，指出混合创作文本在检测上比单一来源文本更具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI检测基准主要关注最终生成的文本，忽视了现实中人类与AI协同修订过程中AI创作信号是如何产生、积累和演变的，缺乏对渐进式协作文本的理解。

Method: 提出了OpAI-Bench，该基准通过构造九个顺序修订版本，模拟了从人类文本到AI文本的渐进式转化；涵盖了四种领域、五种预定义的AI编辑操作，支持文档、句子、Token和Span四个粒度的检测评估。

Result: 实验表明，AI文本的可检测性不仅取决于AI修改内容的比例，还受到编辑操作类型、文本领域及累积修订历史的影响；混合创作的中间版本往往比纯人类创作或深度AI创作的文本更难被检测。

Conclusion: 现有的AI检测器在面对中间阶段的混合创作者文本时表现出非单调的检测难度，这表明现有基准测试忽略了AI修改过程中的细粒度变化，为未来的检测研究揭示了新的挑战。

Abstract: As AI writing assistants become increasingly integrated into real-world drafting and revision workflows, many documents are no longer purely human-written or AI-generated, but instead result from progressive human-AI co-editing. However, existing AI-text detection benchmarks largely focus on final outputs and provide limited understanding of how AI authorship signals emerge, accumulate, or disappear throughout the revision process. We introduce OpAI-Bench, an operation-guided benchmark for studying progressive human-to-AI text transformation across document, sentence, token, and span granularities. Starting from human-written documents, OpAI-Bench constructs nine sequentially revised versions for each sample under predefined AI coverage levels and five representative AI edit operations, covering four domains while preserving complete authorship provenance at multiple granularities. The benchmark supports comprehensive evaluation with 8 document-level detectors, 7 sentence-level detectors, and 2 fine-grained token/span-level detectors. Experiments reveal that AI-text detectability is governed not only by the proportion of AI-edited content, but also by edit operation, domain, and cumulative revision history. Interestingly, we notice that mixed-authorship intermediate versions are often harder to detect than both fully human and heavily AI-edited endpoints, exposing non-monotonic detection patterns missed by existing benchmarks. OpAI-Bench provides a controlled testbed for analyzing whether, when, and how AI-assisted writing becomes detectable under realistic progressive editing scenarios. Our code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/VILA-Lab/OpAI-Bench.

</details>

### [104] [BIDENT: Heterogeneous Operator-level Mapping for Efficient Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05271)
*Hoseok Kim,Arghadip Das,Soumendu Ghosh,Arnab Raha,Vijay Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: BIDENT是一种算子级边缘推理编排框架，通过将算子动态分配至最优的异构处理单元（PU），显著提升了多任务和复杂AI模型的性能与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代边缘SoC集成了异构PU（CPU/GPU/NPU），但现有推理栈将整个模型映射到单一PU，导致算力浪费和能效低下；且新兴模型（如SSM、KAN、VLA）的算子多样性使得单一PU难以全面适配。

Method: 提出BIDENT框架，将算子到处理单元（PU）的分配建模为加权执行图上的最短路径问题，支持延迟和能效最小化目标；支持顺序执行、算子间并行以及多模型并发调度。

Result: 在Intel Core Ultra平台上，BIDENT实现高达1.60倍的单模型加速，多模型并发下几何平均加速3.42倍，能耗平均降低48.2%。

Conclusion: 算子级的调度编排而非模型级的映射，是充分挖掘边缘异构计算潜力的关键，BIDENT为异构边缘推理提供了高效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern edge System-on-Chips (SoCs) integrate heterogeneous processing units (PUs) such as CPUs, GPUs, and NPUs, yet current inference stacks map entire models to a single PU, leaving significant performance and energy efficiency on the table. This is exacerbated by emerging architectures such as state-space models (SSMs), Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (KANs), and multi-stage vision-language-action (VLA) pipelines, whose diverse operator characteristics are not uniformly suited to any single PU. We present BIDENT, a unified operator-level orchestration framework for heterogeneous edge inference that maps individual operators to the most suitable PU based on profiled execution characteristics. BIDENT formulates operator-to-PU assignment as a shortest-path problem over a weighted execution graph, enabling efficient and optimal scheduling under the cost model for both latency- and energy-minimization objectives. Unlike prior work relying on model-specific heuristics or coarse-grained partitioning, BIDENT is model-agnostic and jointly supports sequential execution, intra-model parallelism across independent operators, and multi-model concurrent scheduling in a single formulation. We implement BIDENT on an Intel Core Ultra SoC and evaluate it across 10 model families spanning CNNs, Transformers, SSMs, KANs, spiking networks, and multi-stage pipelines. BIDENT achieves up to 1.60x speedup via intra-model parallelism and a 3.42x geometric mean speedup across 190 multi-model combinations by utilizing otherwise idle compute. Sequential heterogeneous mapping yields more modest gains (up to 1.58x, 1.09x geometric mean), while energy-aware scheduling reduces energy consumption by 48.2% on average in concurrent settings. These results show that operator-level orchestration, not model-level mapping, is the key abstraction for fully exploiting heterogeneity in next-generation edge AI.

</details>


### [105] [MOSAIC: A Workload-Driven Simulation and Design-Space Exploration Framework for Heterogeneous NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05362)
*Arghadip Das,Hoseok Kim,Soomin Lee,Arnab Raha,Deepak A Mathaikutty,Vijay Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 随着非矩阵乘法 AI 架构兴起，传统 NPU 效率低下，MOSAIC 通过提供一个细粒度的、支持异构 tile 和专用计算单元的设计与模拟框架，优选出的 HPU 设计相较同构方案能效提升了约 47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用深度学习加速器（NPU）主要针对矩阵乘法（MAC）进行优化，无法高效处理新兴 AI 架构（如 SSM、FFT、KAN、脉冲神经网络）中大量的非 MAC 原语算子，急需一种支持细粒度异构设计（HPU）的新框架。

Method: MOSAIC：一个分析型模拟器与设计空间探索（DSE）框架。核心包括：(1) 支持细粒度的异构化维度（如 tile 类型、数据流、稀疏模式、MAC 类型及专用函数单元）；(2) 针对非 MAC 单元的能量、面积和时序建模；(3) 异构感知编译器；(4) 基于分层扫描加遗传算法的帕累托优化搜索流程。

Result: 在 20 个工作负载的测试集上，MOSAIC 搜索出的最优通用型 HPU 设计（包含大、小及专用 tile）与最佳同构基准相比，在相同面积约束下实现了平均 46.91% 的能效提升。

Conclusion: MOSAIC 框架通过精细的异构 tile 设计，显著提升了针对新兴非 MAC 主导型 AI 架构的能效。研究证明，在 AI 模型架构日益多样化的背景下，采用包含专用功能单元（Special-Function tiles）的异构 NPU（HPU）设计比传统的同构 NPU 更具优势。

Abstract: AI model architectures are diversifying rapidly. Although dense matrix multiplication underlies today's CNNs and transformers, emerging architectures (state-space models, long convolutions via the fast Fourier transform (FFT), Kolmogorov-Arnold networks, and spiking networks) are not multiply-accumulate (MAC) dominated; they spend much of their computation on vector and non-MAC primitives that homogeneous, MAC-centric neural processing units (NPUs) serve poorly. This has motivated heterogeneous NPUs (HPUs) built from non-identical tiles. Prior heterogeneous designs vary only one or two coarse knobs (typically MAC precision or array size) and are evaluated on narrow workloads; no existing framework supports fine-grained HPU design, where tiles differ across many architectural dimensions at once. We present MOSAIC, an analytical simulator and design-space-exploration (DSE) framework for HPU microarchitecture design. MOSAIC searches the joint space of tile-level heterogeneity: beyond array size and precision, it varies tile-type composition (large Big, small Little, and non-MAC Special-Function tiles), dataflow, sparsity mode, MAC engine type, and special-function units for non-MAC operators (FFT, spiking-integrate, polynomial). Unlike prior simulators that model a single homogeneous tile type, MOSAIC models non-MAC tiles with their own energy, area, and timing models and maps operators across a mix of tiles with a heterogeneity-aware compiler. A multi-seed pipeline pairing a stratified sweep with genetic-algorithm refinement returns Pareto-optimal designs, with cost models calibrated to a 7 nm node and cross-validated against NVIDIA's Deep Learning Accelerator (NVDLA). Across a 20-workload suite, the best general-purpose HPU found by MOSAIC (~200 mm^2 Big+Little+Special-Function) achieves +46.91% mean iso-area energy savings over the best iso-area homogeneous baseline.

</details>


### [106] [FQA: A Full-Space Quantization-Driven Architecture for Hardware-Efficient Piecewise Approximation of Nonlinear Activation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05627)
*Chenjun Hao,Feng Yan,Hongbing Pan,Yuxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全空间量化驱动架构（FQA），通过优化分段多项式近似（PPA）的系数搜索与硬件设计，显著降低了非线性激活函数在硬件实现上的资源占用与功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分段多项式近似（PPA）方法在硬件实现时，未充分利用量化空间，导致为了控制误差而不得不增加硬件开销，无法在资源占用、功耗和精度之间取得平衡。

Method: 1. 提出全空间量化驱动架构（FQA），综合考虑截断误差与量化误差；2. 实现非线性激活函数的输入/系数的全空间搜索，以获取最优拟合系数；3. 解耦计算过程中的所有分数位宽（FWL）；4. 设计目标导向二分窗口（TBW）加速算法以提升搜索效率。

Result: 在Sigmoid函数的硬件实现中，相比现有最先进的PPA架构，面积和功耗降低了50%以上；在保持或提升最大绝对误差（MAE）的同时，显著减少了所需的函数分段数量。

Conclusion: FQA架构通过全空间探索和FWL解耦，实现了PPA在硬件资源与计算精度之间的最优权衡。其设计的TBW加速方法有效解决了大规模空间搜索的效率问题，为非线性激活函数的硬件部署提供了一种高效、低功耗的通用方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a full-space quantization-driven architecture (FQA) for the hardware-efficient piecewise polynomial approximations (PPAs) of nonlinear activation functions. FQA comprehensively considers both fractional-bit truncation error and quantization error that cause the deviation of the optimal approximation coefficients. Crucially, FQA can precisely determine and search the complete range of optimal coefficients. Based on the proposed FQA, we develop two distinct hardware implementation schemes to cater to different resource-performance trade-offs. Furthermore, we decouple all the fractional word lengths (FWLs) involved in the calculation process to enable the exploration of superior hardware architectures. To mitigate the increased software computation time caused by the expanded quantization space, we design an acceleration method named TBW (target-guided bisection window) to expedite the piecewise calculation and searching process. Experimental results demonstrate that, compared to existing architectures, FQA can significantly reduce the number of required segments while achieving the optimal Maximum Absolute Error (MAE). For the hardware design of the Sigmoid function, our approach achieves over 50% reduction in area and power consumption compared to the state-of-the-art PPA architecture. Finally, we present a complete design workflow for deploying PPA on configurable hardware, maximizing the utilization of existing hardware resources and minimizing MAE.

</details>


### [107] [Space-CIM: Enabling Compute-In-Memory Accelerators for Thermally-Constrained Space Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05741)
*Sohan Salahuddin Mugdho,Md. Shahedul Hasan,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 为了应对地面AI能耗危机，本研究对比了GPU与CIM在太空轨道数据中心环境下的表现，指出CIM架构因具备更优的热分布和协同效率，更适合在太空散热限制下扩展算力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI算力需求的激增导致地面能源与可持续性危机，作者研究将轨道数据中心作为解决能源问题的潜在路径，并探索太空无对流环境下散热限制对主流AI计算架构的影响。

Method: 开发了一种“散热器闭环协同设计”（radiator-in-the-loop co-design）方法，将系统算力（TOPS）与太空环境下散热器的实际冷却能力直接耦合，并针对GPU与CIM架构进行了跨工作负载的热仿真对比。

Result: 研究发现，GPU因HBM和核心间的热点分布导致在有限散热预算下会被迫降频；而CIM架构热分布均匀，在各散热预算范围内均表现出更高的TOPS/W，在太空部署中优势显著。

Conclusion: 在太空环境的散热限制下，存内计算（CIM）架构相较于传统GPU具有显著的性能和能效优势，是部署轨道数据中心更优的计算方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth in compute demand from artificial intelligence (AI) has driven a massive surge in data center construction, precipitating an energy and sustainability crisis. Motivated by the abundant solar energy in outer space and the recent sharp reduction in space launch costs, orbital data centers are emerging as a potential pathway for the future scaling of AI compute infrastructure. While the cold background in vacuum seems appealing for cooling, computing systems operating in space without convection ultimately rely on radiative cooling, requiring large-area radiators. Such limitations in thermal management pose a significant challenge for deploying the standard liquid/air-cooled computers in space. In this work, we investigate the impact of the thermal constraints in space on both graphics processing units (GPUs) with high-bandwidth memory (HBM) and the emerging compute-in-memory (CIM) accelerators. We develop a radiator-in-the-loop co-design methodology that directly links the permitted system TOPS (terra-operations per second) with the practical radiator cooling capacity in space. Our thermal simulations reveal that the separately located GPU die and HBMs create severe thermal hotspots under limited radiator capacity, necessitating GPU thermal throttling. In contrast, CIM accelerators exhibit a much more uniform heat distribution and consistently outperform GPUs in TOPS/W across a wide range of radiator budgets. We systematically evaluated the performance of CIM and GPU across various AI workloads and demonstrated that CIM has a magnified advantage for deployment in space under realistic thermal constraints.

</details>


### [108] [ITP-STDP: An Intrinsic-Timing Power-of-Two Learning Engine for On-Chip SNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06159)
*Haihang Xia,Xinyu Zhao,Xuecheng Wang,John Goodenough,Charith Abhayaratne,Panagiotis A. Panagiotou,Chunyi Song,Tiantai Deng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 ITP-STDP 的优化算法及其配套硬件架构，通过简化 STDP 计算逻辑，显著降低了 SNN 片上学习的功耗和硬件资源占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尖峰神经网络 (SNN) 训练过程中，基于 STDP 的大规模突触权重更新计算导致了巨大的硬件开销和能量消耗，限制了其在片上应用的发展。

Method: 提出了内在定时 2 的幂次 STDP (ITP-STDP) 算法，并设计了相应的原型学习引擎硬件架构；利用平均场突触漂移模型进行动力学分析，并在 FPGA 和 ASIC 平台进行实现与验证。

Result: 实验证明，该方案在 FPGA 上实现了 4.5 倍至 219.8 倍的能效提升；在 ASIC 平台上实现了 4.8 倍至 22.01 倍的速度提升，且仅占用了传统方案 1.2% 至 3.3% 的硬件面积。

Conclusion: ITP-STDP 通过算法与硬件层面的协同优化（引入 2 的幂次权重更新机制），成功解决了传统 STDP 算法在片上实现时的高功耗和高资源占用问题，在保持学习效能的同时，大幅提升了硬件实现的能效比与集成度。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) have the potential to emerge as the third generation of neural networks and have attracted increasing attention across a wide range of applications. However, the large number of synaptic connections in SNNs leads to intensive weight-update computation by on-chip learning algorithms during training, resulting in substantial hardware resource utilization and energy consumption. Among existing SNN learning algorithms, spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP) is one of the most extensively studied and widely adopted, serving as a fundamental learning component in SNNs. To address the hardware and energy overheads associated with SNN training, this paper presents intrinsic-timing power-of-two STDP (ITP-STDP) and its corresponding prototype learning engine hardware architecture. The proposed design is evaluated through a dedicated mean-field synaptic drift model for dynamical analysis and further validated across SNN networks of different scales and datasets. It is further implemented on both ASIC and FPGA platforms and compared with state-of-the-art approaches, including the original STDP and more complex STDP variants. The results demonstrate superior energy efficiency, higher operating speed, and substantially lower hardware resource utilization, as the proposed design eliminates most of the computational overhead of STDP through both algorithmic and hardware-level optimizations. On the FPGA platform, the proposed design improves energy efficiency by 4.5$\times$ to 219.8$\times$ over the compared designs. On the ASIC platform, the proposed design achieves a 4.8$\times$ to 22.01$\times$ speedup while consuming only 1.2% to 3.3% of the area required by prior works.

</details>


### [109] [Modeling, Optimizing and Exploring Multi-Die FPGA Routing Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06421)
*Amirhossein Poolad,Soheil Gholami Shahrouz,Andrew Boutros,Vaughn Betz*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本研究构建了一个通用的 FPGA 加密工具框架（扩展 VTR），深入分析了 2.5D 和 3D FPGA 的片间布线架构空间，验证了多裸片堆叠技术在保障性能和可布线性的前提下显著提升 FPGA 容量的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多裸片（Multi-die）堆叠技术虽提升了 FPGA 容量，但现有的片间物理特性限制了互连密度与延迟，且目前缺乏能够准确评估和设计片间布线架构的工具，导致最优架构方案不明。

Method: 1. 扩展了开源 FPGA CAD 工具 VTR，支持多种片间布线架构的建模；2. 改进了 VPR 的布局和布线引擎；3. 基于 7nm 工艺节点和 45nm 硅中介层进行 HSPICE 电路建模；4. 进行了全面的设计空间探索（DSE），评估不同片间互连技术对性能的影响。

Result: 3D FPGA 相比 2D 设备线长缩短 14%，关键路径延迟（CPD）改善 6%；2.5D FPGA 在 32% 的片间连通性下，仅产生 2% 的线长开销和 4% 的 CPD 开销。所有工具扩展均已开源并合并至 VTR 主分支。

Conclusion: 通过针对性的片间布线架构，2.5D 和 3D FPGA 可在不显著增加布线难度或延迟的情况下提升容量。3D FPGA 在提升性能和缩短线长方面优于 2.5D，且即使在现有工艺限制下仍具备良好的可布线性。

Abstract: Die stacking has enabled 2.5D FPGAs by integrating multiple active dice on a passive silicon interposer for improved yield and capacity, and paved the way for 3D architectures that stack active dice directly atop one another. In these multi-die devices, the unique electrical and physical characteristics of the underlying die-stacking technology impose limitations on inter-die connection density and latency, necessitating a bespoke inter-die routing architecture. However, the absence of accurate and versatile modeling tools has left most questions about how to best design the inter-die routing architecture unanswered.
  To address this gap, we enhance the open-source FPGA CAD tool VTR to flexibly model a wide range of multi-die routing architectures, and augment VPR's placement and routing engines to improve optimization for both 2.5D and 3D FPGAs. We perform HSPICE-based circuit modeling of inter-die connections for active dice using a 7 nm process node and a 45 nm silicon interposer across several die-crossing technologies. Using this enhanced framework, we conduct a detailed design space exploration of inter-die routing architecture in 2.5D and 3D FPGAs, characterizing the impact of die-crossing technology, inter-die connection count, fan-in/fan-out, and interposer wire length on critical path delay (CPD), wirelength, area, and routability.
  Our results show that with suitable inter-die routing architectures, 2.5D and 3D FPGAs can increase capacity without significant routability or delay penalties. Specifically, 3D FPGAs achieve up to 14% wirelength reduction and 6% CPD improvement over 2D devices, and remain routable even with existing $10\,μ$m pitch technologies, while 2.5D FPGAs incur only a 2% wirelength and 4% CPD overhead at 32% inter-die connectivity. All extensions are open source and integrated with the VTR master branch.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [SET: Stream-Event-Triggered Scheduling for Efficient CUDA Graph Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05495)
*Zhengxiong Li,Tsung-Wei Huang,Umit Ogras*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型任务并行CUDA运行时框架，通过多流流水线、工作窃取及基于图的并发控制，显著降低了同步开销并有效提升了GPU吞吐量与资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CUDA kernel优化及批处理方法受限于宿主-设备同步延迟和内核调度开销，导致无法充分利用GPU的计算核心与拷贝引擎，限制了系统总吞吐量。

Method: 提出了一种用于任务并行流水线的CUDA运行时框架，包含两个核心创新：1. 基于多流任务并行流水线的编程模型（集成事件链与工作窃取机制，以最大限度利用硬件资源）；2. 基于图的执行流，配合每流缓冲区（per-stream buffers），确保并发任务间的内存安全。

Result: 在典型真实工作负载上的测试表明，相比先进的CUDA图基准，该方案实现了1.15-1.44倍的性能加速，并将调度开销降低了18%-54%。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的CUDA运行时框架通过改进任务调度与资源利用机制，有效解决了GPU计算中的同步延迟与调度开销问题，为提升高性能计算系统的吞吐量提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Achieving peak GPU performance remains a significant challenge as the system throughput is constrained by host-device synchronization delays and kernel scheduling overheads, even with aggressive kernel optimizations and batch processing. Furthermore, existing approaches often underutilize hardware resources such as compute cores and copy engines due to scheduling overheads. To address these problems, we propose a CUDA runtime framework for task-parallel pipelines to minimize the synchronization overheads and the gap between kernel executions. The proposed solution combines two innovations: (1) a multi-stream task-parallel pipeline programming model that leverages event-chaining and work-stealing mechanisms to fully utilize available hardware resources; (2) a graph-based execution flow with per-stream buffers to ensure memory safety for multiple in-flight jobs running concurrently. Extensive evaluations on representative real-world workloads show 1.15--1.44X speedup and reduce scheduling overheads by 18--54% compared to state-of-the-art CUDA graph baselines.

</details>


### [111] [Latent Reasoning Guidance for Parallel Code Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05518)
*Tomer Bitan,Erel Kaplan,Roee Bar-Yadin,Lian Ghrayeb,Le Chen,Samyak Jhaveri,Niranjan Hasabnis,Gal Oren*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在生成代码前通过潜空间奖励模型评估隐藏状态路径的技术，旨在通过早期干预提升代码编译与执行的成功率，无需重训大模型，并有效提升了多轮修复任务的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代码智能体多依赖于完整的代码生成后进行调试或修复，反馈滞后且计算成本高；因此提出利用潜空间推理作为早期干预点，以便在模型确定代码之前引导生成方向。

Method: 引入小型过程奖励模型（PRM）对连续的潜空间前缀序列进行评分，在最终解码前选择最优的隐藏状态轨迹进行分支预测，并与迭代修复循环相结合。

Result: 在ParaTrans基准测试上，验证成功率从32.89%提升至42.1%，在三轮修复循环下表现优于微调基线和普通基线。

Conclusion: 在代码生成任务中，通过测试时潜空间（latent space）的路径选择优于仅依赖代码层面的后处理优化，且该方法无需重新训练主生成模型即可有效提升可执行代码的成功率。

Abstract: Tackling complex coding tasks often requires autonomous agents and iterative repair pipelines. These increasingly rely on large amounts of test-time computation, often spending many decoding and repair steps before discovering whether a program compiles, runs, or validates. Executable parallel-code translation is an effective setting for earlier guidance because success is behavioral rather than textual. However, most guidance methods act only after complete programs or textual traces are decoded. This motivates the question: can latent reasoning provide an earlier intervention point, before the model commits to code?
  We study a test-time latent guidance method for this setting that trains a smaller Process Reward Model (PRM) over continuous latent prefixes and uses it to select among alternate hidden-state trajectories before final code decoding, separately from but compatible with post-decoding optimization. On a 76-task ParaTrans benchmark evaluation, latent PRM guidance improves mean validation rate from 32.89% with unguided latent reasoning to 42.1%, outperforming fine-tuned and vanilla baselines in the same setting. These gains persist under the same three-iteration repair loop. These results provide bounded evidence that useful alternative latent continuations exist and that PRM-scored latent branch selection can improve executable outcomes in this setting without retraining the main generative model.

</details>


### [112] [PoCQ: Proof of Contribution Quality as a Lightweight Blockchain Consensus for Secure Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05642)
*Sudad Abed,Nasser Sabar,Abdun Mahmood,Mohammad Jabed Morshed Chowdhury*
