Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于区块链的PoCQ框架，通过声誉机制和轻量级验证，有效提升了去中心化联邦学习的防御能力与运行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习虽摆脱了对中央服务器的依赖，但仍面临模型投毒攻击、验证不可靠及验证开销过高等安全与性能挑战。

Method: 提出了一种基于区块链的共识框架PoCQ，采用加密承诺与轻量级范数验证进行模型评估，并利用声誉机制动态调整参与者权重，仅在链上存储紧凑的审计元数据。

Result: 在中毒场景下测试表明，该方法在非独立同分布（Non-IID）医疗数据集上准确率提升了34.1%，全局平均准确率提升了11%，验证时间平均缩短了21.27%。

Conclusion: PoCQ通过声誉驱动与轻量级验证，为去中心化联邦学习提供了一种兼顾鲁棒性与高效性的安全解决方案。

Abstract: Decentralized Federated Learning (FL) removes reliance on centralized coordinators but remains vulnerable to model poisoning, unreliable validation, and high validation overhead. This paper introduces Proof of Contribution Quality (PoCQ), a blockchain-based consensus framework designed to secure decentralized FL through reputation-aware validation and aggregation. PoCQ evaluates client updates using cryptographic commitments and lightweight norm-based validation, enabling efficient detection of malicious contributions while limiting validation cost. A reputation-driven consensus mechanism dynamically adjusts the influence of participants based on their historical contribution quality, while the blockchain stores only compact audit metadata to preserve scalability. Extensive experiments under poisoning scenarios across three benchmark datasets demonstrate that PoCQ outperforms the strongest state-of-the-art methods, achieving accuracy gains of 34.1% on challenging medical datasets in highly non-iid settings and an 11% improvement in global average accuracy. In addition, PoCQ reduces validation time by 21.27% on average per round, highlighting its effectiveness in jointly enhancing robustness and efficiency for fully decentralized federated learning.

</details>


### [113] [IN2P3 Computing Center 2024 Workload Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05914)
*Guillaume Cochard,Bertrand Simon*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文发布并分析了IN2P3计算中心2024年全年的大规模实际作业数据集（4400万条），旨在为科研人员提供包含内存使用信息及时间周期性的高保真基准，以改进集群调度算法的设计与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数作业调度数据集往往存在规模小、时间跨度短、缺乏内存使用数据或仅为合成数据等局限性，难以模拟真实的集群生产环境及捕捉用户的周期性行为规律。

Method: 作者对法国国家科学研究中心（CNRS）下属的IN2P3计算中心2024年全年的生产作业进行了详细的日志采集、整理与分析，构建了一个包含4400万作业记录的大型真实工作负载数据集。

Result: 构建了一个涵盖1000名用户、4400万个作业、在包含312台机器（4.6万并发线程和105TB内存）的集群上运行的大型作业特征数据集。

Conclusion: 该数据集因其极大的规模、真实的时间跨度（覆盖2024全年）以及详尽的资源使用记录（含内存数据），成为研究和评估大规模集群调度算法的优质基准，并能有效揭示用户行为的周期性规律。

Abstract: This paper provides and analyzes a dataset detailing the characteristics and execution data of all jobs submitted to the IN2P3 Computing Center (Villeurbanne, France), a national research and support unit of the CNRS, in 2024. The main additional value of this contribution compared to previously available datasets consists in the combination of an extended time interval considered, the inclusion of memory usage data and its recency, on top on improving the diversity of datasets provenance. This allows researchers to simulate and evaluate scheduling algorithms on a real workload over a large time window. Thus, specificities due to seasonal, monthly, and weekly user behaviors can be taken into account, which is not possible with smaller or synthetic datasets. It is composed of 44M jobs submitted by 1k users running on a cluster of a maximum of 312 machines supporting 46k concurrent threads and providing 105To of RAM.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond Greedy Chunking: SLO-Aware Sliding-Window Scheduling for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05933)
*Yuansheng Chen,Yue Zhang,Xuan Mo,Weigang Wu,Jialun Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SlidingServe 是一个面向在线 LLM 推理的 SLO 感知调度系统，通过滑动分块、智能优先级调度和动态路径规划，在提升 30% 系统吞吐量的同时，有效解决了复杂负载下的服务质量（QoS）违约问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推理系统缺乏精细化的 QoS 区分能力，导致资源竞争处理不当、利用率低下，难以满足高吞吐与低延迟并存的在线服务需求。

Method: 提出了一套滑动窗口驱动的调度架构，包含：1. 轻量级批处理延迟预测器（Batch Latency Predictor）；2. 滑动分块技术（SlidingChunker）实现动态分块；3. 多级优先级排序器（Multi-Level Priority Sorter）平衡公平性与效率；4. 基于动态规划的 BatchConstructor，用于处理紧急请求以降低 SLO 违约风险。

Result: 相比现有先进调度系统，SlidingServe 将服务容量提升了高达 30%，并在重负载模式下将 SLO 违约率降低了 16%-53%。

Conclusion: SlidingServe 通过多维度的调度优化（分块、优先级排序及动态规划选择），在保障严格 SLO 的前提下，显著提升了 LLM 在线推理服务的吞吐量与服务质量。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of interactive applications in large language model (LLM) online services, maintaining high system throughput while ensuring user-perceived latency has become a key issue in inference scheduling. Existing LLM service systems rely on coarse-grained output constraints, making it difficult to effectively handle resource contention among multiple requests, resulting in low resource utilization efficiency and limited support for fine-grained quality of service (QoS) differentiation. We present SlidingServe, a sliding-window-driven SLO-Aware scheduling system for online LLM inference. SlidingServe designed a lightweight batch latency predictor to estimate the execution time of a batch. Based on this, SlidingServe uses SlidingChunker to combine information from the current iteration and the next iteration to achieve dynamic chunking and improve the overall system throughput while maintaining strict QoS guarantees. SlidingServe introduces Multi-Level Priority Sorter to sort candidate requests in order to balance fairness and efficiency. Additionally, when multiple requests within the same batch are at risk of SLO violating,SlidingServe introduces BatchConstructor, which uses dynamic programming to select the set of requests to execute in the current round, mitigating the SLO violation risk of critical requests.Our evaluation demonstrates that SlidingServe can improve service capacity by up to 30% compared to advanced scheduling systems under various load conditions, and further reduces the rate of SLO violation by 16%-53% under heavy-load inference mode.

</details>


### [115] [Demystifying NVSHMEM: A System-Level Analysis on Symmetric Memory and Device-Initiated Operations in GPU Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05951)
*Yijun Ma,Siyuan Shen,Tiancheng Chen,Akhil Langer,Jiri Kraus,Benjamin Glick,Craig Belusar,Jeff Hammond,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对 NVIDIA 的 NVSHMEM 通信库进行了系统性分析，探讨了其基于对称内存的编程模型、底层实现及性能优势，并展示了其在提升 GPU 通信效率方面的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 NVSHMEM 在 GPU 集群中应用日益广泛，但目前缺乏对其系统级设计、行为及性能特征的整合性研究，相关知识散落在文档和代码中。

Method: 通过对 NVSHMEM 的编程模型、内部实现（对称内存、单边操作、设备端集合通信）和性能特征进行系统性分析，并以 DeepEP 在稀疏深度学习任务中的应用作为案例研究。

Result: 明确了 NVSHMEM 在细粒度 GPU 驱动通信方面的核心贡献，揭示了其设计权衡（tradeoffs），并验证了其在高性能计算及深度学习工作负载中的能力。

Conclusion: NVSHMEM 是实现高性能 GPU 集群通信的关键系统构建模块，其对称内存编程模型对于突破硬件性能上限至关重要，同时也指出了未来 GPU 通信运行时优化的方向。

Abstract: NVSHMEM is NVIDIA's OpenSHMEM-based PGAS communication library for GPU clusters, enabling GPU-initiated, one-sided communication through symmetric memory. Despite its growing adoption, a system-level understanding of its design and behavior remains scattered across documentation, source code, and application experience. This paper presents a concise study of NVSHMEM's programming model, implementation, and performance characteristics, focusing on symmetric memory, one-sided operations, and device-side collectives. We also examine DeepEP as a case study of NVSHMEM in performance-critical sparse deep learning workloads. Our analysis shows that NVSHMEM pioneered a device-side symmetric-memory programming model that enables fine-grained GPU-driven communication and is important for approaching the hardware performance limit. Overall, this work defines NVSHMEM's role as a systems building block, highlights its design tradeoffs, and identifies opportunities for improving GPU communication runtimes.

</details>


### [116] [LLM-Based Porting of Optimized C++ to CUDA Through Deoptimization and Reoptimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06063)
*Daichi Mukunoki,Ryo Mikasa,Shunichiro Hayashi,Tetsuya Hoshino,Takahiro Katagiri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究提出“去优化-重优化”（Deopt-Reopt）工作流以改善 LLM 对 HPC 代码的 GPU 移植效果。结果显示，虽然该方法在特定算子和模型组合下具有明显优势，但并非普适方案，其性能增益受多种变量制约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPU 导向的经典优化手段在移植高性能计算（HPC）代码到 GPU 时，往往会干扰甚至阻碍基于 LLM 的 CUDA 翻译过程。

Method: 设计了“去优化-重优化”（Deopt-Reopt）工作流：首先简化 CPU 导向的 C++ 代码，随后由 LLM 重新翻译并优化为 CUDA。将其与直接翻译（Direct）进行对比，并通过单次（Single-shot）和迭代（Iterative）两种设置在 12 个 HPC 算子上进行评估。

Result: 在单次设置中，Deopt-Reopt 在部分算子（如 conv2d）上表现更好，但并非总是优于直接翻译；在迭代设置中，Deopt-Reopt 在处理复杂算子时展现了明显的性能优势。该方法对代码编译可行性的影响呈现混合结果。

Conclusion: Deopt-Reopt 是一种有效但非通用的 LLM 辅助 GPU 代码移植技术。其性能提升高度依赖于算子特性、使用的 LLM、迭代预算以及代码的成功编译率，并非总是能带来端到端的性能提升。

Abstract: When porting high-performance computing (HPC) code from CPU to GPU, CPU-oriented optimizations may obstruct LLM-based CUDA translation. We design and evaluate a Deopt-Reopt workflow that first simplifies the input C++ code and then retranslates and reoptimizes it for CUDA, comparing it against direct translation (Direct) on twelve HPC kernels with two LLMs (gpt-oss-120b (O120) and qwen-3-235b-a22b-instruct-2507 (Q235)) in Single-shot (one pass) and Iterative (repeated refinement) settings. In Single-shot, among 18 testable cases Deopt-Reopt was significantly faster among successful trials (after BH-FDR correction) in five - most clearly for conv2d, where CPU- and GPU-oriented designs diverge - but Direct was faster in three, so removing CPU-specific optimizations is not universally beneficial. An exploratory Direct-3 control that equalizes the LLM-call count left Deopt-Reopt ahead in only four of nineteen testable cases, with Direct-3 ahead in four others. In Iterative, repeated generation and repair narrow the mode gap - markedly so for O120 - while Q235 retains large Deopt-Reopt advantages on conv2d, ddgemm, and bgemm. Deopt-Reopt's effect on feasibility is also mixed - sharply higher for some kernels Direct rarely compiles, lower for others. Because performance is conditioned on successful trials, the benefit is conditional rather than a guaranteed end-to-end gain. Overall, Deopt-Reopt is an effective but non-universal technique for LLM-based GPU porting, with gains that depend on the kernel, the model, the search budget, and the success rate.

</details>


### [117] [Discrete Incremental Voting: New Bounds for General Graphs and Expanders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06381)
*Petra Berenbrink,Colin Cooper,Thorsten Götte,Lukas Hintze,Tomasz Radzik*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文分析了分布式离散增量投票过程（DIV）在一般图和正则图上的收敛性能，给出了期望收敛时间的优化上界，并确定了其在正则图上收敛至初始意见平均值的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了量化并优化离散增量投票过程（DIV）在图结构上的收敛速度和收敛结果，以理解其作为一种分布式意见共识机制的性能边界。

Method: 通过分析图的电导（conductance Φ(G)）、度数比（γ(G)）以及初始意见最大值（K）间的关系，推导了DIV过程的期望收敛时间上界；在正则图场景下，进一步结合谱分析方法（第二大特征值）探究其收敛特性。

Result: 得出了DIV期望收敛时间上界为 O(n(Klog(Kn) + γ(G)n) / Φ(G)^2)；证明了正则图中，当第二大特征值满足 o(1/log^2 n) 且 K 满足 o(n/log^2 n) 时，DIV 以高概率收敛至初始意见的加权平均值。

Conclusion: DIV方案的收敛时间界在有界展开图（bounded expansion）类中基本是最优的。对于正则图，在特定谱间隙和初始意见差异的条件下，DIV算法能以高概率（w.h.p.）收敛到初始意见的加权平均值（向上或向下取整）。

Abstract: We analyze the discrete incremental voting process (DIV) introduced by Cooper, Radzik, and Shiraga [OPODIS '23]. In this process, we consider a set $V$ of $n$ nodes connected in an undirected graph $G = (V, E)$ where each node has an integer opinion. In one step a randomly selected node interacts with its randomly selected neighbor and changes its opinion by $1$ in the direction of the neighbour's opinion. The process converges to a unique opinion that, in expectation, is the degree-weighted average of the initial opinions.
  We show that if the graph has conductance $Φ(G)$, the ratio of the average to smallest degree is $γ(G)$, and the maximal difference between initial opinions is $K$, then the expected convergence time is ${O}\left({n\left(K\log (Kn)+γ(G) n \right)}/{Φ(G)^2}\right)$. This bound is essentially optimal for a large class of graphs of bounded expansion. We also show that for regular graphs, if the second largest eigenvalue is $o(1/\log^2 n)$ and $K$ is $o\left({n}/{\log^2 n}\right)$, then w.h.p.\ DIV converges to the initial average opinion (rounded up or down).

</details>


### [118] [On GPU Implementation for Multi-Precision Integer Division](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06386)
*Martin B. Marchioro,Aske N. Raahauge,Marc I. Løvenskjold,Cosmin E. Oancea,Stephen M. Watt*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对GPU环境提出了一种基于牛顿迭代的整数除法算法，有效解决了$2^{15}$至$2^{18}$位超大整数的计算难题，实现了接近理论性能最优的高效执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用GPU在处理超大规模（$2^{15}$至$2^{18}$位）整数除法时面临执行效率与实现的挑战，而现有的CGBN等库不支持此类大精度需求。

Method: 基于牛顿迭代法（Newton iteration）及其移位逆运算，将所有算术保持在整数域，利用数据并行算子；结合前缀和技术与多精度乘法优化，并提出了基于乘法次数的计算代价模型。

Result: 成功实现了适用于特定大精度范围的整数除法算法，并在GPU/CUDA环境下表现出接近理论预测的近最优性能。

Conclusion: 通过算法优化和代价模型分析，实现了在GPU上进行大规模（$2^{15}$到$2^{18}$位）整数除法的高效实现，接近理论性能最优解，填补了现有库（如CGBN）在超大整数除法支持上的空白。

Abstract: This paper presents the issues arising in implementing a fast integer division algorithm on general purpose GPUs. The algorithm uses a Newton iteration based on the shifted inverse operation, keeping all arithmetic in the integer domain and relying on data-parallel operators. The principal contribution is an efficient GPU/CUDA implementation for integer precisions from $2^{15}$ to $2^{18}$ -- sizes not supported by \cgbn{} division. We propose algorithmic refinements, define a cost model in terms of multiplications, build on prefix sums and previous work on multi-precision multiplication, and present an evaluation showing near-optimal performance relative to the model for the target precision.

</details>


### [119] [CarbonSim: A Lifecycle-Aware Framework for Evaluating Carbon Tradeoffs in Hardware Upgrade Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06438)
*Kartik Hans,Kaiwen Zhao,Stephen Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CarbonSim 是一个生命周期感知模拟框架，旨在评估硬件升级对总碳排放的影响，研究发现盲目更新硬件并非总能减少碳足迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ICT需求增长，计算系统的环境影响日益凸显。单纯追求新型硬件的运行能效往往无法抵消其生产制造过程中的高额碳排放，特别是在低负载或清洁电网环境下。

Method: 提出 CarbonSim 框架，整合工作负载画像、机器功率特性、隐含碳库存、调度策略及随时间变化的电网碳强度，支持多种碳核算策略（如均匀摊销与提前归因）。

Result: 通过异构CPU平台的实验验证，证明在轻负载或清洁能源网格下，通过延长旧机寿命减少隐含碳排放的效果，往往优于更换新型高效能硬件。

Conclusion: 硬件更新策略不应仅关注运行能效，而应采取工作负载感知、位置感知和生命周期感知的决策方式。在某些场景下，延长旧硬件使用寿命比更新更节能减排。

Abstract: As the demand for information and communication technologies (ICT) continues to rise, the environmental impact of computing systems is becoming an increasingly critical concern. Although newer hardware often improves performance and energy efficiency, these gains do not always offset the carbon cost of premature replacement, particularly under low-utilization workloads or low-carbon electricity grids. We present CarbonSim, a lifecycle-aware simulation framework for evaluating carbon tradeoffs in hardware upgrade decisions. CarbonSim combines workload execution profiles, machine-level power characteristics, embodied carbon inventories, scheduling policies, and time-varying grid carbon intensity to estimate total emissions under alternative deployment scenarios. The framework supports multiple embodied-carbon accounting strategies, including uniform amortization and front-loaded lifecycle attribution, enabling analysis under different hardware lifespan assumptions. Using heterogeneous CPU generations as calibration platforms, we demonstrate that newer machines do not always minimize total emissions: under lightly loaded workloads or cleaner electricity mixes, extending the useful life of existing hardware can reduce lifecycle carbon despite lower operational efficiency. These results highlight that hardware refresh decisions should be workload-aware, location-aware, and lifecycle-aware.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [PHKT:Personalized Dynamic Hypergraph-enhanced KAN-Transformer for Multi-behavior Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05537)
*Ruijie Du,Hao Chen,Xin Zhang,Dongjing Wang,Ze Zhang,Xudong Shen,Runze Wu,Dongjin Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对多行为推荐问题，本文提出了PHKT架构，通过整合“个性化动态超图”与“KAN增强的Transformer”，实现了对用户异构高阶关系和复杂非线性偏好的精准建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图/超图方法难以处理多行为中的异构语义、用户特异性权重及序列依赖，而标准Transformer的共享前馈映射结构难以适应复杂多行为场景下的差异化 latent patterns。

Method: 提出了PHKT（个性化超图增强KAN Transformer）模型：1. 个性化动态超图模块：根据用户历史行为实现物品相似度的行为感知加权；2. Transformer骨干：用于建模短期与长期动态偏好；3. KAN（Kolmogorov-Arnold Network）：替代传统MLP作为前馈网络，实现更精细的非线性模式建模。

Result: 在Tmall、RetailRocket和IJCAI三个数据集上的实验结果显示，PHKT在多项指标上均优于9个主流基准模型。

Conclusion: PHKT通过引入个性化动态超图和KAN，有效解决了多行为推荐中异构语义建模不足、用户个性化权重缺失及MLP建模长尾模式能力弱的问题，在多项实验中表现卓越。

Abstract: In multi-behavior recommendation, auxiliary behaviors such as clicks, add-to-cart, and purchases can provide richer supervisory information for predicting target behaviors. Although existing graph and hypergraph methods are capable of modeling high-order relationships among users, items, and behaviors, they still have limitations in heterogeneous semantics, user-specific weighting, and sequence dependency modeling. While standard Transformers excel at sequence modeling, their shared feedforward mapping struggles to accommodate the differentiated requirements of heterogeneous latent patterns in multi-behavior scenarios. To address this, this paper proposes the Personalized Hypergraph-enhanced Kolmogorov-Arnold Network Transformer (PHKT). Specifically, we design a personalized dynamic hypergraph module that performs behavior-aware weighting of item similarities based on users' historical behavior sequences to capture user-specific heterogeneous high-order relationships. Meanwhile, a Transformer is used as the temporal backbone to model the evolution of short- and long-term preferences, and KAN is introduced to replace the traditional MLP in the feedforward network to enhance fine-grained modeling capability for nonlinear responses to different latent patterns. Experiments on three real datasets, Tmall, RetailRocket, and IJCAI, show that PHKT consistently outperforms nine strong baseline models across multiple evaluation metrics, demonstrating its effectiveness in multi-behavior preference modeling and target behavior prediction.

</details>


### [121] [ColBERTSaR: Sparsified ColBERT Index via Product Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05568)
*Eugene Yang,Andrew Yates,Dawn Lawrie,James Mayfield,Saron Samuel,Rohan Jha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对ColBERT索引存储开销大和检索效率低的问题，本文提出一种嵌入量化方案构建真正的倒置索引，在大幅压缩索引体积（50-70%）的同时，实现了与现有方法相当的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ColBERT索引（如PLAID）存储开销过大（约为原始文本的5-10倍），且在查询时处理文档token嵌入的收集（gathering）和解压缩环节存在显著的计算效率瓶颈，限制了其在大规模场景下的扩展性。

Method: 引入嵌入量化（embedding quantization）技术，将ColBERT索引转化为倒置索引结构，从理论上证明了其与学习型稀疏检索（learned-sparse retrieval）的等效性。

Result: 该方法在保持模型检索准确性的前提下，将索引大小较一比特PLAID索引进一步缩小了50%至70%。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的嵌入量化方法成功将ColBERT索引转化为真正的倒置索引，在显著降低存储成本（相比PLAID减少50-70%）的同时，保持了原有的检索效果，实现了存储效率与性能的平衡。

Abstract: While ColBERT is an effective neural retrieval architecture, it requires a heavy index structure to support candidate set retrieval based on approximated token embeddings, gathering and decompressing document token embeddings, and applying the MaxSim operation. Indexes in PLAID and similar ColBERT implementations require five to ten times the disk storage of the original raw text, which limits their scalability. Furthermore, prior work has identified that the gathering and decompression stages are the primary inefficiencies at query time. Limiting the number of document tokens that must be gathered by thresholding and score approximation does not eliminate the need for the entire index to support ad hoc queries. In this work, we propose an embedding quantization approach that turns a ColBERT index into a true inverted index. We show that, theoretically, ColBERT with embedding quantization is equivalent to learned-sparse retrieval except for the scoring mechanism. Empirically, we demonstrate that our index is 50-70% smaller than a one-bit PLAID index while retaining retrieval effectiveness.

</details>


### [122] [ANCHOR: Agentic Noise Creation Framework for Human Simulation and Denoising Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05621)
*Xiangming Li,Hua Chu,Chengyu Feng,Jianan Li,Yangtao Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 为了解决推荐系统中隐式反馈噪声的干扰，本文提出了 ANCHOR 框架，通过模拟智能体主动生成带标签的噪声数据，将原本依赖启发式规则的去噪过程转化为监督学习，从而更精准地识别并剔除噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统去噪方法依赖于无监督的边信息或手动设计的启发式规则，存在成本高、泛化能力差、噪声识别不准确以及破坏真实用户偏好表示等局限。

Method: 提出 Creation-Recognition（创造-识别）范式：利用基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体架构（ANCHOR）主动生成带标注的噪声数据，包括模拟真实噪声来源的“偏好外噪声”和通过对抗性优化生成的“边界邻近噪声”，随后训练一个参数化识别器对真实数据进行监督去噪。

Result: 该方法通过主动创建高质量的噪声标签实现了监督学习，成功缓解了传统去噪方法中对噪声误判和真实信息丢失的问题，在处理隐式反馈噪声方面表现出优越性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种名为 ANCHOR 的代理驱动框架，通过将去噪问题从启发式过滤重构为监督学习任务，有效地解决了隐式反馈中的噪声问题，显著提升了推荐系统的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Distilling accurate user preferences from noisy implicit feedback remains a fundamental bottleneck in recommendation systems, highlighting the need for recommendation denoising. However, real-world data lack explicit noise annotations, forcing existing methods to rely on unsupervised side information or handcrafted heuristics. These approaches often incur high external costs, generalize poorly, or depend on unreliable priors, causing noise misidentification and corrupting true user preference representations. To address these limitations, we propose a paradigm-level reformulation of recommendation denoising. Instead of indirectly inferring noisy interactions through heuristics, our Creation-Recognition paradigm proactively creates labeled noisy interactions and trains a dedicated recognizer to identify them, transforming denoising from heuristic filtering into supervised learning. Based on this paradigm, we present ANCHOR, an agent-based framework inspired by recent LLM-as-User research. ANCHOR simulates user behaviors to generate realistic noise labels and enables supervised denoising through two stages: noise creation and noise recognition. In the noise creation stage, ANCHOR adopts a recommender-in-the-loop agentic architecture to synthesize both diverse out-of-preference noise and informative boundary-adjacent noise. For out-of-preference noise, it implements five extensible simulation mechanisms to approximate major sources of noisy implicit feedback. For boundary-adjacent noise, an adversarial boundary refinement mechanism generates ambiguous interactions that challenge the recognizer and target the decision boundary. In the noise recognition stage, ANCHOR leverages the generated labels to train a reusable parametric recognizer that integrates collaborative signals and semantic representations to detect noise patterns in real interaction data.

</details>


### [123] [Agent-Orchestrated Adaptive RAG: A Comparative Study on Structured and Multi-Hop Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05658)
*Anuj Maharjan,Devinder Kaur,Richard Molyet*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应RAG框架，通过引入动态分解与自反射机制优化检索。研究发现，代理增强的应用效果高度依赖于场景，应优先考虑成本与收益的平衡，而非盲目堆叠复杂链式推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG流水线依赖静态、单步检索，难以有效应对复杂查询，限制了大型语言模型（LLM）的性能。

Method: 提出了一种“代理编排自适应RAG框架”，核心包含：1. 动态查询分解；2. 迭代检索机制；3. 有界自反射评估循环。并在DevOps领域知识库和MuSiQue多跳推理基准上进行了评估。

Result: 查询分解在结构化领域（DevOps）显著提升性能（总体得分+0.04，MRR+0.17），但在多跳基准上降低了排序精度；自反射机制有效提高了引文准确性，但付出了显著的延迟代价。

Conclusion: 代理增强型RAG并非在所有场景下皆优。研究表明，必须根据具体的查询类型和领域特征，进行选择性、自适应且考虑成本的编排，而非盲目采用复杂的推理流水线。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances Large Language Models (LLMs) by grounding their responses in external knowledge, but conventional pipelines rely on static, single-step retrieval that limits performance on complex queries. This paper presents an Agent-Orchestrated Adaptive RAG framework that introduces dynamic query decomposition, iterative retrieval, and a bounded self-reflective evaluation loop. We evaluate the system across two complementary datasets: a domain-specific DevOps knowledge base and the multi-hop reasoning benchmark MuSiQue. Using metrics that include overall score, citation accuracy, mean reciprocal rank, and topic coverage, we find that query decomposition yields consistent gains in the structured domain (overall score $+0.04$, MRR $+0.17$ on DevOps) but degrades ranking precision on the multi-hop benchmark, while the reflection mechanism improves citation accuracy at a substantial latency cost. These contrasting results show that agentic enhancements are not universally beneficial and must be applied selectively according to query and domain characteristics. Our findings argue for adaptive, cost-aware orchestration rather than uniformly aggressive reasoning pipelines.

</details>


### [124] [Knowledge Manifold: A Riemannian Geometric Framework for Semantic Mapping and Geodesic Analysis of Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05907)
*Tomonaga Okabe,Kazuhiko Komatsu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于黎曼几何的“知识流形”框架，利用字符n-gram TF-IDF、SPH插值和高斯过程回归，将文档映射至几何空间，从而实现语义可视化、概念路径探索及预测性知识（虚拟文档）的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决传统文档空间映射在处理语义关联和探索潜在研究领域时的局限性，通过构建黎曼几何空间来建模文档间的语义位置关系，并实现对未知研究领域的预测。

Method: 该方法包含五个核心步骤：1. 使用字符n-gram TF-IDF特征（L2归一化）并通过受限应力最小化嵌入二维空间；2. 利用光滑粒子流体动力学（SPH）通过三次样条核进行空间插值；3. 计算方向性知识梯度及相似度；4. 使用高斯过程回归（GPR）进行贝叶斯推断，提供均值、不确定性和贡献度；5. 通过最小化离散黎曼路径能量计算语义空间中的测地线。

Result: 在复合材料与航空航天结构领域的20篇论文语料库上进行了验证，结果显示该方法不仅能可视化已有研究集群，还能通过测地线展示不同主题间的内在联系，并成功生成了前瞻性的虚拟研究方向摘要。

Conclusion: 该框架成功构建了语义流形，能够有效恢复研究聚类，并通过测地线揭示概念联系。此外，SPH与GPR的结合支持“虚拟知识”生成，即预测并生成未被研究但具有潜力的研究方向摘要。

Abstract: We present the knowledge manifold: a Riemannian geometric space in which a corpus of documents is arranged according to semantic positional relationships derived from character n-gram TF-IDF representations. The framework proceeds in five tightly coupled stages. First, each document is converted to a character-level n-gram TF-IDF vector (4-7 grams, up to 250,000 features, L2-normalized) and embedded in a two-dimensional knowledge map via constrained stress minimization with repulsion, variance, and centering regularizers. Second, knowledge at an arbitrary query point is estimated through Smoothed Particle Hydrodynamics (SPH) interpolation using a cubic-spline kernel, yielding an interpolated TF-IDF feature vector that can be linguistically characterized. Third, directional knowledge gradients at 0, 45, and 90 degrees are computed from the SPH interpolation map, and pairwise directional similarity is quantified via inner product and cosine similarity. Fourth, a Gaussian Process Regression (GPR) model, with a Constant x RBF + White kernel fitted on a 10-dimensional SVD projection, provides a Bayesian posterior mean, uncertainty estimate, and per-document contribution rate at the query point. Fifth, geodesics in the knowledge space are obtained by minimizing a discrete Riemannian path energy derived from the SPH-induced metric tensor, using L-BFGS-B with seven deterministic initial-path candidates. We apply the formulation to a corpus of 20 papers in fiber-reinforced composite materials and aerospace structural mechanics, showing that the semantic map recovers meaningful research clusters, geodesic paths reveal natural conceptual bridges between distant topics, and SPH/GPR interpolation enables the generation of virtual knowledge: hypothetical paper abstracts describing unstudied but geometrically predicted research directions.

</details>


### [125] [Edge-Aware Curvature Modeling for Graph Understanding in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06073)
*Zhenghong Lin,Zhibin Shi,Hongyang Dong,Xinjie Ye,Yuhong Chen,Shiping Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CureLLM 框架，通过引入边缘信息增强和曲率感知消息传递机制，解决了图与文本对齐中因忽略边结构导致的“过压缩”问题，显著提升了图感知的 LLM 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图感知的 LLM 多采用节点级对齐，忽略了边级结构，这会导致信息传播次优，并引发类似于“过压缩（over-squashing）”的信息流瓶颈现象。

Method: 提出了 CureLLM 框架：1. 使用无需训练的文本提示（prompt）机制直接注入边缘信息；2. 设计曲率感知图表示学习，仅在正曲率边上进行模态信息传递，以缓解过压缩问题。

Result: 在 11 个真实世界数据集上，与 20 种现有方法对比，CureLLM 在性能上表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: CureLLM 通过整合边缘信息和曲率感知机制，解决了图与文本模态对齐中的“过压缩”问题，在 11 个数据集上实验证明优于现有的 20 种对比方法。

Abstract: Recently, graph-aware Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promising capabilities in jointly modeling graph-structured data and textual information. Existing approaches typically employ a graph encoder and a frozen LLM to obtain node representations from graph and textual views, followed by node-level alignment to bridge the two modalities. However, such alignment mechanisms primarily focus on node information while overlooking edge-level structures, leading to suboptimal information propagation across views. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive theoretical analysis to uncover why node-level alignment is insufficient for aligning textual and graph representations. Specifically, we prove theoretically for the first time that neglecting edge information leads to suboptimal solutions and negatively curved edges induce bottlenecked information flow, giving rise to the over-squashing phenomenon between graph and textual views. To address the two challenges, we innovatively proposed a CureLLM framework of Curvature-enhanced Graph Representations for Large Language Model whose goal is to inject the signals of edge information into the existing LLMs. Specifically, CureLLM first introduces the training-free textual prompt mechanism to make the LLM model generate the output directly based on the edge-aware prompt without learnable parameter costs. Furthermore, a novel curvature-aware graph representation learning is designed to capture the edge structure information to enhance the downstream tasks, where the message passing between text and graph representations only depends on edges with positive curvature. Finally, we conduct evaluations with 20 different compared methods on 11 real world datasets from various domains and the experiment results demonstrate the superiority of our proposed CureLLM framework.

</details>


### [126] [WebKnoGraph: GNN-Powered Internal Linking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06106)
*Emilija Gjorgjevska,Georgina Mirceva,Miroslav Mirchev*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文发布了WebKnoGraph框架，通过图神经网络和嵌入技术量化评估内部链接策略，指出完全自动化虽能显著提升权重，但人工审核对维护语义连贯性至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的内部链接优化高度依赖人工判断或固定模板，缺乏针对性的量化评估工具，容易导致网站权重分配不当或语义连贯性受损。

Method: 1. 使用GraphSAGE模型对网站进行图建模及链接评分；2. 使用嵌入技术（Embeddings）表达页面特征；3. 通过将网站嵌入更大规模的宿主图（Host Graphs，包括FineWeb真实爬取图及Barabási-Albert合成图）进行干预实验评估；4. 综合指标包括PageRank权威度、语义一致性及权威波动性。

Result: 1. 自动化选择在权威度提升（Authority Yield）方面表现更强，但牺牲了更多语义一致性；2. 专家介入模式更好地平衡了语义连贯性，在针对低PageRank页面时可获得最高权威收益，但在投入产出比上相对较低。

Conclusion: 提出了一种“自动化推荐生成+专家审核”的混合工作流。该研究强调了评估内部链接策略时，不能仅关注权重提升（Authority Yield），还必须平衡语义一致性（Semantic Coherence）和权重波动（Authority Volatility）。

Abstract: Internal link optimization is a recurring task in search engine optimization, yet many production workflows rely on manual judgment, fixed page templates, or generic tool recommendations. Practitioners need ways to evaluate candidate links before deployment because link changes can redistribute authority and affect semantic coherence in ways that are difficult to isolate after release. We present WebKnoGraph, an open-source framework for evaluating internal linking strategies on website crawls. The framework models a website as a directed graph, represents pages by embeddings, scores candidate links with GraphSAGE, and evaluates interventions by embedding the site into larger host environments. We instantiate WebKnoGraph on a production crawl of Kalicube.com and compare automatic with expert-assisted link selection in an empirical FineWeb-based host graph and a synthetic Barabási-Albert host graph, using PageRank-based authority metrics and semantic coherence. The results show that automatic selection generally produces stronger authority redistribution, with higher Authority Yield, but also larger semantic coherence costs. Expert-assisted selection better preserves semantic coherence and, when targeting low-PageRank pages, achieves the highest Authority Yield, although with the least favorable loss-gain balance. Authority Volatility provides an additional stability perspective, but is interpreted cautiously because the two regimes use different numbers of intervention sets. These findings support a practical workflow in which candidate intervention sets are generated at scale, evaluated jointly across authority gain, volatility, loss-gain balance, and semantic coherence, and then reviewed for editorial deployability before implementation.

</details>


### [127] [Bridging the Semantic-Collaborative Gap: An Asymmetric Graph Architecture for Cold-Start Item Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06225)
*Anh Truong,John Trenkle,Yuanbo Chen,Honghong Zhao,Abdullah Alchihabi,Effy Fang,Michael Tamir*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Tubi提出了一种名为Shallow-RHS的非对称链路预测架构，通过将内容冷启动转化为归纳图补全问题，仅使用固有特征为新内容生成协同感知嵌入，成功解决了冷启动推荐难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同过滤和基于图的推荐模型依赖历史交互数据，无法处理新内容（内容冷启动）及冷启动设备，且Tubi的生产环境要求新内容必须即时获得嵌入并支持近似最近邻检索。

Method: 提出了一种非对称链路预测架构 Shallow-RHS：(1) LHS（设备侧）使用基于时序观看历史的消息传递来捕捉协同信号；(2) RHS（内容侧）塔结构极为精简，仅利用固有特征，强制进行从固有特征空间到协同过滤空间的外推学习；(3) 利用该模型实现冷启动内容的即时嵌入生成，并通过检索相似“替身”邻居实现隐含的图补全。

Result: 在大规模线上实验中，Shallow-RHS在冷启动内容的参与度、推广速度、曝光获取以及冷启动设备的参与度方面均取得了显著的相对提升。

Conclusion: Shallow-RHS架构通过强制内容编码器仅使用固有特征来映射到协同过滤感知的嵌入空间，有效解决了内容和设备的冷启动问题，并在大规模线上实验中证实了其在提升冷启动内容参与度和曝光效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: Collaborative filtering and graph-based recommendation models are highly effective because they leverage observed user interactions, but this dependence creates a fundamental cold-start challenge when newly added content has no interaction history. In Tubi's production retrieval system, this challenge is further constrained by the serving interface: new content must be assigned a standalone embedding immediately, and the model must also produce device embeddings suitable for approximate nearest-neighbor retrieval. We address this setting by formulating cold-start recommendation as an inductive graph-completion problem on a temporal bipartite device-content graph. We propose Shallow-RHS, an asymmetric link-prediction architecture in which the left-hand side (LHS) device tower leverages temporally valid watch-history message passing to capture collaborative signals, while the right-hand side (RHS) content tower is intentionally shallow with respect to the graph and encodes content solely from intrinsic features. The RHS tower does not use ID-based embeddings, content-side subgraphs, neighbor aggregation, or interaction-derived representations, forcing the content encoder to map intrinsic features into a collaborative-filtering-aware embedding space. After training, the learned content encoder generates embeddings for both warm and newly ingested content, enabling implicit graph completion through retrieval of warm surrogate neighbors. We further extend the same representation-completion principle to device cold-start by constructing cohort-based embeddings from demographic features. Large-scale online experiments demonstrate consistent relative improvements in content cold-start engagement, promotion speed, impression acquisition, and device cold-start engagement.

</details>


### [128] [OneReason Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06260)
*OneRec Team,Biao Yang,Boyang Ding,Chenglong Chu,Dunju Zang,Fei Pan,Han Li,Hao Jiang,Honghui Bao,Huanjie Wang,Jian Liang,Jiangxia Cao,Jiao Ou,Jiaxin Deng,Jinghao Zhang,Kun Gai,Lu Ren,Peiru Du,Pengfei Zheng,Rongzhou Zhang,Ruiming Tang,Shiyao Wang,Siyang Mao,Siyuan Lou,Teng Shi,Wei Yuan,Wenlong Xu,Xingchen Liu,Xingmei Wang,Xinqi Jin,Yan Sun,Yan Wang,Yifei Hu,Yingzhi He,Yufei Ye,Yuhao Wang,Yunhao Zhou,Yuqin Dai,Zhao Liu,Zhipeng Wei,Zhixin Ling,Ziming Li,Zixing Zhang,Ziyuan Liu,An Zhang,Changxin Lao,Chaoyi Ma,Chengru Song,Defu Lian,Fan Yang,Guowang Zhang,Hao Peng,Jiayao Shen,Jie Chen,Jun Xu,Junmin Chen,Kun Zhang,Kuo Cai,Mingxing Wen,Minmao Wang,Minxuan Lv,Qi Zhang,Qiang Luo,Sheng Yu,Shijie Li,Shijie Yi,Shuang Yang,Shugui Liu,Shuni Chen,Tinghai Zhang,Tingting Gao,Xiang Wang,Xiangyu Wu,Xiangyu Zhao,Xiao Lv,Xiaoyou Zhou,Xuming Wang,Yong Du,Zejian Zhang,Zhaojie Liu,Zhiyang Zhang,Zhuang Zhuang,Ziqi Wang,Ziyi Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对生成式推荐模型无法有效应用思维链（CoT）的问题，本文提出 OneReason 框架，通过增强物品语义感知和三层认知驱动的 CoT 训练，成功激活了推荐模型的推理推理潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐模型（OneRec 系列）虽具备扩展性，但受限于物品序列难以构建有效的思维链（CoT），导致其推理能力无法像 LLM 那样被有效激活；此外，直接迁移 LLM 的 CoT 模式在推荐任务中效果不佳。

Method: 1. 预训练阶段：强化物品 token 的感知能力；2. 指导微调（SFT）阶段：引入三层认知增强的思维链（CoT）格式；3. 强化学习（RL）阶段：采用“先专用后统一”的训练配方以提升思维能力。

Result: 验证了在推荐任务中，有效的推理能力取决于“对物品语义的感知”以及“对用户行为序列的认知重构”，提出的 OneReason 框架有效提升了模型的推理与推荐性能。

Conclusion: 通过增强物品语义感知（Perception）与基于行为序列的认知推理（Cognition），OneReason 成功激发了生成式推荐模型的推理能力，验证了“思考模式”在推荐系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation models in the OneRec family have been widely deployed in many real-world services, such as short-video, live-streaming, advertising, and e-commerce. However, these generative models can only benefit from the scaling advantage, while their reasoning ability is hard to activate, since we cannot construct meaningful Chain-of-Thought (CoT) sequences consisting of itemic tokens only. Inspired by the success of the reasoning-style ``think before answer'' paradigm in the LLM field, we conduct preliminary studies (i.e., OneRec-Think, OpenOneRec) to explore reasoning capability in generative recommendation. Nevertheless, we notice an unexpected phenomenon: the thinking mode does not show advantages over the non-thinking mode. Drawing insights from recent findings on CoT robustness in multi-modal language models, we argue that effective reasoning in recommendation rests on two factors: perception, the ability to ground itemic tokens in their underlying language semantics, and cognition, the ability to reorganize a user's behavior sequence into coherent latent interest points. We therefore propose OneReason, which includes: (1) strong itemic token perception in pre-training, (2) a three-level cognition-enhanced CoT format for recommendation tasks in SFT, and (3) a specialize-then-unify training recipe in RL to enhance the thinking ability.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [PivCo-Huffman](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05765)
*Marcin Zukowski*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: PivCo-Huffman 提出了一种基于小波树的改进型 Huffman 编码，利用 SIMD 技术极大提升了处理速度，并可通过引入 ANS 技术在压缩效率与解压速度之间取得优秀平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 Huffman 编码在现代高性能计算环境下存在局限性，作者旨在通过新的数据结构设计，利用 SIMD 指令集提升 Huffman 编码与解码的吞吐量，并缩小与 ANS（非对称数字系统）编码在压缩率上的差距。

Method: 基于小波树（wavelet trees）数据结构构建 PivCo-Huffman，支持 SIMD 友好型编码与解码操作；并针对倾斜（skewed）节点引入了 ANS 编码以优化压缩率。

Result: 实验表明，PivCo-Huffman 在解码吞吐量方面优于现有的先进 Huffman 编解码器；通过结合 ANS 编码，能够在维持高速解压的同时，使压缩比逼近专用的 ANS 编码器。

Conclusion: PivCo-Huffman 是一种高效的编码方案，通过结合小波树结构与（可选的）ANS 编码，成功在保持高解压速度的同时实现了压缩比的优化，是 Huffman 编码在现代 SIMD 硬件架构下的有效改进。

Abstract: Huffman encoding has been an enduring technique for 70+ years, ubiquitous in compression algorithms since its invention. In this paper we propose a new approach to Huffman coding, based on a data structure from wavelet trees. The resulting pivot-coded Huffman (PivCo-Huffman) enables high-performance SIMD-friendly encoding and decoding operations. In our tests PivCo-Huffman consistently outperforms state-of-the-art Huffman codecs in decoding throughput. Additionally, we show how ANS-coding can be selectively applied to skewed nodes in this structure, yielding compression ratios approaching those of ANS-based codecs while preserving very high decompression speeds.

</details>


### [130] [Worst-Case Update Complexity of the Preisach Extremum Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05245)
*Piotr Frydrych*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析与数据结构优化，彻底解决了Preisach极值栈在最坏情况下的复杂性难题，证明了通过指状树可将操作时间复杂度从 $Θ(k)$ 优化至 $O(\log k)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Preisach极值栈算法虽然在分摊意义上具有 $O(1)$ 的高效性能，但在对抗性输入下表现较差（最坏情况为 $Θ(k)$），缺乏对该类算子复杂性界限的系统性理论研究。

Method: 1. 理论复杂性分析：证明了任何紧凑的最小化表示在最坏情况下均不可避免 $Θ(k)$ 的输出变化。
2. 算法改进：利用Preisach擦除属性的单调性，将边界检测优化至 $O(\log k)$。
3. 数据结构优化：引入指状树（finger-tree）实现搜寻与删除操作的 $O(\log k)$ 最坏情况时间复杂度。

Result: 1. 证明了任何紧凑的最小化表示最坏情况至少为 $Θ(k)$。
2. 建立了边界检测的 $O(\log k)$ 上界。
3. 提供了一种基于指状树的实现方案，在保持算子最小性的前提下，实现了搜寻与删除操作的 $O(\log k)$ 最坏情况时间复杂度。

Conclusion: 该研究通过三种不同层面的复杂性分析，确定了Preisach极值栈在处理速率无关算子时的最坏情况复杂性界限，并证明了可以通过指状树（finger-tree）实现 $O(\log k)$ 的最坏情况时延，从而解决了该问题的复杂性最优性问题。

Abstract: The Preisach extremum stack $Π_n$ is the minimal sufficient statistic for the class $\mathcal{R}$ of computable rate-independent functionals in the Kolmogorov complexity sense [1]. Its standard update algorithm runs in amortised $O(1)$ time, but adversarial inputs can force $Θ(k)$ operations per step (where $k$ is the current depth). We establish a three-level complexity picture: (i) any compact exact $\mathcal{R}$-minimal representation incurs $Θ(k)$ output changes per step in the worst case (in a model-independent output-change metric); (ii) the monotone ordering of the Preisach wiping property enables binary search, reducing boundary detection to $O(log k)$, though physical deletion remains $Θ(d)$; (iii) a finger-tree implementation achieves $O(log k)$ worst-case time per step for both search and deletion, at the cost of a more complex data structure, while maintaining exact $\mathcal{R}$-minimality with no approximation error. These results settle the worst-case complexity of the Preisach extremum stack across all three levels.

</details>


### [131] [Exponential Quantum Space Advantage for Approximating Max-$k$SAT in the Streaming Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05366)
*Haoyu Wang,Guangxu Yang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Max-kSAT和Max-2OR的量子流算法，通过仅用polylog(n)的空间实现了优于经典流算法的近似效果，确认了量子流模型在Max-kSAT等问题上具有指数级的空间优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索量子流式计算模型在解决典型组合优化问题（Max-kSAT和Max-2CSPs）时，相比于经典算法（需要Ω(√n)空间以达到相近近似比）是否具有空间复杂度上的指数级优势。

Method: 设计并分析了一次性（one-pass）量子流算法，利用polylog(n)空间复杂度的量子算法分别处理Max-kSAT（0.7172近似比）和Max-2OR（0.7425近似比）问题。

Result: 1. Max-kSAT：实现了0.7172近似比，突破了经典算法在polylog空间下的瓶颈（约为0.7071），证明存在指数级空间优势；2. Max-2OR：实现了0.7425近似比；3. 理论成果：完成了对所有布尔Max-2CSPs量子空间优势的完整分类。

Conclusion: 本文通过在Max-kSAT和Max-2OR问题上展示仅需polylog(n)空间即可达到特定近似比的量子流算法，证明了量子计算在流式处理模型中相比经典算法具有指数级的空间优势，并完成了对所有布尔Max-2CSPs量子空间优势的分类。

Abstract: In this paper, we give a one-pass quantum streaming algorithm for Max-$k$SAT that uses $\operatorname{polylog}(n)$ space and achieves a $0.7172$-approximation on instances with $n$ variables. In contrast, prior work by Chou, Golovnev, and Velusamy (FOCS 2020) implies that achieving an approximation ratio better than $\sqrt{2}/2 \approx 0.7071$ for Max-$k$SAT requires $Ω(\sqrt{n})$ space for any classical streaming algorithm. Therefore, it yields an exponential quantum space advantage for Max-$k$SAT in the streaming setting.
  We further give a one-pass quantum streaming algorithm for Max-2OR that uses $\operatorname{polylog}(n)$ space and achieves a $0.7425$-approximation on instances with $n$ variables. Combining with the known results, it gives a complete classification of quantum space advantages for all Boolean Max-2CSPs.

</details>


### [132] [Learning-Augmented Online Minimization with Dual Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05380)
*Christian Coester,Alexa Tudose,Alexander Turoczy*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用对偶线性规划预测的在线最小化算法框架，通过利用对偶解的高稳定性特性，在度量任务系统等问题上实现了比传统方法更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的在线算法往往难以在性能和适应性之间取得平衡，且现有的预测增强算法多聚焦于原始解或在线最大化问题，缺乏针对在线最小化问题的有效预测方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于对偶预测的在线学习增强算法框架；利用机器学习预测对偶线性规划的最优解，并证明了对偶解在实例扰动下具有比原始解更好的稳定性。

Result: 为度量任务系统（metrical task systems）和层级集合覆盖（laminar set cover）问题设计了算法；通过理论分析证实了性能提升，并通过 $k$-服务器问题和停车许可问题的实验进行了验证。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了利用对偶线性规划的机器学习预测，可以有效改善在线最小化问题的算法性能。这种基于对偶解的稳定性策略在处理在线任务系统和层级集合覆盖问题时表现出优越性，为在线算法的设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We present learning-augmented algorithms for two general classes of online minimization problems: metrical task systems and laminar set cover. Both algorithms achieve improved theoretical guarantees using machine-learned predictions of an optimal solution to the dual linear program. Unlike optimal primal solutions, which can change drastically under tiny instance perturbations, these dual solutions are much more stable, which ensures the existence of good (and learnable) predictions for families of similar instances. While previous work has used dual predictions in offline settings and for online maximization problems, our algorithms are, to the best of our knowledge, the first demonstration that such dual predictions can be effective for online minimization. Our theoretical results are complemented by experiments on the $k$-server problem and the parking permit problem.

</details>


### [133] [The Cascade Log: Reference-Stable Windowing over Tiered Append Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05467)
*Faruk Alpay,Levent Sarioglu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出 Cascade Log 解决分层追加存储中的引用稳定性问题，通过区间映射实现高效、无异常的数据检索，且在空间与更新代价上达到理论最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期的追加结构（如编辑日志、事件存储）通常采用分层存储以节省内存，但这破坏了引用稳定性，导致读取时常发生悬空、陈旧、损坏或快照偏移等跨层异常。

Method: 提出了一种基于区间映射（Interval Map）的引用稳定分层追加结构 —— Cascade Log，它将多个单例条目折叠为摘要驱动的区间节点，并通过不可变根实现快照一致性。

Result: Cascade Log 在空间复杂度上达到 $Θ(A)$（$A$ 为碎片数），支持 $O(\log A)$ 的点查询和 $O(\log A+k)$ 的范围查询，在追加为主的工作负载下表现出优异的亚线性扩展性能，并通过实验验证了其异常消除能力。

Conclusion: Cascade Log 通过区间映射（interval map）和不可变根（immutable roots）有效地解决了分层追加日志中的引用稳定性问题，在保证空间和查询效率匹配理论下界（$Ω(A)$ 空间和 $Ω(\log A+k)$ 查询）的同时，实现了对分片追加历史的亚线性增长。

Abstract: A long-running append-mostly sequence, such as an edit log, event store, or versioned working set, is usually tiered into a bounded hot stratum and colder folded summaries. This saves memory but breaks stable references: a handle minted while a record is hot may later be resolved after the record has moved into a digest, after it has been superseded, or while a fold is in flight. We define the resulting cross-tier anomalies--dangling, stale, corrupt, and snapshot-skewed resolution--and present the Cascade Log, a reference-stable tiered append structure. The structure keeps a single persistent coalescing interval map over handles as the sole authority on each live version; folding a contiguous run replaces many singleton entries by one digest-backed interval node, and immutable roots provide snapshot tokens. Its cost is characterized by the fragmentation $A$, the number of index pieces, namely live handles plus maximal same-digest runs. The index uses $Θ(A)$ space, resolves a point in $O(\log A)$, reports a $k$-handle range in $O(\log A+k)$, and performs $a$ appends and $s$ supersedes in $O((a/B+s)\log A)$ update work for fold block size $B$. Matching lower bounds show that $Ω(A)$ space and $Ω(\log A+k)$ ordered range cost are unavoidable, and an adversary can force $A=Θ(s)$. Thus the index is sublinear on append-dominated histories and grows linearly only under fragmenting edits. A reference implementation and reproducible experiments to $10^6$ records validate the anomaly-freedom and the fragmentation bounds.

</details>


### [134] [Online Min-Cost Matching with General Arrivals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05546)
*Josh Ascher,Eric Balkanski,Jason Chatzitheodorou,Vasilis Gkatzelis*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了完全在线的最小成本匹配问题，指出在该场景下，随机排列模型与未知 i.i.d. 模型之间存在显著的性能差异（前者无界，后者为 $O(\log^2 n)$）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的在线最小成本匹配问题通常假设一侧是静态的。但在许多实际应用中，所有匹配方都是动态到达的。本文旨在研究这种更一般的“双方均在线”的最小成本完美匹配问题，并评估不同输入假设下的算法性能。

Method: 通过理论分析探讨了不同输入模型（对抗性、随机排列、未知 i.i.d.）下的竞争比。在未知 i.i.d. 模型下，设计了一种能够应对未知分布的算法来处理匹配问题。

Result: 证明了对于任意算法，在对抗性模型和随机排列模型下，竞争比均为无界；而在未知 i.i.d. 模型下，给出了一个 $O(\log^2 n)$ 竞争比的算法。

Conclusion: 在完全在线的最小成本完美匹配问题中，算法性能在对抗性模型和随机排列模型下是无界的；但在未知独立同分布（i.i.d.）模型下，存在 $O(\log^2 n)$ 竞争比的算法。这一发现证实了随机排列模型与未知 i.i.d. 模型在竞争比可实现性上的本质差异。

Abstract: In the classic online min-cost matching problem, the goal is to match a sequence of requests that arrive dynamically over time to a set of static servers, aiming to minimize the total cost of the matching. This assumes that there are two distinct "sides" and that only one of these sides arrives online, but many of the motivating applications violate these assumptions. We study online min-cost perfect-matching when \emph{all} participants arrive online and, upon arrival, they need to either be matched to someone from a waiting pool or to join the waiting pool. We evaluate the competitive ratios achievable in different input models and show that for both the adversarial and the random-order input models the competitive ratio of any algorithm is unbounded. In contrast, for i.i.d. arrivals we give a $O( \log^2{n})$-competitive algorithm, even if the distribution that generates these arrivals is unknown to the algorithm. This result implies a rare example of separation in the achievable competitive ratio between the random-order and the unknown-i.i.d. input models.

</details>


### [135] [Multi-Objective Submodular Maximization with Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05596)
*Ting Hou,Yanhao Wang,Yiping Wang,Cen Chen,Minghao Zhao,Fan Dang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了满足差分隐私（DP）约束下的多目标次模最大化（MOSM）问题，提出了两种集成DP机制的算法，并通过实验证明了其在实际应用中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的研究分别探讨了差分隐私下的单目标次模最大化和非隐私环境下的多目标次模最大化（MOSM），但尚无关于在满足差分隐私约束下处理MOSM问题的先验研究，因此本文旨在解决这一研究空白。

Method: 提出了两种新型算法：1. 基于经典贪心算法的扩展算法；2. 结合截断技术（truncation technique）的算法。两者均集成了差分隐私机制。

Result: 通过数值实验（包括最大覆盖与设施选址应用），验证了所提算法在多目标设置下的有效性与计算效率。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了多目标次模最大化（MOSM）在差分隐私（DP）环境下的研究空白，提出的两种算法在隐私保护和优化性能之间实现了有效的权衡。

Abstract: In this paper, we study multi-objective submodular maximization (MOSM) subject to a cardinality constraint under differential privacy (DP). Specifically, we aim to select a set of at most $k \in \mathbb{Z}_{+}$ elements to maximize the minimum of $d > 1$ monotone submodular functions while satisfying $\varepsilon$-DP. Although extensive studies have been conducted on both differentially private single-objective submodular maximization on sensitive data and non-private MOSM, to the best of our knowledge, there has not yet been any prior work on MOSM with DP. We propose two novel algorithms: the first extends the classic greedy algorithm and the second employs a truncation technique, both of which are integrated with DP mechanisms for privacy protection and achieve approximation guarantees for MOSM. Finally, we conduct numerical experiments on two submodular maximization applications, namely maximum coverage and facility location, in multi-objective settings to validate the efficacy and efficiency of our proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [136] [Detecting Large Quasi-cliques on Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05809)
*Luciano Gualà,Simone Pellegrini,Luca Pepè Sciarria,Alessandro Straziota*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文探讨了动态网络中的准派系检测问题，针对 Pang 等人（WWW 2024）的静态方法，设计了针对增量和完全动态场景的高效算法，显著提升了计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现实网络中大型准派系（quasi-cliques）检测的需求，现有算法主要针对静态网络，缺乏对动态变化（如边插入和删除）的高效处理方法，亟需将 Pang 等人（WWW 2024）的先进静态算法扩展至动态环境。

Method: 1. 提出了一个保持当前图准派系精确解的完全动态基准算法（更新复杂度 O~(Δ)）。
2. 在仅增量场景下，利用顶点 γ-度的动态估计算法，实现了 O(log Δ) 的更新时间。
3. 在完全动态场景下，设计了在保证准派系质量的前提下，通过近似维护实现显著性能提升的优化算法。

Result: 1. 增量更新算法相比于基准算法在真实数据集上实现了高达 207 倍的加速。
2. 完全动态算法相比基准算法实现了高达 21 倍的加速，且准派系的规模和密度仅有可接受的损失。
3. 提供了算法的理论复杂度分析及充分的实验验证。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了首个处理动态网络中准派系检测的高效算法框架，通过对不同更新场景（仅增量 vs 全动态）的设计，在保持高质量解的同时显著提升了计算性能，有效拓展了 Pang 等人（WWW 2024）研究的适用范围。

Abstract: Motivated by the problem of detecting large and cohesive groups of vertices in real networks, the task of finding large \emph{quasi-cliques} has attracted considerable attention across different research areas. From a computational complexity perspective, strong inapproximability results are known for this problem, yet several heuristics have been proposed to identify large quasi-cliques in real-world networks. Recently, [Pang \emph{et al.}, (WWW 2024)] introduced a similarity-based approach that represents the current state of the art. In this work, we extend that approach to \emph{dynamic} networks, thereby addressing an open problem posed by [Pang \emph{et al.}, (WWW 2024)].
  We first present a Baseline fully dynamic algorithm where edges of the network can be both inserted and deleted. The algorithm exactly maintains the same quasi-clique returned by the algorithm by Pang et al. on the current graph, with update time $\widetilde{O}(Δ)$, where $Δ$ is the maximum degree. We then focus on the practically relevant incremental case, where only edge insertions are allowed, and design an algorithm with $O(\log Δ)$ update time. This method leverages a novel technique for dynamically maintaining accurate estimates of vertex $γ$-degrees, a core component of framework by Pang et al., and achieves up to $207\times$ speed-up over the Baseline while preserving comparable solution quality. Finally, we extend the approach to the fully dynamic setting, supporting both insertions and deletions, obtaining up to $21\times$ speed-up with limited and acceptable loss in quasi-clique size and density. We provide a formal analysis of our algorithms and validate them through an extensive set of experiments on real-world datasets.

</details>


### [137] [Workload-Aware Autotuning of Block Size in Square-Root Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06145)
*Ruize Zhao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用机器学习（KNN）对平方根分解的块大小进行自动调优，替代传统的 B=sqrt(n) 经验法则，实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的平方根分解块大小选择 B=sqrt(n) 虽然在渐进分析上是自然的，但在实际工程实现中往往不是最快的，存在显著的性能优化空间。

Method: 将块大小自适应调整视为算法工程问题，利用机器学习方法（测试了 KNN 等模型）对工作负载进行建模，并通过重复分组交叉验证进行训练与评估；同时引入置信门控（confidence gate）来控制潜在的性能下降风险。

Result: KNN-9 模型将平均遗憾值（mean regret）从 1.2882 降至 1.0646，几何平均加速比达到 1.151x；置信门控有效地减少了性能损耗，在跨数据集（如 Zipf-Hotspot）测试中也表现出优于固定块大小的稳健性。

Conclusion: 固定块大小选择 B=sqrt(n) 在实际应用中并非最优，块大小选择应具备工作负载感知和平台感知能力。机器学习模型（特别是 KNN）不仅能有效提升性能，且能捕捉任务统计特征，而非仅仅依赖死记硬背。

Abstract: The textbook choice B=sqrt(n) for square-root decomposition is asymptotically natural, but it is not always the fastest implementation choice. We study block-size autotuning as a reproducible algorithm-engineering problem and show that a learned workload model can improve over fixed sqrt(n) on the tested implementation. Under repeated grouped cross-validation, the best policy is a full-feature KNN-9 model that reduces mean regret from 1.2882 to 1.0646 and yields a paired geometric-mean speedup of 1.151x. A confidence gate retains most of that gain while reducing slowdowns. A family-free full-observation follow-up remains better than fixed blocking, which suggests that the model is learning from workload statistics rather than memorizing labels. In contrast, short-prefix variants do not produce a successful low-overhead online tuner in the current prototype. External validation is selective but supportive: Zipf-Hotspot is the strongest out-of-distribution case, and a six-window Baleen follow-up still improves over fixed blocking. Overall, block-size choice is workload aware and platform aware, and the fixed sqrt(n) rule leaves substantial performance on the table.

</details>


### [138] [Temporal matching in trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06439)
*Márk Hunor Juhász,Péter Madarasi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究了时间树上的最大匹配问题（Δ-匹配和γ-匹配），证明了其在多数一般情形下是NP难和APX难的，但在特定限制条件下可多项式求解，并给出了这些问题的PTAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时间图（Temporal Graphs）模型下，由于节点的匹配受到时间维度约束（Δ-匹配与γ-匹配），传统的匹配算法不再适用，需要探索这些在树形结构下受限匹配问题的计算复杂度和算法可行性。

Method: 通过逻辑归约（Reduction）将不同时间模型联系起来，使用动态规划（Dynamic Programming）算法解决特定约束下的问题，并利用复杂性理论证明硬度结果。

Result: 1. 对于Δ≥2及稀疏情形，最大Δ-匹配在时间树上是NP难的；最大γ-匹配亦具有类似硬度且在二部图上是APX难的。2. 识别出若干易解情形（如每条边只出现一次的Δ-匹配）。3. 提出用于有界局部使用和局部稀疏假设下的动态规划算法，并证明了最大Δ-匹配和γ-匹配在时间树上存在多项式时间近似方案（PTAS）。

Conclusion: 研究了时间树图上的最大匹配问题，证明了Δ-匹配和γ-匹配在多种情形下的NP难性与APX难性，同时也识别了若干多项式时间可解的特殊情形，并证明了PTAS的存在性。

Abstract: We study maximum matching problems in temporal graphs whose underlying graph is a tree. We consider two temporal models. In a $Δ$-matching, selected time edges sharing an endpoint must have time ticks differing by at least $Δ$. In a $γ$-matching, the selected objects are blocks of $γ$ consecutive appearances of the same underlying edge. We also consider the related ordered static problem of $d$-distance matchings.
  We show that maximum $Δ$-matching remains NP-hard on temporal trees for every $Δ\geq 2$, even in the sparse case where each edge appears at most twice. Using a reduction between the temporal models, we obtain the analogous result for maximum $γ$-matching on temporal trees, even when each edge admits at most two $γ$-edges. We also show, via a reduction from $d$-distance matching, that maximum $γ$-matching is APX-hard even when the underlying graph is bipartite.
  Complementing these hardness results, we identify several tractable cases. We prove that maximum $Δ$-matching is polynomial-time solvable on temporal trees in which every edge appears exactly once, and that maximum $γ$-matching is polynomial-time solvable when each edge admits at most one $γ$-edge. We also give dynamic-programming algorithms under bounded local-use and local-sparsity assumptions, and derive polynomial-time solvability of maximum $d$-distance matching when the input bipartite graph is a tree. Finally, we prove that both maximum $Δ$-matching and maximum $γ$-matching admit polynomial-time approximation schemes on temporal trees.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [The Evaluation Blind Spot: A Stereological Theory of Benchmark Coverage for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05169)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过建立LLM基准测试的立体学理论，揭示了目前排行榜普遍存在的低维覆盖缺陷及严重的排名不稳定性，并利用子模优化提出更高效的评估子集构建方案，同时从数学上解决了Gardner问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM基准测试评分体系在面对高维能力空间时存在结构性盲点，导致排名不稳定和信息冗余，需要量化这种覆盖范围的局限性并寻求更优的评价子集。

Method: 1. 提出基于立体学的LLM基准测试覆盖理论，推导 Hausdorff 距离界限；2. 建立卡方投影模型进行模拟实验；3. 采用子模贪心算法（Nemhauser算法）优化基准集选择；4. 利用最优恢复理论解决Gardner问题；5. 通过对排行榜和Chatbot Arena的实证数据进行反事实验证。

Result: 1. 主流排行榜的有效维度（d_eff）仅为 2.86-4.80 之间，结构性盲点远超统计噪声；2. 模拟实验显示排名前两名的模型在随机拆分下极易换位（成功率92%）；3. 发现仅需7个关键基准测试即可覆盖90%的能力空间；4. 验证了特征结构（eigenstructure）在识别不可替代评价指标方面的有效性。

Conclusion: LLM基准测试存在显著的结构性盲点，现有的评分系统即便在统计上看似稳定，实际上也无法准确区分模型能力，且存在极高的排名不稳定性。通过子模贪心算法可以将基准集精简为一个小而核心的子集，同时保持高覆盖率并增强跨时间的可迁移性。此外，本文解决了Gardner 1995年提出的问题，为凸支撑函数的最佳恢复理论提供了数学基础。

Abstract: We give a stereological theory of LLM benchmark coverage. For any suite with effective dimensionality d_eff, the visible Hausdorff distance between two convex capability profiles consistent with the same scores is bounded by epsilon + C R m^(-1/(d_eff-1)), with matching Lipschitz lower bound. Empirically, three independent leaderboards (Open LLM v2, an extended 12-benchmark suite, LiveBench) all have d_eff in [2.86, 4.80] on their competitive frontier; the structural blind spot exceeds the observed runner-up score gap by two orders of magnitude and dominates statistical noise by 52-127x. Under a chi-squared projection model, the isotropic prior is the optimistic case; across six hidden-capability priors and four ambient dimensions the simulated half-split swap rate of the top two models stays in [0.38, 0.49], and a 500-trial random visible/held-out split shows that 92% of trials swap the top-1 ranking with on average 2.83 of 5 top-5 models changing. A submodular greedy algorithm with the Nemhauser (1 - 1/e) guarantee finds a stable core of 4 benchmarks; 7 of 12 suffice for 90% coverage, and the trained subset transfers across temporal quarters with 93-97% retention. A counterfactual validation across 12 internal benchmarks and 27 Chatbot Arena categories confirms that the eigenstructure predicts which evaluations are irreplaceable (rho = -0.69, p = 0.013 for removal disruption) and which external evaluations bring new information (rho = +0.38). As a second, independent theoretical contribution, we resolve Gardner's Problem 1.5 (1995) for C^2 support functions, establishing the minimax rate Theta(R/(kappa m^(2/(D-1)))) in general dimension via optimal recovery theory on S^(D-1).

</details>


### [140] [ERRORQUAKE: Heavy-Tailed Error Severity Distributions in Open-Weight Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05170)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出仅使用准确率无法评估大模型幻觉，提出了Errorquake-10k基准，通过分析错误的严重程度分布（b指标），证明了即使在相同准确率下模型间的错误表现也存在本质差异，呼吁将严重程度分布作为关键评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的幻觉基准测试大多只给出标量的错误率（scalar error rate），将所有错误同等对待，无法体现不同严重程度错误（如日期错误 vs. 虚构案例）带来的影响差异。需要更细粒度的评估方法来揭示模型在错误表现上的定性差异。

Method: 1. 构建了包含10,000个查询的Errorquake-10k基准测试，涵盖8个领域和5个难度等级。
2. 对响应进行0-4的连续严重程度评分。
3. 计算并拟合21个开源模型各自定义的严重程度分布指数（b值，即Gutenberg-Richter分布上尾斜率）。
4. 通过人机对比验证（ICC=0.85）及模型间的对比度分析，量化错误分布的差异。
5. 证明了“非归约定理”（Non-Reducibility Theorem），即严重程度分布与错误率在信息上是不互补的。
6. 对错误类型进行分类，发现错误类型随严重程度的变化存在规律（低严重度多为检索缺失，高严重度多为伪造）。

Result: 1. 在相同准确率下，不同模型表现出显著不同的错误严重程度分布。
2. 在210对模型中，有85对在准确率匹配的情况下仍有显著的严重程度分布差异。
3. 证明了约64.5%的模型分布差异无法仅通过错误率来解释。
4. 模型规模越大，倾向产生的错误类型发生系统性偏移。

Conclusion: 准确率并非评价大语言模型幻觉的唯一指标。错误严重程度分布（Severity Distribution）提供了关于模型能力的重要特征，应与准确率一起作为评估标准。

Abstract: At matched accuracy, open-weight LLMs differ substantially in the shape of their error severity distribution -- a difference invisible to the scalar error rate. Hallucination benchmarks report a single error count and treat all errors as equivalent, yet a wrong date and a fabricated court ruling differ by orders of magnitude. We introduce Errorquake-10k, a 10,000-query benchmark scoring each response on a continuous 0-4 severity scale across 8 domains and 5 difficulty tiers, and we fit per-model severity distributions for 21 open-weight models. For each model we estimate a severity distribution index (b, the Gutenberg-Richter upper-tail slope) with 95% bootstrap confidence intervals. Headline: across the 210 model pairs, 85 have disjoint 95% b confidence intervals at matched accuracy (|Delta epsilon| < 0.05) on human-consensus scoring, e.g. deepseek-v3.2 vs. ministral-14b at epsilon = 0.586 and Delta b = 0.47. A 519-item three-rater human validation study confirms measurement reliability (ICC(2,k=3) = 0.85), validates the LLM-judge ranking (rho = 0.89), and confirms the dense-model scaling correlation on human data (rho_s = -0.86). We prove a Non-Reducibility Theorem showing that severity profile and error rate are informationally non-redundant (I(b; model | epsilon) = 1.56 bits; 64.5% of cross-model b variance is unexplained by epsilon). A severity mechanism taxonomy (kappa = 0.83) reveals that error type shifts categorically with severity: low-severity errors are retrievals (71%); high-severity errors are fabrications (39%) -- and this composition differs by model size (p < 0.0001). Severity distribution should be reported alongside accuracy; it carries discriminative information that the error rate cannot.

</details>


### [141] [Staged Factorial Screening for Budget-Constrained Micro-Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05186)
*Felipe Chavarro Polania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对低预算微预训练任务，提出并验证了一种基于多阶段因子设计的筛选流程，证明了从短时筛选过渡到“桥接模型”扩展比盲目的超参搜索更具成本效益与性能稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预算有限的微预训练场景下，如何高效地在共享加速器上从大量候选配置中筛选出最优方案，以避免在低质量方向上浪费昂贵的计算预算。

Method: 构建了一个基于单GPU的受限预训练实验框架，执行了613次实验，包含短时（2-10分钟）分段部分因子设计（staged fractional-factorial workflow）、全量重跑（full seeded reruns）、锚点检查、贪婪/随机基线对比，以及长时（12-24小时）的桥接模型验证。使用Benjamini-Hochberg校正处理多重假设检验。

Result: 短时预算内参数（batch, depth, width）的惩罚效应最明显，随预算增加而减弱；通过因子设计能有效识别部分有效配置；随机搜索虽能找到强基准但缺乏因子归因能力；桥接模型（bridge anchor）在长时预训练（24小时）中展现了最低的损失，但其性能受硬件环境影响。

Conclusion: 研究推荐了一种受限资源下的微预训练策略：利用短时高效的因子设计实验（designed screens）识别高惩罚方向，通过重复实验锚定有潜力的配置，并在缩小空间内进行局部优化。证据表明以“桥接模型”（bridge model）为核心进行扩展在24小时内表现最优，而非追求硬件无关的排名或通用的超参数优化。

Abstract: Budget-constrained micro-pretraining often requires triaging many candidate recipes on a shared accelerator before larger search budgets are spent. We study whether a staged fractional-factorial workflow can recover stable early effect structure in this setting. On a fixed autoresearch-derived single-GPU training loop, we run 613 experiments across pilot and follow-up screens at 2, 5, and 10 minutes; full 16-condition seeded reruns at 5 and 10 minutes; targeted seeded anchor checks; same-host greedy and matched-cost random baselines; a 60-minute bridge package; and bounded Windows A100 and Linux L40S anchor continuations through 24 hours. Main penalties from total batch, depth, and width are largest at short budgets and relax as budget increases. Within the predeclared seeded full-screen families, D, A, B, and C retain non-zero estimates at 5 and 10 minutes after within-budget Benjamini-Hochberg correction, while E does not. Random search can reach strong incumbents in this 32-condition space, but repeatedly in the same low-penalty region and without factor attribution. The 60-minute bridge anchor has the lowest mean, although that package does not separate workflow refinement from the larger bridge model's capacity advantage. In bounded 12-hour and 24-hour three-anchor continuations on both hosts, the bridge has the lowest sample mean while the non-bridge ordering stays host-sensitive. We therefore present a bounded methods result: use short designed screens to identify high-penalty directions, confirm promising anchors under repeated runs, and refine locally inside the reduced space. The evidence supports a bridge-centered recommendation through 24 hours on two hosts, not hardware-invariant ranking or general hyperparameter-optimization superiority.

</details>


### [142] [PyCC.id: A package for hypothesis-driven equation discovery with structural identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05191)
*Federico J. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文介绍了 PyCC 工具库，通过引入特征曲线骨架方法，系统化地整合领域先验假设来辅助常微分方程（ODE）的发现，解决了传统数据驱动建模中逆问题病态及模型歧义的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的方程发现（逆问题）通常存在病态性，导致产生多个数学上拟合效果相似但物理意义不同的候选模型。现有的约束方法仍会导致多解问题，且缺乏交互式、系统化的工具来结合领域专家的先验知识进行模型识别与筛选。

Method: 该论文提出了一种基于“骨架”（skeletons）的假设驱动方法。通过定义与常微分方程（ODE）族相关的特征曲线骨架，结合领域先验知识进行迭代模型细化，并集成了神经网络、符号回归和稀疏回归等多种方程发现范式。在此基础上，作者开发了开源 Python 库 PyCC。

Result: 开发并发布了 PyCC 开源 Python 库。该工具支持用户以模块化的方式定义骨架和假设，从而能够利用结构可辨识性特性筛选模型，显著提升了从时间序列数据中发现准确 ODE 的过程效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PyCC 库提供了一个灵活且模块化的框架，通过整合特征曲线（CC）驱动的骨架方法，有效地解决了数据驱动方程发现中病态逆问题导致的多解性问题，便于研究人员进行假设驱动的 ODE 模型构建。

Abstract: Data-driven equation discovery is fundamentally an inverse problem that seeks to infer the governing differential equations of a system directly from time-series measurements. A known issue is the ill-conditioned nature of the inverse problem, which frequently produces multiple mathematical models that fit the data similarly well. One path to address this issue is by incorporating known hypotheses and constraints into the training phase beforehand. While this approach effectively reduces the search space, it still results in multiple candidate models, forcing practitioners to rely on post-hoc manual filtering based on their own domain expertise. A recent approach incorporates structural `skeletons' inspired by characteristic curves (CCs), defining a hypothesis-driven methodology. In this methodology, practitioners define a skeleton, which is associated with a family of ordinary differential equations (ODEs), and then add their hypotheses and priors based on their domain knowledge to refine the obtained model iteratively. An important advantage of this approach is that some skeletons have demonstrable structural identifiability properties, which are useful for checking whether the skeleton is correct or should be discarded. Furthermore, this formalism enables the use of multiple equation discovery paradigms due to its modularity (such as neural networks, symbolic regression, and sparse regression). In this work, we present the Python library PyCC, which condenses these efforts into a flexible tool that allows researchers and engineers to seamlessly define their skeletons and hypotheses to discover ODEs from time-dependent data.

</details>


### [143] [Temporal Preference Concepts and their Functions in a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05194)
*Ian Rios-Sialer,Shantanu Darveshi,Shuai Jiang,Avigya Paudel,Anastasiia Pronina,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过机械可解释性技术揭示了LLM内部处理“决策时间偏好”的机制，发现该偏好在模型内部存在特定编码，并证明了通过转向向量可主动干预和控制模型的长短期决策倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）日益被用于涉及“短期收益 vs 长期后果”决策的任务，但目前尚不清楚LLMs底层是如何表征及解决这一权衡问题的。旨在揭示其内部决策机制，并探索如何实现主动控制。

Method: 1. 因果定位（Causal Localization）：使用基于梯度的归因分析（gradient-based attribution）和激活修补（activation patching）技术定位模型（Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507）中负责时间偏好的子图；
2. 几何分析：研究残差流中时间跨度（time horizon）信息的编码几何结构；
3. 行为评估：对比分析LLM与人类在跨期决策中的折现差异及稳定性；
4. 转向实验：利用转向向量（steering vectors）对模型的时间偏好进行实时调整。

Result: 1. 成功在Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507模型中层至高层定位到了时间偏好的关键子图；
2. 发现模型内部残差流准确编码了时间跨度的几何表征；
3. 实证结果表明LLM贴现未来的速率远低于人类，且该偏好在不同上下文间高度不稳定；
4. 验证了转向向量能够有效干预并改变模型的时间决策偏好。

Conclusion: 通过机械可解释性方法定位并验证了LLM中时间偏好（即远见与短视权衡）的内部表征，并证明了通过干预（转向向量）控制模型决策的可行性。这为实现可控的AI规划与推理提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed to make decisions that require trading off near-term gains against long-term consequences, yet little is known about how they internally represent or resolve these tradeoffs. In this work, we causally localize an underlying subgraph for temporal preference in a distilled LLM (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), identifying mid-to-upper-layer nodes through converging evidence from gradient-based attribution and activation patching. We find that the geometry of time horizon is encoded in the residual stream at the expected localized layers. A behavioral analysis reveals that unintervened LLMs discount the future several times less steeply than humans, yet this preference is unstable across contexts, motivating explicit control rather than implicit reliance on training. Finally, we find suggestive evidence that steering vectors can shift temporal preference. Our work demonstrates how mechanistic interpretability can bring us closer to reliable control over how LLMs plan and reason

</details>


### [144] [State commitment learning: training language models to distinguish computation from memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05201)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对推理模型过度依赖中间思考草稿的问题，本文提出CERL训练框架，通过反事实擦除机制强迫模型学会区分重要状态与临时计算，在不损害性能的前提下提升了模型输出的稳定性和逻辑纯粹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理模型无法区分有效的计算过程与无用的、错误的“推理残余”（如废弃的尝试路径），导致下游预测受到无效历史记录的干扰，影响模型表现的稳定性。

Method: 1. 提出“状态承诺学习”（State Commitment Learning）目标，区分永久存储状态与临时计算内容；2. 定义“持续状态充分性”（Persistent-state sufficiency）作为可训练的准则；3. 提出“反事实擦除强化学习”（CERL），通过对比保留思考过程与擦除思考过程的路径，仅对后者奖励，迫使模型学习将核心逻辑固化。

Result: 在数学、长链逻辑、科学问答及多轮工具使用任务中，CERL显著降低了答案生成对隐藏思考过程的依赖，表现超越了单纯关注正确率的奖励模型（Correctness-only RL）和长回答监督微调（Long-answer SFT）基线。

Conclusion: CERL框架成功减少了模型推理对“中间思考过程”的依赖，确保了最终答案的鲁棒性和可靠性，且未牺牲模型在多项复杂任务中的准确率。

Abstract: Reasoning language models do not distinguish tokens used for computation from tokens that constitute persistent state: once generated, all hidden thoughts remain in context and influence future predictions. As a result, downstream reasoning may depend on failed attempts, dead ends, and private scratch work that should not be safely relied on later. We recast this phenomenon as a new training objective, state commitment learning: training models to explicitly distinguish information that should be committed as persistent state from temporary computation that can be discarded. We define a counterfactual criterion, persistent-state sufficiency, which makes it trainable and measurable whether an answer remains usable after hidden thoughts are erased. We then propose Counterfactual Erasure RL (CERL), which evaluates, under the same prefix, both a path that keeps hidden thoughts and a path that erases them, and gives reward only when the erasure path remains correct. We also introduce the Erasure Dependence Protocol and show across mathematics, long-chain logic, scientific QA, and multi-turn tool-use evaluation that CERL substantially reduces answer dependence on hidden thoughts without sacrificing accuracy, consistently outperforming correctness-only RL and long-answer SFT baselines.

</details>


### [145] [Adaptive Oscillatory-State Alignment for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06010)
*Zhangyao Song,Ziqiong Li,Xiangfei Qiu,Chao Zha,Yinfei Xu,Tao Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AOSNET是一种新型长时序列预测框架，弃用了传统的固定周期模板，转向基于希尔伯特变换的自适应振荡状态对齐，能灵活处理非平稳时间序列中的动态频率和相位变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的周期性预测方法（如固定周期、全局频谱或固定模板）难以建模现实世界中复杂的非平稳周期动力学（如演变的周期、相位漂移和频率波动），导致模型与实际时间序列动态失配。

Method: 提出了AOSNET（希尔伯特引导的预测框架）：将周期预测重构为“自适应振荡状态对齐”问题。该方法通过从观测序列和可学习的全局振荡先验中提取解析信号描述符，利用描述符条件门控（Descriptor-conditioned gate）实现局部状态的动态对齐与修正，而非简单的模板匹配。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上实现了最先进（SOTA）或极具竞争力的预测精度，同时保持了较快的推理速度；受控实验验证了该方法在处理振幅调制、相位漂移和频率波动方面的显著优势。

Conclusion: AOSNET通过引入希尔伯特引导的自适应振荡状态对齐机制，克服了传统固定模板周期建模在面对动态周期变化（如振幅调制、相位漂移和频率波动）时的局限性，在多项基准测试中达到了SOTA性能，且在非平稳性越强的序列上表现越具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting benefits from inductive biases that expose recurring temporal structure. Existing periodic forecasting methods typically model recurrence through predefined periods, global spectral components, or fixed learnable templates. However, real-world temporal dynamics are rarely rigidly periodic: oscillatory behavior often evolves through amplitude modulation, phase drift, and local frequency variation. Under these conditions, fixed-template periodic modeling can become fundamentally mismatched to the underlying temporal states. We propose AOSNET, a Hilbert-guided forecasting framework that reformulates periodic forecasting from fixed template matching to adaptive oscillatory-state alignment. AOSNET extracts analytic-signal descriptors from both the observed sequence and a learnable global oscillatory prior, then adaptively aligns local states through a descriptor-conditioned gate that selectively preserves reliable observations while softly correcting mismatched regions. The learned prior serves not as a rigid repeated template but as a flexible oscillatory reference interpreted through local state dynamics. Experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art or highly competitive accuracy with fast inference speed. Controlled synthetic studies isolating amplitude modulation, phase drift, and local frequency variation confirm that the advantage of oscillatory-state alignment consistently increases as non-stationarity intensifies.

</details>


### [146] [Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Restores Symmetry in Deep Linear Networks with Multi-Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05219)
*Hee-Sung Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了在深度学习中，离散梯度下降的大步长优化会导致网络偏向于学习共享的特征表示，而非梯度流所预测的单一路径专业化，解释了深度在路径竞争中的动态调节作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往基于梯度流（Gradient Flow）的分析认为多路径网络会发生对称性破缺导致“赢家通吃”（单路径专业化），但该结论无法解释实际训练中观察到的现象，动机在于探究离散优化及大步长如何改变神经网络的路径选择过程。

Method: 利用深度线性网络模型，对比了连续梯度流（Gradient Flow）与离散大步长梯度下降（Gradient Descent）的优化动力学；通过分析损失函数的尖锐度（Sharpness）与路径分布的关系，推导了离散动力学中的重新平衡机制。

Result: 1. 单路径解是尖锐的极小值（Sharp Minima）；2. 分布式路径可降低尖锐度（ sharpness 随路径数和深度的增加而降低）；3. 训练初期表现出GF预测的对称性破缺，但后期在大步长梯度下降的“边缘稳定性”作用下，会进入重新平衡阶段，促使信号在多路径间重分布。

Conclusion: 通过分析梯度下降（Gradient Descent）在离散步长下的动力学，发现深度神经网络的训练并非总是导致“赢家通吃”的单一路径专业化，而是通过“边缘稳定性”（Edge of Stability）的振荡驱动信号重新分布，进而倾向于学习共享的特征表示。

Abstract: Recent analyses of multi-pathway Deep Linear Networks use Gradient Flow to predict a "winner-takes-all" specialization in which path symmetry breaks and each feature concentrates in a single pathway. In this work, we show that discrete Gradient Descent (GD) with a large step size tells a different story. We prove that single-path solutions are sharp minima, whereas distributing signals across pathways reduces sharpness by a factor that decreases with both the number of pathways and depth. Consequently, while early training reproduces the depth-driven symmetry breaking predicted by GF, oscillations at the Edge of Stability subsequently override this tendency and drive the network into a re-balancing phase, where signals redistribute across pathways. Together, these results clarify how depth shapes pathway competition and explain why large-step GD favors shared representations rather than persistent single-pathway dominance.

</details>


### [147] [Differentiable Efficient Operator Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05232)
*Xiaohuan Pei,Jiyuan Zhang,Yuanfan Guo,Weiguo Feng,Tao Huang,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种可微算子搜索框架，统一了现有的手动令牌缩减方式，通过自动搜索替代人工设计，在多模态模型中实现了更高效的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态基础模型多依赖人工设计的令牌缩减（token-reduction）算子，但这些算子缺乏通用框架，且人工设计可能并非最优。作者希望通过统一的理论框架将这些操作标准化，并利用自动化搜索提升模型效率。

Method: 提出了“高效算子搜索”（Efficient Operator Search）框架。该方法将现有的剪枝、合并、池化等操作统一到一个共享算子空间，通过参数化层激活、保留预算和操作行为，利用可微搜索策略在给定预算与成本约束下，自动搜索最优的令牌缩减策略。

Result: 该框架不仅能够复现代表性的人工设计基线，还能发掘出超越现有手动设计的混合算子。在多模态基准测试中，搜索到的算子在积极的视觉令牌缩减场景下表现出极具竞争力的准确性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过可微算子搜索（Differentiable Operator Search），可以将高效多模态推理的重心从人工设计转移到算法自动搜索，从而实现更优的准确率-效率权衡。

Abstract: Efficient multimodal foundation models often rely on manually designed token-reduction operators, such as pruning, merging, pooling, and adaptive reweighting. Although these operators appear different, we show that they can be interpreted as distinct regimes of a shared operator space. Based on this view, we introduce Efficient Operator Search, a differentiable framework that jointly searches where to reduce tokens, how many tokens to retain, and how reduced token information should be processed. The proposed search space parameterizes layer activation, retention budget, and operator behavior, while the search policy optimizes task performance under one-sided budget and cost constraints. This formulation recovers representative hand-designed baselines as special cases and further discovers hybrid operators beyond isolated manual designs. Experiments on multimodal benchmarks show that the searched operators achieve competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs, especially under aggressive visual-token reduction. These results suggest that efficient multimodal inference can be reframed from manual operator design to differentiable operator search.

</details>


### [148] [DiffSlack: Learning under Nonlinear Inequality Constraints via Learnable Slack Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05247)
*Ziqian Wang,Chenxi Fang,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffSlack 提出了一种可微投影层，通过可学习的松弛变量和高斯-牛顿投影，有效解决了神经网络中复杂非线性不等式约束的强制执行问题，实现了高约束满足率与计算高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理神经网络中非线性硬约束的方法通常存在结构限制或计算开销过大的问题，难以应对大规模、耦合紧密的约束场景，如车辆路径规划问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为 DiffSlack 的可微投影层。该方法通过引入可学习的松弛变量将不等式约束转化为等式约束，并结合阻尼高斯-牛顿（damped Gauss-Newton）投影算法，将原始预测映射到可行流形上；同时采用两阶段课程学习（curriculum learning）策略来稳定训练。

Result: 在包含 200 个非线性约束的车辆路径规划任务中，DiffSlack 相比现有基线方法实现了更高的规划成功率和几何约束满足度，且在推理预算相当的情况下表现更优。实车与 CARLA 仿真实验验证了其轨迹的执行效能。

Conclusion: DiffSlack 是一种实用且可扩展的方案，能够将复杂的非线性硬约束嵌入神经网络，在保持端到端可微性的同时，显著提高了约束满足率和规划成功率，适用于自动驾驶等工程领域。

Abstract: Enforcing nonlinear inequality constraints in neural networks remains challenging, especially when the output is subject to many coupled constraints. Existing hard constraint methods often impose structural restrictions on the constraint set or introduce substantial computational overhead for large-scale nonlinear problems. Here, we propose DiffSlack, a differentiable projection layer for nonlinear inequality-constrained neural prediction. DiffSlack reformulates inequalities as equalities with learnable slack variables, which are predicted as part of the augmented network output and provide a data-driven warm start for damped Gauss-Newton projection. The projection layer maps raw predictions onto the augmented feasible manifold while preserving end-to-end differentiability. A two-stage curriculum further stabilizes training and improves constraint satisfaction. We evaluate DiffSlack on vehicle path planning with 200 nonlinear inequality constraints from collision avoidance, curvature limits, and waypoint spacing.
