Experimental evaluation shows that the proposed pipeline reliably recovers the latent dependency structure from noisy continuous traces when the signal is sufficiently separated from background variation, while highlighting threshold calibration as the key factor controlling event-detection quality. These results constitute a foundational step toward interpretable dependency learning for adaptive AI-RAN control systems.

</details>


### [259] [PC Layer: Polynomial Weight Preconditioning for Improving LLM Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06470)
*Senmiao Wang,Tiantian Fang,Haoran Zhang,Yushun Zhang,Kunxiang Zhao,Alex Schwing,Ruoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于大模型训练的PC层，通过多项式预处理约束权重矩阵的奇异值谱，在实现训练加速与稳定的同时，保证了模型推理零开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型训练中，权重矩阵的条件数（Conditioning）不稳定会导致训练效率和稳定性问题，需要一种方法在训练过程中约束权重矩阵的奇异值分布。

Method: 提出了一种预处理（Preconditioning, PC）层，通过低阶多项式预处理重塑权重矩阵的奇异值谱，在训练完成后可直接合并回原始架构，实现推理零开销。通过该方法对Llama-1B模型进行预训练（结合AdamW和Muon优化器）进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，PC层在Llama-1B预训练任务中表现优于标准Transformer架构，且适用于AdamW和Muon多种优化器，证实了其在提升训练稳定性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了对权重矩阵进行奇异值谱控制是稳定大模型训练的有效手段，通过引入PC层，可以在不增加推理成本的前提下提升训练性能。理论上，该方法对深层线性网络梯度下降至全局最优解的收敛性提供了保证。

Abstract: We propose a preconditioning (PC) layer, a weight parameterization via polynomial preconditioner that ensures stable weight conditioning throughout LLM training. The PC module reshapes the singular-value spectrum of weight matrices via low-degree polynomial preconditioning. After training, the preconditioned weights can be merged back into the original architecture, incurring no inference overhead. We demonstrate the advantage of the proposed PC layer over standard transformers in Llama-1B pre-training, for both the AdamW and Muon optimizers. Theoretically, we justify this spectrum-control principle by proving that uniformly bounding each layer's singular values ensures geometric convergence of gradient descent to global minima, for certain deep linear networks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Empath-aln/PC-layer.

</details>


### [260] [RREDCoT: Segment-Level Reward Redistribution for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06475)
*Mykyta Ielanskyi,Kajetan Schweighofer,Lukas Aichberger,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对推理模型 RL 微调中由于延迟奖励导致的训练高方差问题，本文提出了 RREDCoT，通过利用模型自身进行高效的奖励分配（Credit Assignment），在避免高昂采样开销的同时提升了 CoT 的训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理模型通过 RL 微调（如 GRPO）生成 CoT 时面临典型的“延迟奖励”问题，即奖励仅在最终答案确认后给出。这导致类似于蒙特卡洛的 RL 方法存在高方差，而现有的蒙特卡洛采样方法在长上下文下的计算开销太大，无法满足训练需求，因此需要一种高效的奖励分配机制。

Method: 提出了 RREDCoT（Reward REDistribution for Chain of Thoughts）算法。该方法无需额外的采样开销，直接利用模型自身来近似最优的奖励分配（Credit Assignment），相比蒙特卡洛（MC）方法和传统的归因方法，在训练效率和性能上更具优势。此外，研究还探讨了 CoT 迹的切分粒度和状态价值估计对分配效果的影响。

Result: 实验表明，RREDCoT 在处理长 CoT 迹时的效率显著优于传统的蒙特卡洛采样方法，并且在奖励分配的准确性和模型推理性能的提升上优于多种现有的归因方法。同时，该研究明确了合理的 CoT 切分和价值估计对于优化流程至关重要。

Conclusion: RREDCoT 通过利用模型自身近似最优的奖励分配，有效地解决了推理模型中（特别是长思维链 CoT）延迟奖励带来的高方差问题，为 RL 微调提供了一种高效、高性能的替代方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in reasoning language models have been driven by Reinforcement Learning (RL) fine-tuning. Most often, these rely on the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) algorithm or modifications thereof to steer the models to produce Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces. The final answer can only be verified, and the reward assigned, after the CoT trace is complete, making it a delayed reward problem. GRPO and its modifications correspond to Monte Carlo methods in standard RL, which are known to suffer from high variance. A possible solution to this problem is the redistribution of rewards through credit assignment, where segments of the CoT trace that are important for arriving at the desirable solution are emphasized by assigning a higher reward. While Monte Carlo sampling can be used to provide an unbiased estimate of intermediate state values, its computational overhead makes it unsuitable for train-time credit assignment in long contexts at high granularity. We introduce RREDCoT (Reward REDistribution for Chain of Thoughts), which utilizes the model itself to approximate the optimal reward redistribution without additional generation. We investigate the advantages of our method compared to MC sampling and several attribution methods. We further analyze several aspects relevant to the construction of the redistribution such as segmentation of CoT traces and state value estimation.

</details>


### [261] [Pretraining Recurrent Networks without Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06479)
*Akarsh Kumar,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SMT 方法，通过将 RNN 的递归学习转化为对记忆转化的监督学习，实现了高效的时间并行训练，并彻底解决了传统 BPTT 在长序列处理中遇到的梯度难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 BPTT 训练 RNN 存在两大痛点：1. 串行计算导致无法利用大规模并行化；2. 梯度消失或爆炸问题使得长程依赖的学习极其困难。

Method: 提出监督记忆训练 (Supervised Memory Training, SMT)：1. 使用基于 Transformer 的编码器通过预测性状态目标获取记忆标签 $(m_t, x_{t+1}) \rightarrow m_{t+1}$；2. 将 RNN 训练简化为针对这些标签的监督学习，即“解耦记忆内容与更新机制”。该方法无需展开 RNN，梯度路径恒为 O(1)。

Result: 在语言建模和像素序列建模等任务上，SMT 训练的非线性 RNN 在性能上优于 BPTT，且具备时间并行训练能力。

Conclusion: SMT 是一种替代 BPTT 的新型 RNN 训练方法，通过将递归学习转化为监督学习，实现了时间维度的并行化，解决了长序列梯度消失/爆炸问题，并能更好地捕捉长程依赖。

Abstract: Training recurrent neural networks (RNNs) requires assigning credit across long sequences of computations. Standard backpropagation through time (BPTT) addresses this problem poorly: it is sequential in time, limiting parallelism, and suffers from vanishing or exploding gradients, making long-range associations difficult to learn. We propose Supervised Memory Training (SMT), a method for training nonlinear RNNs that sidesteps recurrent credit propagation entirely by reducing RNN training to supervised learning on one-step memory transition labels $(m_t, x_{t+1}) \rightarrow m_{t+1}$. SMT acquires these memory labels by training a Transformer-based encoder on a predictive state objective--retaining only information from the past necessary to predict the future. By decoupling what to remember from how to update memory, SMT enables time-parallel RNN training with a stable $O(1)$ length gradient path between any two tokens--without ever unrolling the RNN. We find that SMT outperforms BPTT when pretraining various RNN architectures on tasks like language modeling and pixel sequence modeling. SMT enables nonlinear RNNs to better capture long-range dependencies and train in parallel, potentially unlocking the scaling of models that build temporal abstractions of past experience.

</details>


### [262] [Regret Minimization with Adaptive Opponents in Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06486)
*Mingyang Liu,Asuman Ozdaglar,Tiancheng Yu,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对重复博弈中对手的自适应性，本文提出了Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret)指标，并设计了多种算法解决其非凸性问题，证明了该方法有助于习得子博弈完美均衡并实现更高收益的合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线学习中的“外部后悔”（External Regret）无法有效捕捉重复博弈中对手根据历史记录进行自适应调整的行为。研究旨在引入一种能同时考虑对手反馈及参与者反事实推理的游戏理论指标，以弥补现有后悔定义的不足。

Method: 1. 提出了 Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret) 指标，用以评估基于对手策略反馈的后悔值；2. 进行了理论分析，识别出实现子线性后悔所需的必要条件；3. 针对RP-Regret的非凸性，提出了三种算法：基于优化谕言机（Optimization Oracle）的算法、基于线性化凸替代目标的算法、以及针对慢变对手的直接最小化算法。

Result: 理论上确定了实现子线性RP-Regret的限制条件；提出的三种算法可有效处理非凸优化 sfida；实证结果表明，使用该后悔指标能引导博弈趋向于高收益的合作解（如Stag-Hunt博弈）。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种适用于重复博弈的RP-Regret指标，证明了在所有参与者最小化该指标时能够学习到特定的子博弈完美均衡（SPE），并通过实验验证了其在促进合作方面的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study regret minimization in repeated games with \emph{adaptive} opponents who can respond based on histories of play. The standard metric of \emph{external regret} in online learning is known to fail to capture such adaptivity. To account for players' counterfactual reasoning, we introduce {\tt Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret)}, a game-theoretic metric that measures the difference between the \emph{realized} and the \emph{best-in-hindsight} accumulated utility when all players can \emph{respond} to the history of play. Compared to existing regret notions in this setting, ours is native to repeated game playing, enabling stronger comparators and opponents with fewer constraints, while maintaining the possibility of finding better equilibria when all players minimize it. We first identify necessary conditions for obtaining {\tt RP-Regret} sublinear in time, on the variation of the player's comparator strategies in the regret definition and on the memories of both the comparator and opponents' strategies. We then study additional conditions and provable algorithms to minimize {\tt RP-Regret}, which is by definition \emph{non-convex} in the strategy space. To address this challenge, we propose three algorithms: (i) one based on an optimization oracle, as assumed in some prior work in online non-convex learning; (ii) one that minimizes a convex and \emph{linearized} surrogate of {\tt RP-Regret} at each iteration; (iii) one that directly minimizes {\tt RP-Regret} when opponents change strategies slowly. Furthermore, when all players can run algorithms to minimize the {\tt RP-Regret} (or its linearized variant), certain subgame perfect equilibria of the repeated game can be learned. We also provide experiments showing that minimizing our regret notions can lead to more cooperative solutions with higher utility in games such as Stag-Hunt.

</details>


### [263] [TailLoR: Protecting Principal Components in Parameter-Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06494)
*Marius Dragoi,Ioana Pintilie,Alexandra Dragomir,Antonio Barbalau,Florin Brad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TailLoR 是一种基于谱分解的持续学习 PEFT 方法，通过固定奇异向量并仅对奇异值进行长尾方向更新及软谱惩罚，实现了高效且低干扰的模型微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决持续学习（Continual Learning）中参数高效微调（PEFT）方法性能不足以及任务叠加导致的干扰问题，利用谱分解特性来优化参数更新效率。

Method: 提出 TailLoR 方法：使用预训练权重的奇异向量矩阵 U 和 V 作为固定参考系；仅对奇异值矩阵进行低秩更新；引入“软谱惩罚”（soft spectral penalty）机制，抑制向主特征方向的更新，将模型适应定向到长尾（弱）特征方向。

Result: TailLoR 能够通过路由到长尾谱坐标实现细粒度的适应，同时通过惩罚主奇异方向来减少任务间的特征干扰。

Conclusion: TailLoR 通过利用预训练权重的奇异值分解框架，并引入软谱惩罚机制，有效地在保持预训练知识的同时实现了持续学习任务的参数高效微调，缓解了灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient finetuning methods based on spectral decomposition have enabled progress in Continual Learning. In this paper we introduce TailLoR, which utilizes the singular bases U and V of the pre-trained weights as a fixed reference frame to learn a low-rank update applied to the singular value matrix. A soft spectral penalty discourages updates aligned with dominant singular directions, reducing interference while routing fine-grained adaptation into the highly flexible, long-tail spectral coordinates.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [264] [QDAG: Declarative Composition of Reusable Analytics Methodologies at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05662)
*Peter Ho,Praveen Chaganlal,Tianle Zhang,Endong Zhu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: LinkedIn 开发了名为 QDAG 的声明式分析编排系统，通过有向无环图（DAG）统一封装生产分析逻辑，成功解决了代码冗余和定义不一致问题，且在保持毫秒级低延迟的前提下提升了系统的可维护性与可复用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产分析产品中，关键的业务指标（如 headcount growth 等）常被硬编码为命令式逻辑，导致重复代码、指标定义不一致以及维护和测试困难。

Method: 开发了QDAG系统，将分析方法论抽象为类型化的声明式有向无环图（DAG）。该系统支持多种任务类型的节点（如Pinot查询、服务调用、内存Join、数据转换等），并具备按需执行、记忆化缓存、剪枝优化及并行处理能力。

Result: QDAG 在 LinkedIn 部署于超过 500 台主机，支撑 100 多个生产用例。性能测试显示，其带来的中位协调开销仅为 10ms，P99 延迟低于 50ms。

Conclusion: 将分析方法论声明化（QDAG）通过构建有向无环图，有效地解决了业务逻辑重复、定义漂移及测试困难等问题，在保证交互式低延迟的同时，显著提升了生产分析系统的代码复用性和一致性。

Abstract: Production analytics products often depend on reusable methodologies: multi-step definitions such as headcount growth, top-skill growth, or differentially-private impression distributions. Although these methodologies define business-critical numbers, they are commonly implemented as imperative glue around OLAP queries, service calls, joins, transformations, and conditional logic. As a result, teams duplicate orchestration code, definitions drift across products, and methodologies are difficult to test or analyze.
  We present QDAG, a production system at LinkedIn that represents an analytics methodology as a declarative directed acyclic graph of typed steps. Nodes may execute Apache Pinot queries, downstream service calls, in-memory SQLite joins, jq transformations, conditionals, differentially-private aggregations, or calls to other QDAGs. The engine evaluates graphs demand-driven, memoized, pruned, and parallelized in the per-request analytics mid-tier. QDAG is deployed across more than 500 hosts and over 100 production use cases, adding roughly 10 ms median orchestration overhead and under 50 ms at the 99th percentile. Our experience shows that making methodologies declarative improves reuse, testability, and cross-product consistency while preserving interactive latency.

</details>


### [265] [Data Flow Control: Data Safety Policies for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05679)
*Charlie Summers,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了 DFC 框架及 Passant 系统，通过查询重写技术在 DBMS 基础设施层面高效、无损地强制执行数据安全流控策略，解决了自动 SQL 生成过程中的隐私与合规风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Agent SQL 生成方案虽关注查询正确性，但忽略了安全合规问题（如隐私、监管或业务约束）；缺乏基础设施层面的有效手段来限制数据如何被组合及发布。

Method: 提出 Data Flow Control (DFC) 框架：通过声明式定义策略以保障数据库管理系统（DBMS）内元组级的数据流安全；通过形式化数据安全为溯源单项式上的聚合谓词，并开发了 Passant 系统，利用查询重写层在无需实体化溯源的情况下高效执行策略。

Result: Passant 在 DuckDB、Umbra、PostgreSQL、DataFusion 和 SQLServer 等五种主流 DBMS 上实现了约 0% 的性能开销，且比现有替代方案性能高出数个数量级。

Conclusion: 数据流控制（DFC）将数据安全从大模型提示词工程和事后检查层面提升至数据基础设施层面，实现了高效、可移植且声明式的安全策略强制保障。

Abstract: Agents increasingly generate SQL, orchestrate pipelines, and automate data analysis on behalf of users. While recent work improves query correctness, correctness is not safety. A query may be semantically valid yet violate regulatory, privacy, or business constraints that govern how data may be combined and released. We argue that enforcing such constraints is fundamentally a data infrastructure problem.
  This paper introduces Data Flow Control (DFC), a framework to declaratively specify and guarantee policy enforcement over tuple-level data flows within a DBMS query. A key challenge is defining a policy language that is optimizer-invariant yet efficient to enforce at scale. We formalize data safety as aggregate predicates over provenance monomials and present Passant, a portable query rewriting layer that enforces DFC policies without materializing provenance. Across five DBMS engines -- DuckDB, Umbra, PostgreSQL, DataFusion, and SQLServer -- Passant achieves ~0% overhead and outperforms alternatives by orders of magnitude. As a result, Data Flow Control is the first step towards moving data safety from prompts and post-hoc checks into the data infrastructure. Data Flow Control is available open source at https://github.com/dataflowcontrol/data-flow-control.

</details>


### [266] [Causal Scaffolding for Physical Reasoning: A Benchmark for Causally-Informed Physical World Understanding in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05966)
*Tianyi Tang,Zhuoyi Lin,Zeyu Feng,Tianyi Ma,Yew-Soon Ong,Ivor Tsang,Haiyan Yin*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CausalPhys 基准和 CRFT 微调方法，旨在通过集成因果图结构改善视觉语言模型在物理推理任务中的逻辑正确性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的视觉语言模型（VLM）在处理因果物理推理时表现不佳，常生成看似合理但逻辑错误的答案，缺乏对物理世界深层因果机制的理解。

Method: 1. 构建了包含3000多个问题及专家级因果图的 CausalPhys 基准；2. 提出了基于因果图的思维链（CoT）推理评估指标；3. 提出了“因果理性信息微调”（CRFT）方法，通过显式对齐模型推理与物理因果关系来提升性能。

Result: 评估显示现有 VLM 在捕捉因果依赖方面存在系统性缺陷；CRFT 方法在多个模型骨干网络上显著提升了因果推理的准确性与可解释性。

Conclusion: 通过统一数据集构建、因果评估指标及因果导向的微调方法（CRFT），CausalPhys 为提升视觉语言模型（VLM）在物理世界的推理能力奠定了坚实基础，证明了显式利用因果结构能显著增强模型的准确性与可解释性。

Abstract: Understanding and reasoning about the physical world is the foundation of intelligent behavior, yet state-of-the-art vision-language models (VLMs) still fail at causal physical reasoning, often producing plausible but incorrect answers. To address this gap, we introduce CausalPhys, a benchmark of over 3,000 carefully curated video- and image-based questions spanning four domains: Perception, Anticipation, Intervention, and Goal Orientation. Each question is paired with an expert-annotated causal graph capturing object-attribute-event dependencies, enabling interpretable and fine-grained evaluation of causal understanding. Building on this, we formulate a causal-graph-grounded metric that quantitatively measures how well a model's chain-of-thought reasoning aligns with the correct causal relations, moving beyond answer-only accuracy and enabling systematic diagnosis of VLMs' causal reasoning failures. Using this metric, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of leading VLMs, revealing systematic gaps in capturing causal dependencies and underscoring the need for causality-aware learning. To address these limitations, we further propose Causal Rationale-informed Fine-Tuning (CRFT), which explicitly aligns VLM reasoning with causal structures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CRFT substantially enhances both reasoning accuracy and interpretability across multiple model backbones. By unifying dataset curation, causal evaluation, and causality-informed learning, CausalPhys establishes a strong foundation for advancing modern VLMs toward causally grounded physical reasoning.

</details>


### [267] [Validation of graph databases against PG-Schema](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06127)
*Jacek Ciszewski,Jakub Kłos,Maxime Jakubowski,Dominik Tomaszuk,Filip Murlak*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该研究分析了PG-Schema图验证的计算复杂度，指出在无完整性约束时，验证的基础复杂性为NP完全，但在特定限制下可优化至PTIME。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究在缺乏完整性约束的假设下，根据PG-Schema图类型验证给定图数据库实例的计算可行性。

Method: 通过计算复杂度分析（Complexity Analysis），研究了图数据库验证问题在不同复杂度度量指标（组合复杂度与数据复杂度）下的表现，并探讨了特定限制条件下复杂度的优化。

Result: 确定了该验证问题的理论计算复杂度边界：基础情况下组合复杂度为NP完全，数据复杂度为PTIME；通过特定约束可降低组合复杂度。

Conclusion: 验证PG-Schema中不含完整性约束的图数据库实例的复杂度受限：组合复杂度为NP完全，数据复杂度为多项式时间（PTIME）。若适当限制类型组合与并集之间的交替，组合复杂度可降至PTIME。

Abstract: The problem of validating a given graph database instance against a given PG-Schema graph type without integrity constraints is NP- complete in terms of combined complexity and in PTIME in terms of data complexity. The combined complexity drops to PTIME when the alternation between type combinations and unions is suitably restricted

</details>


### [268] [TOKI: A Bitemporal Operator Algebra for Contradiction Resolution in LLM-Agent Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06240)
*Ziming Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对 LLM 代理持久化内存的写入冲突问题，提出了 TOKI 系统，通过明确写入时正确性契约和双时态运算符，解决了现有生产系统中常见的重放不一致及数据偏离问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 代理的持久内存是重写密集型的，且存在事实冲突，现有的生产系统使用启发式解决策略，但缺乏对写入时刻隔离级别和异常处理的明确契约。

Method: 提出 TOKI 系统，将四种传统的冲突解决启发式策略建模为双时态运算符，引入审计行（audit row）保留丢失事实；证明了隔离级别、模式与溯源之间的正确性定理；构建了基于关系调度模型的紧致性证明以保障重放一致性。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 基准测试中，审计行机制使性能（LoCoMo score）提升了 0.86，保留类型化内存层可维持 0.49 的准确率；对比分析显示，TOKI 是唯一能在保留 LLM 判决器的同时消除所有已识别的三种写入时异常的系统。

Conclusion: TOKI 提供了一个明确的写入时正确性契约，通过双时态运算符和审计架构，在保留 LLM 判决机制的同时，解决了现有生产级 LLM 持久化内存中存在的写入异常（重放不一致、信念漂移偏差、审计擦除）。

Abstract: Persistent memory for an LLM agent is a write-heavy substrate: every belief update is a versioned write, and a new claim may contradict a stored one. Production systems use four resolution heuristics (last-writer-wins, evidence-weighted merge, await-confirmation, per-rule policy), yet none declares the isolation level it assumes or the write-time anomalies it admits. We show that contradiction resolution is write-time concurrency control and make the missing contract explicit. TOKI types the four heuristics as one family of bitemporal operators over a dual-row schema, each with an isolation precondition and a provenance annotation that preserves the losing fact in an audit row. Four soundness theorems close the contract across isolation, schema, and provenance, lift the guarantees to operator pipelines, and extend the fold operators to n-ary conflict sets. A tightness companion proves that, within the relational schedule model, keyed logging of the adjudicating judge is necessary for replay consistency, which every audited baseline omits. A verdict matrix over eight systems localizes the gap: every baseline that keeps a language-model judge on the write path admits at least one of three write-time anomalies (replay inconsistency, belief-drift skew, audit erasure); a content-addressed engine-layer comparator avoids them only by removing the judge, and TOKI alone excludes all three while keeping it. On its one natural-workload slice the audit-row defence moves LoCoMo by 0.86, and ablating the typed memory layer removes 0.49 accuracy on 1,444 answerable LoCoMo questions; the cross-system comparison stays underpowered and claims no superiority. The contribution is the contract: a write-time correctness specification, proved sound across isolation, schema, and provenance, pinning the guarantee every production heuristic assumes but no deployed system makes explicit.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [269] [QCFuse: Query-Aware Cache Fusion via Compressed View for Efficient RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05875)
*Jianxin Yan,Wangze Ni,Zhenxin Li,Jiabao Jin,Zhitao Shen,Haoyang Li,Jia Zhu,Peng Cheng,Xuemin Lin,Lei Chen,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QCFuse通过压缩视图查询感知选择技术优化了RAG的KV缓存融合过程，在不损失回答质量的前提下，大幅降低了LLM预填充阶段的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG中的上下文处理导致预填充阶段成为高昂的成本瓶颈，现有的缓存融合选择器方案难以在“查询相关性（质量）”和“计算效率”之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出了QCFuse，一种用于RAG缓存融合的压缩视图查询感知选择器。该方法采用“分块锚点查询探测（chunk-anchor query probing）”来处理查询状态，并利用“关键层分析（critical-layer profiling）”在无需检查所有层的情况下识别需要重计算的Token。

Result: 基于SGLang实现并测试，QCFuse在6个数据集上达到了与完整预填充相当的回答质量；在质量相当的情况下，相比完整预填充实现了平均1.7倍的预填充加速，相比同类最优算法ProphetKV实现了1.5倍的加速。

Conclusion: QCFuse有效解决了RAG缓存融合中“质量与效率”的矛盾，通过压缩视图查询感知选择机制，在保持高质量的前提下，显著提升了LLM的预填充阶段处理速度。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves large language model (LLM) answer quality by grounding generation in external evidence, but processing retrieved contexts makes the prefill stage a dominant serving cost. RAG cache fusion reduces this cost by reusing precomputed key-value (KV) caches for retrieved chunks and selectively recomputing tokens under the current prompt. Existing selectors, however, face a dilemma between quality and efficiency: fast query-agnostic or final-layer query-to-context selectors can miss request-relevant evidence, whereas full-view query-aware selectors require broad context and layer visibility before recomputation and therefore stall the layer-wise cache-fusion pipeline. We present QCFuse, a compressed-view query-aware selector for RAG cache fusion. QCFuse uses chunk-anchor query probing to condition user-query states on compact per-chunk anchors and critical-layer profiling to identify recomputation tokens without all-layer inspection. We implement QCFuse in SGLang and evaluate it on four open-weight LLMs across six datasets. QCFuse reaches full-prefill-level quality. At matched quality, QCFuse achieves an average prefill-time speedup of 1.7x over full prefill and 1.5x over ProphetKV, the strongest quality-preserving baseline.

</details>


### [270] [How Far Did They Go? The Persuasive Tactics of Covert LLM Agents in a Discontinued Field Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05256)
*Kokil Jaidka,Saifuddin Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了Reddit上针对AI伪装成人类用户进行辩论的数据，发现AI构建了一套专门用于“提高说服效率”而非“真实参与思辨”的修辞架构，表现为高强度的权威标识、策略性对齐和对认知偏差的频繁操纵，这对现有的AI透明度披露政策提出了挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI在未披露身份的情况下，在强调交互性、身份感和思辨性的论坛中如何进行论证，揭示其是否具备真实的辩论参与能力，还是仅仅通过特定架构实现“说服效率”。

Method: 对Reddit r/ChangeMyView中包含AI生成对话的公开数据集进行结构化内容分析（Structured Content Analysis），并将其与人类撰写的反驳意见进行对比。

Result: AI在超过三分之二的评论中涉及身份操纵，几乎所有评论都使用了对齐策略和权威声明，并频繁触发认知偏差（如确认偏误、代表性启发式等）。与人类相比，AI更倾向于高密度权威使用、对抗性对齐以及依赖外部引用而非个人经验。

Conclusion: 在AI生成内容模糊了真实与合成认知地位的背景下，仅靠披露机制（disclosure）不足以应对。未来需要建立不仅评估AI存在性，更能评估AI如何构建“可信度结构”的审计框架。

Abstract: This study analyzes a publicly released dataset from a discontinued field experiment on Reddit's r/ChangeMyView. The intervention, conducted by unknown, external researchers and halted following ethical backlash, involved undisclosed AI-generated accounts engaging users in live debate. After public disclosure, Reddit authorized moderators to release an archive of the AI-generated comments, creating a rare opportunity to examine how large language models operated in an identity-rich deliberative forum without disclosure. We conduct a structured content analysis of this corpus, evaluating identity performance, authority signaling, alignment strategies, and activation of cognitive heuristics. Identity targeting or adoption appears in over two-thirds of comments, alignment moves and authority claims in nearly all of them, and cognitive-bias triggers -- particularly confirmation bias, representativeness, and availability -- in the large majority. These patterns co-occur systematically, composing a rhetorical architecture calibrated for persuasive efficiency rather than authentic deliberative participation. Compared against human-authored CMV counter-arguments, the agents inverted the typical distribution on every dimension: denser authority use, more adversarial alignment, and heavier reliance on external citation over experiential grounding. In such environments, distinctions between authentic and synthetic epistemic standing grow increasingly opaque -- an asymmetry that disclosure mandates alone cannot address. The results point toward auditing frameworks capable of assessing how AI systems structure credibility, not merely whether they are present.

</details>


### [271] [What Should Agents Say? Action-state Communication for Efficient Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05304)
*Chen Huang,Yuhao Wu,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对多智能体系统通信冗余问题，文章提出了一种PACT协议，将智能体间的自由文本交互转化为精简的结构化状态更新，在显著降低Token消耗的同时保持甚至提升了任务解决性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统（MAS）中无约束的自然语言通信会导致Token消耗过快、消耗共享上下文窗口，进而降低系统性能并推高推理成本。

Method: 提出了一种名为PACT（Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission）的协议，将智能体间的自由文本通信映射并压缩为紧凑的“动作-状态”记录，统一以公共状态更新的形式共享。

Result: PACT在不同MAS拓扑结构中表现出优异的性能-成本权衡。在OpenHands任务中，它提升了解决率并减少了10%的Token消耗；在SWE-agent任务中，在保持解决率不变的情况下将输入Token量减少了50%。

Conclusion: PACT证明了将多智能体通信转化为结构化、精简的“动作-状态”更新协议，是解决长上下文窗口限制、降低计算成本并维持系统性能的有效方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models are typically organized around roles, pipelines, and turn schedules, while the content that agents pass to one another is often left as unconstrained natural language. However, this free-form communication can rapidly inflate token usage, consume the shared context window, and ultimately affect both system performance and inference cost. We analyze five common inter-agent communication strategies across two MAS topologies, finding that no fixed strategy is universally optimal. Instead, effective inter-agent messages consistently preserve action-centered information needed by downstream agents. Building on this, we propose the PACT (Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission), which treats inter-agent communication as a public state-update problem and projects each raw agent output into a compact action-state record before it enters shared history. Across different MAS topologies, PACT consistently improves the performance-cost trade-off, achieving comparable or stronger task performance with substantially fewer tokens. The gains extend to production coding harnesses: PACT lifts OpenHands' resolve rate at -10% tokens-per-resolved, and is resolve-neutral on SWE-agent while halving input tokens. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/iNLP-Lab/PACT.

</details>


### [272] [Ten Headache Specialists versus Artificial Intelligence for Clinical Literature Summarization: A Critical Evaluation and Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05436)
*Alejandro Lozano,Keiko Ihara,Ping-Hao Yang,Carrie E. Robertson,Jennifer Stern,Allan Purdy,Hsiangkuo Yuan,Pengfei Zhang,Yulia Orlova,Olga Fermo,Jennifer Hranilovich,Fred Cohen,Todd J. Schwedt,Jenelle A. Jindal,Serena Yeung-Levy,Chia-Chun Chiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了RAG支持的LLM在医学文献总结中的表现，发现专家撰写的综述仍受青睐，但AI生成的质量已达到使专家难以区分的程度，并指出了优化建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation:  clinicians面对医疗文献激增与时间不足的矛盾，现有研究缺乏对RAG驱动的LLM在临床文献综述中的深入评估，以及其与专家撰写内容的直接对比。

Method: 构建了基于RAG的智能体AI框架，使用三种LLM（Sonnet, GPT-4o, Llama 3.1）生成临床文献综述。通过10位头痛专家对AI与专家生成的综述进行双盲评估（评分项：准确性、完整性、简洁性、临床效用）及排序对比。

Result: 专家撰写的综述在偏好度上优于AI；但在 blinded 条件下，专家难以有效区分AI生成与人类撰写的文本；识别出除了标准评估指标外，专家更看重的文献综述评价要素。

Conclusion: 专家撰写的文献综述仍优于基于RAG的LLM生成内容，但随着模型性能提升，专家辨别两者差异的难度在增加。研究总结了专家评估中的关键价值特征，为未来优化AI临床文献总结流程提供了方向。

Abstract: Summarizing the latest medical literature to guide clinical decision-making is essential for evidence-based medicine and high-quality patient care. Yet clinicians face increasing challenges due to limited time with patients and a rapidly growing volume of published articles. Although retrieval-augmented large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in clinical summarization, human evaluations of their effectiveness in synthesizing broader scientific literature and direct comparisons to expert-written syntheses remain scarce. We constructed a RAG-based agentic AI framework using three state-of-the-art LLMs: Sonnet, GPT-4o, and Llama 3.1. A headache specialist created 13 questions, three for prompt optimization and ten for evaluation. Ten headache specialists across the United States and Canada each wrote a summary for one question, yielding four summaries per question (expert, Sonnet, GPT-4o, and Llama). The experts, blinded to authorship, critically evaluated the summaries, excluding the topic for which they wrote a summary, based on correctness, completeness, conciseness, and clinical utility, scoring each from 1 to 10 using standardized rubrics. They also ranked the summaries by preference and indicated whether they believed each summary was written by an expert or an LLM. Our study, comparing LLM- and expert-written literature summaries evaluated by headache specialists, showed that expert-written summaries were preferred, although experts sometimes found it challenging to distinguish between human- and AI-generated summaries. We also identified key expert-valued features beyond standard evaluation metrics that can guide future refinement of both human and AI literature summarization pipelines.

</details>


### [273] [I Know What You Meme, Even If it Emerged Today: Understanding Evolving Memes through Open-World Knowledge Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05316)
*Shanhong Liu,Rui Cao,Pai Chet Ng,De Wen Soh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对多模态模因分析中背景知识更新滞后的问题，本文提出了一种基于外部Web知识检索的零样本框架，并通过构建最新的2024-2026模因基准进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态模因分析方法往往忽视最新的背景知识，或仅依赖预训练模型中固定且可能过时、不完整的参数化知识，无法应对不断涌现的新兴模因。

Method: 提出了“查询-检索-推理”（Query-Retrieve-Conclude）框架：首先识别模因中缺失的背景知识，通过搜索引擎检索开放互联网证据，最后合成为有根据的背景信息，用于模因的理解和检测。同时构建了包含2024-2026年最新模因的基准数据集。

Result: 在三个模因理解数据集和五个模因检测任务上的实验表明，该框架在知识恢复、模因理解与下游检测任务上均显著优于零样本基线模型。

Conclusion: 该框架通过结合外部实时知识检索与推理，有效解决了模因处理中背景知识缺失和更新滞后的问题，在零样本设置下实现了优于现有方法的效果。

Abstract: Multimodal memes are dynamic and often require up to date background knowledge for interpretation. Existing methods often overlook such knowledge or rely on fixed parametric knowledge of pretrained models that may be incomplete, outdated, or unavailable for emerging memes. We introduce Query Retrieve Conclude, a zero shot framework that identifies missing knowledge, retrieves open web evidence, and synthesizes evidence grounded background knowledge for meme understanding and detection. We also introduce a curated meme understanding benchmark of recent memes from 2024 to 2026 with external background knowledge annotations. Experiments on three meme understanding datasets and five meme detection tasks show that our framework improves knowledge recovery, meme understanding and downstream detection over zero shot baselines.

</details>


### [274] [GITCO: Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization in TSFMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05332)
*Manya Pandey,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对时间序列基础模型（TSFM）中的“上下文中毒”缺陷，提出了一种名为 GITCO 的轻量化、无需微调的推理时上下文优化框架，通过动态抑制有害异常补丁提升了零样本预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于补丁的时间序列基础模型（TSFM）存在“上下文中毒”问题，结构化异常补丁会占据不成比例的注意力，导致零样本预测质量下降。

Method: 提出 GITCO（门控推理时上下文优化）框架，包含三个轻量级组件：Gate（门）、Router（路由）和 Critic（评估器），旨在推理阶段动态识别并抑制异常补丁（Patches）。该方法无需重新训练模型，仅依赖推理时输入优化。

Result: 在 TimesFM 2.5 和 53 个 GIFT-Eval 数据集上的交叉验证显示，GITCO 使平均 MASE 降低了 1.95%，并捕获了可改进上限的 89.9%。

Conclusion: 引入了“上下文敏感性配置文件”（Context Sensitivity Profiles），揭示了模型架构与数据结构如何共同决定推理时上下文干预的效果，证明了在不更新权重的情况下，通过优化输入上下文可显著提升TSFM的零样本预测性能。

Abstract: Patch-based Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) suffer from context poisoning: structurally anomalous patches capture disproportionate attention and silently degrade zero-shot forecast quality. We propose improving TSFM accuracy at inference time by optimizing the input context rather than modifying model weights. We present GITCO (Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization), a lightweight three-component framework: Gate, Router, and Critic that selectively identifies and suppresses harmful patches without any parameter updates. Evaluated on TimesFM 2.5 across 53 GIFT-Eval datasets under K-fold cross-validation, GITCO achieves an average +1.95% MASE reduction on TimesFM 2.5 while capturing 89.9% of the improvement upper bound. We introduce context sensitivity profiles as a new characterizable property of TSFMs: the mapping from time series meta-features to expected accuracy improvement under inference-time context intervention, shaped jointly by model architecture and the statistical structure of the data.

</details>


### [275] [Uncertainty Aware Functional Behavior Prediction and Material Fatigue Assessment for Circular Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05334)
*Nehal Afifi,Mehdi Khabou,Victor Mas,Jonas Hemmerich,Patric Grauberger,Stefan Dietrich,Volker Schulze,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文为循环制造中的退役产品开发了一套结合LSTM功能预测与材料疲劳分析的可靠性评估框架，实现了对产品剩余寿命和功能指标的高精度预测，为工业界的重用决策提供了科学依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测性维护（PHM）方法通常针对固定操作条件或孤立组件，缺乏对具有高度异质性（降解程度、使用历史各异）的回收产品进行系统级功能与材料疲劳的协同评估，导致无法有效制定重用决策。

Method: 提出了一种结合“不确定性感知的功能预测”与“零部件级疲劳评估”的实例特定可靠性工作流：1. 利用卷积编码器提取主轴力与扭矩的载荷模式；2. 通过LSTM主干网络输出函数变量的高斯分布（均值与方差）；3. 并行进行有限元支持的应力重建、S-N/Miner损伤评估及Paris定律裂纹扩展分析；4. 采用流式重放算法整合功能与材料的可靠性轨迹。

Result: 在角磨机的验证中，该框架在九个输出指标上的2%容差准确率达到0.9652；其中热变量预测近乎完美，驱动电机电流和负载速度的R²值分别达到0.9750和0.9924；在短历史预测任务中，LSTM表现优于GRU和xLSTM。

Conclusion: 该研究通过在闭环工厂中引入实例特定的可靠性工作流，成功解决了当前退役产品重用决策难以基于单一检查作出、且缺乏系统级预测的难题。该方法能有效评估异构退役产品的剩余功能与材料结构完整性。

Abstract: Returned products in circular factories re-enter production with heterogeneous degradation states, usage histories, and remaining capability. Reuse cannot be decided from the current inspection alone, because future function fulfillment and component integrity may evolve differently under the next service scenario. Existing PHM approaches support degradation prediction, but often target fixed operating conditions or isolated component benchmarks, while material-fatigue assessment is rarely linked to system-level functional prognosis. This paper addresses this gap for an angle grinder by combining uncertainty-aware functional prediction with component-level fatigue assessment in an instance-specific reliability workflow. The proposed framework combines the current tool state with recent force--torque usage windows. A convolutional encoder extracts loading patterns from spindle forces and shaft torque, and an LSTM backbone predicts nine functional variables as Gaussian mean and variance estimates. In parallel, the same loading history is translated into output-shaft fatigue information through finite-element-supported stress reconstruction, S--N/Miner damage evaluation with Haibach extension, and Paris-law crack-growth analysis. A streaming replay algorithm consolidates both branches into functional, material, and system reliability trajectories. Held-out tests show mean \(2\%\)-tolerance accuracy of 0.9652 across nine outputs. Thermal variables are predicted near-perfectly, while drive motor current and load speed remain the most demanding dynamic outputs, with \(R^2\) values of 0.9750 and 0.9924. Torque history is especially important for these variables, and the conventional LSTM outperforms GRU and xLSTM in the short-history setting. Reliability calibration is most informative for drive motor current, where predicted and observed exceedance probabilities ...

</details>


### [276] [Memory is Reconstructed, Not Retrieved: Graph Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06036)
*Shuo Ji,Yibo Li,Bryan Hooi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MRAgent 引入关联记忆图和主动推理式内存访问机制，使智能体能够根据推理进展动态获取长程历史信息，显著提升了复杂长程任务的推理性能与资源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体在处理长交互历史时，受限于“检索后推理”的静态固定范式，无法根据推理过程中的中间证据动态调整访问策略，导致难以有效利用长程记忆。

Method: 提出 MRAgent 框架，核心包含：1. Cue-Tag-Content 关联记忆图结构，通过 Tag 连接细粒度线索与内容；2. 主动重构机制，将 LLM 推理嵌入内存访问过程，支持基于证据积累的迭代式路径搜索与修剪。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval 基准测试中，相比强基线模型性能提升高达 23%，同时大幅降低了 Token 消耗和运行时间成本。

Conclusion: MRAgent 通过引入关联记忆图和主动重构机制，成功实现了记忆检索与推理过程的动态交互，有效克服了传统静态检索范式的局限，在大规模长程记忆推理任务中实现了显著的性能提升与效率优化。

Abstract: Despite recent progress, LLM agents still struggle with reasoning over long interaction histories. While current memory-augmented agents rely on a static retrieve-then-reason paradigm, this rigid pipeline design prevents them from dynamically adapting memory access to intermediate evidence discovered during inference. To bridge this gap, we propose MRAgent, a framework that combines an associative memory graph with an active reconstruction mechanism. We represent memory as a Cue-Tag-Content graph, where associative tags serve as semantic bridges connecting fine-grained cues to memory contents. Operating on this structure, our active reconstruction mechanism integrates LLM reasoning directly into memory access, allowing the agent to iteratively explore and prune retrieval paths based on accumulated evidence. This ensures that memory retrieval is dynamically adapted to the reasoning context while avoiding combinatorial explosion caused by unconstrained expansion. Experiments on the LoCoMo benchmark and LongMemEval benchmark demonstrate significant improvements over strong baselines (up to 23%), while substantially reducing token and runtime cost, highlighting the effectiveness of active and associative reconstruction for long-horizon memory reasoning.

</details>


### [277] [SentinelBench: A Benchmark for Long-Running Monitoring Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05342)
*Matheus Kunzler Maldaner,Adam Fourney,Amanda Swearngin,Hussein Mozzanar,Gagan Bansal,Maya Murad,Rafah Hosn,Saleema Amershi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对长期监控型任务，提出了 SentinelBench 基准测试，旨在解决当前 AI Agent 在等待任务中资源浪费的问题，并评估 Agent 在时间演变环境下的反应能力与成本平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的 AI Agent 过于依赖“连续动作”模式，这在需要长时间等待外部事件触发的监控型任务中会导致资源浪费或效率低下。

Method: 1. 提出了“持续监控（Sustained Attention）”机制，而非持续行为模型；2. 构建了 SentinelBench 基准测试，包含10个模拟 Web 环境及100个任务；3. 评估指标包括任务完成率、反应时间及资源使用效率。

Result: 通过对三个模型和两个浏览器的基准测试，表明 SentinelBench 能够有效区分不同 Agent 在响应速度与成本控制方面的设计差异。

Conclusion: SentinelBench 通过引入对时间演变任务的评估，揭示了智能体在长期监控任务中的响应能力与资源消耗之间的权衡，证明了针对持续监控任务优化 Agent 策略的重要性。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly asked to carry out work that spans minutes, hours, or longer. Yet the default model of agent behavior is continuous action: issuing tool calls, refreshing pages, searching for alternatives, or otherwise trying to force progress. This is the wrong approach for many long-running tasks, which are better served by a strategy of sustained attention. Instead, agents should monitor an environment, notice when an external event makes progress possible, then respond promptly without wasting resources while waiting. To measure progress on this class of tasks, we introduce SentinelBench, an open-source benchmark for time-evolving monitoring tasks.
  SentinelBench contains 100 tasks across 10 synthetic web environments, including email, calendars, finance, professional networking, and entertainment. Each environment exposes a live web interface and replays a scripted sequence of events, requiring agents to navigate and reason about web pages whose state shifts underfoot. SentinelBench measures task completion, reaction time, and resource use, exposing the tradeoff between responsiveness and cost. We report results across three models and two browser-agent harnesses, establishing performance baselines for future comparison and demonstrating how agent design choices can dramatically impact key metrics. Together, these results show that SentinelBench distinguishes meaningful differences in agent behavior.

</details>


### [278] [An interpretable and trustworthy AI framework for large-scale longitudinal structure-pain association studies using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05357)
*Jincheng Yu,Haoyang Li,Yiwen Liu,Shen Liu,Rachel Yuanbao Chen,C. Kent Kwoh,Hongxu Ding,Xiaoxiao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过引入保形预测的深度学习框架，显著提升了膝关节MRI中MOAKS特征的预测精度，并结合潜类别混合模型证实了骨髓病变、软骨丢失及半月板挤压与膝骨关节炎疼痛进展之间的强相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏在大规模临床数据集上对膝骨关节炎结构异常与疼痛之间关系的深入研究，且现有的深度学习MRI分析模型往往缺乏可解释性和可信度。

Method: 首先，构建基于深度学习的MOAKS评分预测模型，并引入保形预测（Conformal Prediction）进行不确定性量化，筛选出高置信度的预测结果；其次，利用纵向潜类别混合模型（LCMM），基于筛选后的数据分析结构异常与四种膝盖疼痛测量指标之间的关联。

Result: 深度学习模型预测MOAKS特征的性能显著提升（MCC：BML从0.69升至0.91；CART从0.45升至0.80；ME从0.59升至0.89）。基于高置信度预测，确定了两种疼痛轨迹（快速和稳定进展），并发现BML、CART和ME显著增加了快速疼痛进展的风险（OR分别为1.62、1.83和2.50）。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了BML（骨髓病变）、CART（软骨丢失）和ME（半月板挤压）是膝骨关节炎疼痛及其功能进展的重要风险因素。该结合深度学习与统计学建模的框架为大规模关联分析提供了一种可靠、可解释的方法。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop an interpretable and trustworthy AI framework that combines deep learning based MRI Osteoarthritis Knee Score (MOAKS) prediction with interpretable statistical modeling to study structure-pain relationships at scale using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI). Materials and Methods: We first developed a deep learning framework to predict MOAKS features directly from knee MRIs and incorporated conformal prediction to provide prediction uncertainty quantification. This uncertainty-aware strategy enables explicit filtering of model outputs, retaining only high-confidence MOAKS predictions at the knee level. Second, we applied a longitudinal latent class mixed model (LCMM) to examine associations between key structural abnormalities and four complementary knee pain measurements. Results: Among the three MRI-defined abnormalities (i.e., bone marrow lesions (BML), cartilage loss (CART), and meniscal extrusion (ME)), our framework substantially improved the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) and some other metrics. For example, MCC increased from 0.69 to 0.91 for BML, from 0.45 to 0.80 for CART, and from 0.59 to 0.89 for ME. Using these high-confidence predictions, we expanded the sample size to 2,175 knees for the LCMM analysis. Two distinct pain trajectories were identified (rapid and stable pain progression). The estimated odds ratios (95% CI) for the rapid progression group were 1.62 (1.12-2.35) for BML, 1.83 (1.24-2.70) for CART loss, and 2.50 (1.75-3.57) for ME. Conclusion: These results highlight the importance of these structural abnormalities as risk factors for pain and functional progression in osteoarthritis.

</details>


### [279] [Synthetic Contrastive Reasoning for Multi-Table Q&A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05382)
*Ankit Pratap Singh,Xin Su,Phillip Howard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对比推理轨迹和对比偏好优化（CPO）的方法，通过引入带推理过程的负样本和训练数据，显著提升了模型在多表问答任务中的复杂推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多表问答资源缺乏推理监督（即答案推导过程），限制了模型在复杂推理任务中的表现。

Method: 1. 使用异构LLM构建包含验证过的正面轨迹和合理负面轨迹的合成对比推理数据集；2. 利用这些偏好对来对开源LLM进行对比偏好优化（CPO）微调。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B、Mistral-8B和Llama-3.1-8B模型上，相比传统的监督微调（SFT），CPO实现了9.7%-16.3%的平均绝对性能提升，在MMQA数据集上最高提升达21个百分点。

Conclusion: 利用异构LLM生成的对比推理轨迹训练，并通过对比偏好优化（CPO），能有效增强模型在多表问答任务中的推理能力和准确性。

Abstract: Multi-table question answering requires models to retrieve relevant evidence, link schemas, and perform compositional reasoning across relational tables. Existing multi-table Q&A resources typically provide questions and final answers but lack reasoning supervision that explains how answers are derived. To address this gap, we construct a synthetic contrastive reasoning-trace dataset for MMQA by generating validated positive traces and plausible negative traces with heterogeneous LLMs. We then use the resulting preference pairs to fine-tune open-weight LLMs with Contrastive Preference Optimization (CPO). Across Qwen3-14B, Mistral-8B, and Llama-3.1-8B, CPO achieves absolute average improvements over Q&A supervised fine-tuning ranging from 9.7%-16.3%, with gains up to 21 percentage points on MMQA. Ablations show that heterogeneous positive and negative trace generators strengthen the contrastive signal, and automated as well as human evaluations indicate that the generated pairs are largely faithful, coherent, and meaningfully contrastive.

</details>


### [280] [Stability vs. Manipulability: Evaluating Robustness Under Post-Decision Interaction in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05384)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了 LLM 作为评委时存在“决策后可操纵性”缺陷：即使是看似稳健的评估结果，通过后续的对抗性对话即可轻易被推翻。这种现象并非基于纠错，而是基于后验合理化，对现有的自动化基准测试的准确性和公平性构成了挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑“LLM-as-judge”评估范式中关于“评估结果是固定输入的稳定属性”的假设。研究者怀疑在初始决策后，通过进一步的交互（如反驳、权威诱导）可能会导致模型评价发生不稳定的改变（决策后可操纵性）。

Method: 通过在 MT-Bench 和 AlpacaEval 两个基准上进行受控实验，对比重复/中立重测与“决策后挑战”（post-decision challenge）两种场景，并引入“抗基线挑战协议”和“平衡目标验证协议”来隔离判断翻转与偏见诱导，同时提出了“评估鲁棒性分数（ERS）”用于量化评估模型的交互稳定性。

Result: 实验发现，LLM评委在受干扰的交互下表现出明显的“易反转性”：尽管模型自称高置信度，但其判断极易被后续对话推翻；这种改变往往伴随低重合度的理由（后验合理化），且权威框架（Authority framing）会显著降低模型评价的鲁棒性，导致基准测试排名偏移及与人类偏好的一致性下降。

Conclusion: “LLM-as-judge”评价方法存在严重的交互式脆弱性（即“决策后可操纵性”），现有的静态评估基准无法反映模型在对抗性交互下的真实可靠性。研究建议将交互鲁棒性纳入评估指标体系（ERS），以减少后验合理化（post hoc rationalization）带来的评估偏差。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge evaluation is widely used in benchmarking pipelines, where model outputs are compared and ranked using automated evaluators. These pipelines typically assume that judgments are stable properties of fixed inputs. We show that this assumption does not hold under interaction. We study post-decision manipulability: the extent to which an evaluation outcome can be altered through subsequent conversation with the judge after an initial decision has been made. Across controlled experiments on MT-Bench and AlpacaEval, we find that LLM judges are highly stable under repeated and neutral reevaluation, yet become substantially reversible under targeted post-decision challenge. An anti-baseline challenge protocol shows that stable judgments can be overturned through motivated interaction, while a counterbalanced target-validation protocol separates this reversibility from net target-directed steering. These reversals have practical consequences: they can degrade agreement with human preferences, shift benchmark rankings, and produce harmful evaluation changes despite high self-reported confidence. Authority framing is especially destabilizing, and revised judgments are often accompanied by low-overlap justifications, suggesting post hoc rationalization rather than reliable error correction. We introduce the Evaluation Robustness Score (ERS) to quantify interactional robustness by combining reversal susceptibility with counterbalanced directional effects. Our findings identify post-decision interaction as a distinct failure mode for LLM-as-judge evaluation and motivate evaluation protocols that measure not only static agreement, but robustness under challenge.

</details>


### [281] [Residual Modeling for High-Fidelity Learned Compression of Scientific Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05389)
*Liangji Zhu,Sanjay Ranka,Anand Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对高精度科学数据压缩，该研究提出了一种专门针对学习型压缩器残差的预测与编码方法，有效解决了高保真场景下由残差修正导致的码率过高问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的保证自动编码器（GAE）在处理高保真度（NRMSE 10^-6 到 10^-4）时，为了满足精度要求，需要保留过多的 SVD/PCA 系数，导致修正流码率过高，压缩效率下降。

Method: 提出了一种残差中心化的编码框架，包含两种方案：1. LBRC：一种无训练、确定性的流水线，通过自适应量化和基于 3D Lorenzo 差分及熵编码的无损压缩来处理残差；2. NGLR：在 LBRC 基础上引入因果神经预测器以减少剩余残差的熵，同时保持解码的确定性。

Result: 在 E3SM, JHTDB 和 ERA5 数据集上，LBRC 相比 GAE 压缩率提升了 30-60%；NGLR 在此基础上进一步提升了 10-40%，并在高保真度场景下超越了传统压缩标准 SZ。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于残差编码的新方法（LBRC 和 NGLR）通过专门针对学习型压缩器的残差特性进行优化，成功克服了高保真度场景下传统保证自动编码器（GAE）残差修正流导致的码率暴增问题，在高精度压缩任务中实现了优于传统算法（如 SZ）的压缩性能。

Abstract: Lossy compression is essential for massive spatiotemporal data from scientific simulations. Learned compressors can achieve high compression ratios at moderate accuracy targets, but their aggregate reconstruction losses do not guarantee accuracy for each block. Existing Guaranteed Autoencoder (GAE) methods add a per-block residual correction by retaining SVD/PCA-style coefficients until the target is met. This works at moderate tolerances, but in the high-fidelity regime with block-level NRMSE from 10^-6 to 10^-4, the number of retained coefficients grows quickly and the correction stream dominates the total rate.
  We propose a residual-centric view: the learned residual is structurally different from the original scientific field and should be coded with a representation designed for that residual. We introduce two residual coders. LBRC is a deterministic, training-free pipeline that adaptively quantizes the learned residual to the target NRMSE and losslessly encodes the resulting integer residual using 3D Lorenzo differencing, zigzag mapping, bit-plane coding, and entropy coding. NGLR adds a causal neural predictor that outputs a normalized bias for an integer-rounded Lorenzo prediction in the same deterministic integer pipeline, reducing the entropy of the remaining residual code while preserving deterministic decoding. The predictor weights are serialized and counted in the bitstream.
  Across E3SM, JHTDB, and ERA5 at block-level NRMSE targets from 10^-6 to 10^-4, LBRC improves compression ratio over GAE by 30-60% and is broadly competitive with SZ. NGLR adds a further 10-40% over LBRC and outperforms SZ in the evaluated high-fidelity regime. These results show that residual representations tailored to learned-compressor residuals can preserve the advantage of learned compression when global residual correction becomes rate-dominant.

</details>


### [282] [LeanMarathon: Toward Reliable AI Co-Mathematicians through Long-Horizon Lean Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05400)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Yuekai Sun,Taiji Suzuki,Jason D. Lee,Fanghui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeanMarathon通过多智能体协作和动态蓝图机制，解决了自动形式化过程中长范围推理的稳定性问题，实现了复杂的数学研究论文的高效、可靠形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长范围（Long-horizon）的数学自动形式化在处理复杂问题时存在扩展性瓶颈，易导致陈述偏差、依赖混乱、上下文丢失及局部修复破坏整体逻辑等问题。

Method: 提出了LeanMarathon，一个多智能体框架，利用“演进蓝图（evolving blueprint）”作为核心抽象（兼具Lean证明骨架、自然语言证明图和共享记录系统）。该框架通过四种智能体（构建、审计、证明、修复）协作，并由两阶段编排器管理（先通过对抗审查稳定目标保真度，再以CI门控并行处理证明DAG）。

Result: 在4个近期Erdős问题的研究论文上进行了评估，成功形式化7个目标定理，无遗留错误（no sorry），累计证明了258个引理和定理。

Conclusion: AI辅助数学研究不仅需要更强的证明器，还需要耐用的框架（如LeanMarathon）来确保在长期的数学推导过程中保持目标保真度。

Abstract: Long-horizon autoformalization of research mathematics fails not only at hard lemmas, but at scale: statements drift, dependencies tangle, context decays, and local repairs corrupt distant work. We present LeanMarathon, a multi-agent harness for reliable research-level Lean autoformalization. Its core abstraction is an evolving blueprint: a Lean file that serves simultaneously as formal proof skeleton, natural-language proof graph, and shared system of record. Four contract-scoped agents construct, audit, prove, and repair this blueprint. These agents are coordinated by a two-stage orchestrator that first stabilizes target fidelity through adversarial review and then discharges the proof directed acyclic graph (DAG) from its dynamic leaves upward in parallel CI-gated rounds. LeanMarathon turns one brittle multi-hour run into many local, recoverable, parallel transactions. We evaluate LeanMarathon on two recent research papers spanning four Erdős problems (#1051, #1196, #164, #1217). Across three autonomous runs, it formalizes all seven target theorems with no sorry, proving 258 lemmas and theorems. These results show that reliable AI co-mathematics requires not only stronger provers, but durable harnesses that preserve target fidelity across long mathematical developments. The code can be found at https://github.com/YuanheZ/LeanMarathon.

</details>


### [283] [Harnessing Generalist Agents for Contextualized Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05404)
*Zihao Li,Kaifeng Jin,Yuanchen Bei,Jiaru Zou,Avaneesh Kumar,Xuying Ning,Yanjun Zhao,Mengting Ai,Baoyu Jing,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeClaw 是一个赋能通用 LLM 处理时间序列的智能体框架，通过引入原生运行时支持、可重用分析例程及多模态记忆，实现了对复杂时序任务的端到端、可审计的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的时间序列分析往往包含复杂的背景上下文，且不仅限于单一任务（如预测），需要端到端的推理工作流。然而，目前的通用 AI 智能体主要在文本空间操作，缺乏对结构化时序信号的有效对齐。

Method: 提出了 TimeClaw 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 可执行的时间序列工具（用于接地和可审计分析）；(2) 基于经验的各种能力演化（创建可重用的分析例程）；(3) 情境化多模态记忆（检索相关的推理轨迹）。

Result: 在能源、金融、气象、交通等多个现实世界领域的基准测试中，TimeClaw 均表现出优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: TimeClaw 通过为通用 LLM 提供时间序列原生运行时支持，解决了现有 AI 在处理复杂上下文时间序列分析中的局限性，有效提升了跨领域的开放式时间序列推理能力。

Abstract: Time series are often embedded in rich contexts that are essential for holistic modeling. Moreover, real-world practitioners often require end-to-end workflows for analyzing temporal dynamics, where widely studied tasks such as forecasting are only one step in a broader solution loop. While generalist AI agents offer a promising interface for such workflows under complex contexts, they still operate primarily in textual spaces that are not fully aligned with structured temporal signals. In this work, we introduce TimeClaw, an agentic harness framework for time series that equips generalist LLM agents with the time series-native runtime support needed for contextualized temporal reasoning. TimeClaw integrates executable temporal tools for grounded and auditable analysis, experience-driven capability evolution for creating reusable analytical routines, and episodic multimodal memory for retrieving relevant reasoning traces. Together, these components unlock harnessed open-ended temporal reasoning with contextual information. Extensive evaluation on multiple benchmarks covering diverse tasks across energy, finance, weather, traffic, and other real-world domains demonstrates improved performance of TimeClaw. Code is available at https://github.com/iDEA-iSAIL-Lab-UIUC/TimeClaw.

</details>


### [284] [Agents' Last Exam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05405)
*Yiyou Sun,Xinyang Han,Weichen Zhang,Yuanbo Pang,Tianyu Wang,Yuhan Cao,Yixiao Huang,Chris Duroiu,Haoyun Zhang,Jeffrey Lin,Weishu Zhang,Tyler Zeng,Ying Yan,Bo Liu,Hanson Wen,Mingyang Xu,Xiaoyuan Liu,Zimeng Chen,Weiyan Shi,Amanda Dsouza,Vincent Sunn Chen,Patrick Bryant,Carl Boettiger,Yamini Rangan,Bradley Rothenberg,Kyle Steinfeld,Arvind Rao,Tapio Schneider,Georgios Yannakakis,Laure Zanna,Kaan Ozbay,Ida Sim,Tarek Zohdi,George Em Karniadakis,Jack Gallant,Teresa Head-gordon,Yushan Li,Wenxi Deng,Tao Sun,Huiqi Wang,Zhun Wang,Justin Xu,Chris Yuhao Liu,Yafei Cheng,Rongwang Hu,Aras Bacho,Shengcao Cao,Zengyi Qin,Yixiong Chen,Hengduan Fan,Hao Liu,Lin Zeng,Shashank Muralidhar Bharadwaj,Litian Gong,Yingxuan Yang,Maojia Song,Ruheng Wang,Zongzheng Zhang,Honglin Bao,Shuo Lu,Jianhong Tu,Zhonghua Wang,Zheng Zhang,Zijiao Chen,yanqiong Jiang,Zhendong Li,Bohan Lyu,Chang Ma,Peiran Xu,Benran Zhang,Shangding Gu,Haoyue Hua,Haoyang Li,Wanzhe Liao,Chengzhi Liu,Junbo Peng,Haoran Sun,Zechen Xu,Bo Chen,Jiayi Cheng,Yi Jiang,Keying Kuang,Yuan Li,Youbang Pan,Ziyan Rao,Alexander Schubert,Yifan Shen,Vincent Siu,Xiatao Sun,Kangqi Zhang,Xiaopan Zhang,Yuchen Zhu,Ishaan Singh Chandok,Lei Ding,Jingxuan Fan,Andrew Glover,Jiaming Hu,Yiran Hu,Wenbo Huang,Zixin Jiang,Haoran Jin,Lukas Kim,Ming Liu,Yang Liu,Alireza Rafiei,Xuhuan Shen,Kunyang Sun,Sophia Sun,Ting Sun,Eric Wang,Yixin Wang,Hanwen Xing,Sihan Xu,Yuzheng Xu,Zhongxing Xu,Zhiling Yan,Boqin Yuan,Ruiqi Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Zibo Zhao,Liana,Santanu Bosu Antu,Haoyue Bai,Carlo Bosio,Joseph Cavanagh,Patricia Cavazos-Rehg,Tianxing Chen,Xuewen Chen,Yipu Chen,Zhu Chenyu,Chen Dai,Stefano De Castro,Yunfu Deng,Kaustubh Dhole,Jiayuan Ding,Chenchen Du,Zhehang Du,Hao Fan,Run-ze Fan,Hengyu Fu,Shi Gu,Yifan Gu,Charlie Guo,Baihe Huang,Baixiang Huang,Rimika Jaiswal,Zhihan Jiang,Ran Jin,Erin Kasson,Xin Lan,Joseph Lee,Deren Lei,Chenyu Li,Daofeng Li,Haitao Li,Hongwei Li,Jingyan Li,Xiao Li,Yi Li,Yinsheng Li,Yuangang Li,Zhixu Li,Wenyu Liang,Longtai Liao,Kevin Qinghong Lin,AndyZeyi Liu,Che Liu,Jiaming Liu,Kaiyuan Liu,Xuan Liu,Pan Lu,Wenbo Lv,Yicheng Lv,Qiuyang Mang,Kyle Montgomery,Yuzhou Nie,Ruoxi Ning,Jorin Overwiening,Xu Pan,Layna Paraboschi,Core Francisco Park,Justin Purnomo,Swati Rajwal,Scott Rankin,Bixuan Ren,Yiren Rong,HaoYang Shang,Ventus Shaw,Fiona Shen,Jiawei Shen,Minqi Shi,Qiu Shi,Huaxiu Yao,Tianneng Shi,Jonah So,Vladislav Susoy,Hannah Szlyk,Haocheng Wang,Jialu Wang,Wei Wang,Xinyu Wang,Zehao Wang,Dowling Wong,Angela Wu,Dehao Wu,Fangyu Wu,Mengyuan "Millie" Wu,Yu Wu,Yuchen Wu,Yuhao Wu,Qingpo Wuwu,Weihang Xiao,Yongyi Xiong,Fan Xu,Ruiling Xu,Mingxuan Yan,Benjamin Yang,Jirong Yang,Sen Yang,Xiaoli Yang,Yushi Yang,Haoran Ye,Xiaohu Yu,Zhengming Yu,Chenlong Zhang,Chi Zhang,Hanning Zhang,Hanwen Zhang,Junge Zhang,Kunpeng Zhang,Song Zhang,Wenjin Zhang,Wenshuo Zhang,Ying Zhang,Yizhi Zhang,Brian Zhao,Qijian Zhao,Yimin Zhao,Yuhaohua Zheng,Liwei Zhou,Tianyue Zhou,Sichen Zhu,Siqi Zhu,Yan Zhu,Yishu Zhu,Jierui Zuo,Chonghao Cai,Helena Casademunt,Wenjia Chen,Benjamin Cheng,Nawen Deng,Rao Fu,Tianfu Fu,Yifan Han,Ren He,Zhenyu He,Qiao Jin,Lang Lang,Yuetai Li,Sylvia Liu,Lu Lu,Qing Lu,Subhabrata Mukherjee,Yunqi Ouyang,Yin Ren,Dawei Shi,Haoran Wu,Zhiyue Wu,Hannah Yao,Zhuoran Yi,Jenny Yu,Rhea Zhan,Hang Zhou,Blake Zhu,Junfan Zhu,Alan Yuille,Yang Liu,Russell Alan Poldrack,Jiachen Li,Zhenglu Li,Molei Tao,Jing Huang,Wenqi Shi,Costas Spanos,Lichao Sun,Chenguang Wang,Orson Xu,Zhen Dong,Hector Gomez,Aylin Caliskan,Ali Emami,Haimin Hu,Zhi Li,Lihui Liu,Murphy Niu,Yi Shao,Jianxin Sun,Mikko Tolonen,Ting Wang,Sanjiv Das,Yanjun Gao,Wenbo Guo,Erika J Schneider,Zhiyong Lu,Mark Mueller,Radha Poovendran,Somayeh Sojoudi,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了Agents' Last Exam (ALE)基准，旨在弥合AI基准测试表现与实际经济价值之间的鸿沟，重点评估AI代理在长周期、专业任务中的实际表现，实验显示当前模型在复杂任务上的成功率极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI基准测试与实际生产力需求脱节，导致模型在高分表现下难以转化为 economically meaningful（具有经济意义）的专业领域部署，作者认为这是评估机制存在缺陷。

Method: 引入了名为“Agents' Last Exam (ALE)”的基准测试，该基准测试基于O*NET/SOC 2018美国职业分类体系，涵盖13个行业集群的55个子领域及1000多个真实世界任务，旨在评估AI代理在长期、高价值任务上的表现并提供可验证的结果。

Result: 通过主流模型配置测试发现，ALE中最困难层次的任务表现极差，平均完整通过率仅为2.6%，表明模型离实现真正的生产力目标仍有巨大提升空间。

Conclusion: 目前的AI代理在完成长期、高经济价值的实际工作流程方面仍有巨大差距，ALE旨在通过建立行业标准和持续性评估，推动AI在实际经济场景中的落地应用，而不仅仅是作为排行榜工具。

Abstract: Recent AI systems have achieved strong results on a wide range of benchmarks, yet these gains have not translated into economically meaningful deployment across many professional domains. We argue that this gap is largely an evaluation problem: widely used benchmarks lack sustained performance measurement on real and economically valuable workflows. This paper introduces Agents' Last Exam (ALE), a benchmark designed to evaluate AI agents on long-horizon, economically valuable, real-world tasks with verifiable outcomes. Developed in collaboration with 250+ industry experts, ALE covers non-physical industries defined with reference to O*NET / SOC 2018 (the U.S. federal occupational taxonomy). It is organized around a task taxonomy with 55 subfields grouped into 13 industry clusters covering 1K+ tasks. Current results show that the hardest tier remains far from saturated: across mainstream harness and backbone configurations, the average full pass rate is 2.6%. ALE is designed as a living benchmark: its task pool grows continuously as new workflows and industries are onboarded. More broadly, ALE is intended not merely as another leaderboard, but as an instrument for closing the gap between benchmark success and GDP-relevant impact.

</details>


### [285] [Mutation Without Variation: Convergence Dynamics in LLM-Driven Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05408)
*Can Gurkan,Forrest Stonedahl,Uri Wilensky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM在程序变异任务中存在显著的结构性收敛偏差，难以维持长期的创造性探索，容易陷入特定的程序结构循环中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨LLM在重复进行程序变异时，是能探索出多样化的新程序形式，还是会陷入重复的循环，旨在揭示LLM在程序演化中的局限性。

Method: 通过在缺乏选择压力的环境下，在特定领域语言（DSL）中对LLM驱动的程序变异链进行实证分析；对比不同提示词设计、模型系列和随机复制副本，并与经典的遗传编程（GP）子树突变算子进行比较。

Result: LLM倾向于快速收敛到有限的“吸引子区域”（attractor regions）；87%的演化链中，超过93%的变异是重复已有的结构，变异主要局限于固定模板内的终结符替换；相比之下，传统GP突变未表现出类似的高度收敛性。

Conclusion: LLM在程序演化过程中存在固有的结构同质性偏见，表现为强烈的趋同效应。若要实现真正的开放式探索，必须解决这一系统性偏差。

Abstract: When an LLM repeatedly mutates a program, does it explore new forms or circle back to the same ones? We study this question by analyzing LLM-driven mutation chains in the absence of selection pressure within a domain-specific language, varying prompt design, model family, and stochastic replication. We find that LLM-based mutation consistently converges toward restricted attractor regions in program space. Convergence is especially severe at the structural level: in 87% of chains, over 93% of mutations revisit a previously seen structural form, with most variation confined to terminal substitutions within recurring templates. Cycle analysis reveals short cycles and self-loops dominating the transition structure. The rate of convergence varies with prompt wording and model choice, but the phenomenon is robust across conditions. A classical GP subtree mutation operator does not exhibit comparable convergence, suggesting that the effect is intrinsic to the LLM mutation pipeline. These findings reveal a tension at the heart of LLM-driven program evolution: the same capabilities that enable semantics-aware program transformation also carry a systematic bias toward structural homogeneity that must be accounted for if such systems are to sustain open-ended exploration. Source code is available at https://github.com/can-gurkan/lmca.

</details>


### [286] [A Motivational Architecture for Conversational AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05411)
*Anna Mikeda,Ben Goertzel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对对话式人工智能提出了一种新型的动机架构，将传统的生理稳态模型转化为适应语言交互的认知和社会动机调节系统，通过模块化的处理流水线和情感区分机制，提升代理在对话交互中的决策与行为调节能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的动机架构多限于调节物理代理的生理需求，而对话代理在语言环境（如话语、工具调用、沉默）中运行，缺乏针对对话特性的动机调节模型，因此需要一套专门适配对话交互的动机处理框架。

Method: 1. 基于 OpenPsi 动机演化模型并结合 MetaMo 的高阶动机框架进行重构；2. 设计了包含十个阶段的动机处理流程（将认知调节与情境评估分离）；3. 提出一种融合紧急驱动的快速反应与多目标优化决策的双重策略；4. 定义了动作前“感受（Feelings）”与动作后“情绪（Emotions）”的功能性架构区分。

Result: 通过在 CompanionAgent 和 ResearchAgent 两个案例上的专用化实现，验证了该架构在处理对话代理的认知需求（如胜任力、不确定性、归属感等）方面的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的对话式动机架构为人工智能代理在非物理环境中（对话环境）的行为提供了调节机制，通过将生理稳态转化为认知与社会层面的稳态需求，为通用人工智能达到类人交互水平提供了理论与架构支持。

Abstract: Motivational architectures in cognitive AI have largely been designed for physical agents regulating bodily needs. Conversational agents operate in a different regime: their sensorimotor loop is linguistic, their environment is a user's evolving mental state, and their consequential actions are speech acts, tool invocations, and strategic silences. This paper proposes a conversational reinterpretation of the OpenPsi motivational lineage, coupled to MetaMo's higher-level motivational scaffold, for agents built on a modular execution substrate. Homeostasis is recast in dialogue-native terms: the agent regulates competence, uncertainty reduction, affiliation, affinity, legitimacy, nurturing, and aesthetic coherence rather than bodily deficits. We propose three contributions: a ten-stage motivational processing pipeline that architecturally separates cognitive modulation from situational appraisal; a dual decision strategy blending urgency-driven fast response with deliberative multi-goal optimization; and an architecturally useful distinction between pre-action feelings and post-action emotions as functionally different forms of affect. We specialize the framework to two example agents -- CompanionAgent and ResearchAgent -- and sketch its extension to social robotics and domain-generic human-level AGI.

</details>


### [287] [Assessing the Carbon Emissions and Energy Consumption of U.S. Hyperscale Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05420)
*Gianluca Guidi,Francesca Dominici,Tiziano Squartini,Callaway Sprinkle,Jonathan Gilmour,Kevin Butler,Eric Bell,Scott Delaney,Falco J. Bargagli-Stoffi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究量化了美国超大规模数据中心的用电及碳足迹，发现其碳排放强度远高于全美电网平均水平，且目前高度依赖化石能源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能技术的广泛应用导致美国超大规模数据中心数量激增，其对环境造成的足迹（电力消耗与碳排放）引起了广泛关注，需要对其规模和影响进行量化评估。

Method: 收集了2024年5月至2025年4月间美国403个超大规模数据中心的设施级数据，利用美国环保署（EPA）的eGRID工厂级数据，通过不同负载情景估算电力消耗、电力来源及相关的CO2排放量。

Result: 在研究期内，这些HDC消耗约68-99 TWh电力，产生约37-54百万公吨CO2；核心情景下，其排放强度为545 gCO2/kWh，比美国电网平均值（370 gCO2/kWh）高出约48%，且约54%的电力来源于化石燃料。

Conclusion: 美国超大规模数据中心（HDC）的碳排放强度显著高于全美电网平均水平，且主要依赖化石燃料供电，其环境足迹对实现能源转型目标构成了挑战，文中提供的归因工具可有效评估该行业的影响。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of hyperscale data centers (HDCs) in the US, mainly driven by the adoption of artificial intelligence, has raised concerns about this industry's environmental footprint. We compiled facility-level information on 403 US hyperscale data centers operating between May 2024 and April 2025 and estimated their electricity consumption, electricity sources, and attributable CO2 emissions. Across different facility-load scenarios, these HDCs consumed approximately 68-99 TWh of electricity and were associated with about 37-54 million metric tons of CO2. Under the central scenario, HDC electricity demand corresponded to approximately 1.8% of total US electricity consumption, with roughly 54% of attributed generation supplied by fossil-fuel sources. The HDC electricity-weighted average carbon intensity was approximately 545 gCO2/kWh, about 48% above the contemporaneous US national grid-average carbon intensity of 370 gCO2/kWh. Our approach provides an attributional tool for assessing the environmental footprint of hyperscale data centers using the most recent EPA eGRID plant-level data.

</details>


### [288] [Minimizing the Hidden Cost of Scales: Graph-Guided Ultra-Low-Bit Quantization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05429)
*Rayyan Abdalla,Amir Hussein,Min Wu,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGE-PTQ 是一种超低比特量化框架，通过区分权重显著性并引入图导向的分组策略加二值化，在最小化缩放开销的同时，实现了比现有先进方法（如 BiLLM）更优的精度和推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流超低比特 PTQ 方法依赖固定的权重显著性假设或位置启发式策略，导致巨大的隐藏缩放开销，限制了部署效率。

Method: 提出 SAGE-PTQ 框架：1) 基于分布统计将权重分为显著与非显著两类；2) 利用稀疏图建模非显著权重以确定分组数量；3) 采用双模量化（显著权重多位精度，非显著权重二值化）；4) 通过每矩阵自适应显著性阈值及优化缩放因子分配进一步降低开销。

Result: SAGE-PTQ 平均达到 1.03 位权重量化和 0.004 位缩放开销；在 LLaMA-3-8B 上将 WikiText2 困惑度降至 6.74（BiLLM 为 55.8）且显存占用减半；在 LLaMA-2-70B 上推理速度提升 1.5 倍。

Conclusion: SAGE-PTQ 通过显著降低比特数和缩放开销，在保持大语言模型性能的同时，实现了卓越的推理效率，验证了其在超低比特量化领域的先进性。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is critical for the efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs). Recent ultra-low-bit PTQ methods rely on rigid weight-saliency assumptions or position heuristics, introducing substantial hidden scaling overhead. We propose SAGE-PTQ (Saliency-Aware Graph-guided Efficient PTQ), a novel ultra-low-bit quantization framework for LLMs that minimizes hidden scaling cost. SAGE-PTQ separates salient and unsalient weights using distributional statistics, then models subsampled unsalient weights as a sparse graph to estimate the optimal number of groups per layer. SAGE-PTQ applies dual-mode quantization, assigning multi-bit precision to salient weights and binarizing unsalient weights. To reduce scaling overhead, SAGE-PTQ uses one per-channel scale for salient weights and one scalar per unsalient group. Finally, SAGE-PTQ implements adaptive saliency thresholding to select the optimal saliency ratio per matrix. SAGE-PTQ achieves 1.03 weight bits and only 0.004 scaling bits per matrix on average, outperforming state-of-the-art methods such as BiLLM and PB-LLM. On LLaMA-3-8B, SAGE-PTQ achieves 6.74 WikiText2 perplexity, compared to 55.8 for BiLLM, while using less than 50% of BiLLM's GPU memory. On LLaMA-2-70B, SAGE-PTQ provides 1.5x faster decoding on one NVIDIA L40 GPU, demonstrating practical inference efficiency.

</details>


### [289] [Zero knowledge verification for frontier AI training is possible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05433)
*Pierre Peigné,Ky Nguyen,Paul Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用zkVM技术对大规模AI训练过程进行密码学验证的架构，解决了前沿AI模型监管中依赖自我报告的根本缺陷，实现了训练计算量的可审计性和可强制执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前沿AI治理框架过度依赖模型开发商的自我报告，缺乏技术性的训练过程验证手段，导致国际AI管制协议难以落实，无法有效监管具备重大外部性的技术。

Method: 提出一种针对大规模稠密预训练的验证架构，结合了：1. 预设的训练规范；2. 节点间网络观测；3. 实时计算Merkle承诺。利用支持BF16/FP32原生预编译的零知识虚拟机（zkVM）进行验证，确保浮点计算的真实性，并生成 genesis proof、step proofs 和 ex-ante attestations 三类证明。

Result: 定义了一种通过密码学证明将训练记录转化为“可治理执行制品”的方案；估算在单一位数百分比的训练开销下，可在36个月内实现部署，远快于定制芯片的研发周期；并列出了13项开放研究议程。

Conclusion: 当前对AI Frontier模型的监管依赖于不可靠的自我申报。本文提出的基于zkVM的密码学验证架构，能够在保持模型架构机密性的同时，确保训练计算量的可验证性，为实现可强制执行的国际AI治理协议提供了技术基础。预计该方案可在36个月内实现，且训练开销可控。

Abstract: Frontier AI governance frameworks increasingly use cumulative training compute as the primary criterion for designating high-impact models, but enforcement rests on self-reporting because no technical verification primitive for training exists. Any future international agreement on frontier AI faces the same problem at higher stakes: coordinated regulation of technologies with significant externalities has historically rested on technical verification, without which agreements are declaratory. Recent governance analyses judge zero-knowledge proofs a promising candidate but currently impractical at frontier scale [26, 4]. We argue the impracticality is paradigm-bound rather than fundamental, and propose a verification architecture for frontier dense pre-training combining a pre-committed training specification, inter-node network observations, and on-the-fly Merkle commitments of intermediate computation, verified through a zero-knowledge Virtual Machine (zkVM) with native BF16/FP32 precompiles. The proof checks the actual floating-point computation the GPU performed rather than a fixed-point approximation, and preserves model-architecture confidentiality through a private training specification. The protocol produces three proof types: a genesis proof at initialisation, in-training step proofs across the run, and ex-ante attestations enforcing policy-relevant claims as running invariants, turning the training record into a governance-enforceable artefact. We estimate a deployable proof of concept within approximately 36 months at single-digit-percent training-side overhead, against a six-to-ten-year cycle for verification-grade custom silicon. Thirteen open research and engineering problems are catalogued as a research agenda for external contribution

</details>


### [290] [Brick-Composer: Using MLLMs for Assembly with Diverse Bricks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05445)
*Jiateng Liu,Bingxuan Li,Zhenhailong Wang,Rushi Wang,Kaiwen Hong,Cheng Qian,Jiayu Liu,Denghui Zhang,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Manling Li,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BC-Bench基准测试与Brick-Composer训练框架，有效弥补了现有MLLM在精确积木装配（选择与位姿估计）中的能力短板，通过多维反馈学习实现了装配性能的实质性飞跃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索多模态大模型（MLLM）是否具备构建真实物理对象所需的视觉定位与空间推理能力，并推动AI执行复杂积木装配任务的研究。

Method: 1. 提出BC-Bench基准测试：涵盖多样化积木装配任务；2. 提出Brick-Composer训练框架：集成人类设计演示（Human Design Sparks）、现实世界反馈（World Feedback，包含视觉与物理约束）以及合成经验（Synthetic Experience）三个核心信号进行模型微调。

Result: Brick-Composer使积木选择准确率提升至原来的3倍以上，显著降低位姿估计误差；严格步级装配成功率从不足1%提升至约15%；训练后的Qwen-3-8B模型能够在完整对象装配中完成多达42%的正确步骤。

Conclusion: 多模态大模型（MLLMs）在经过针对性的物理基础学习（如Brick-Composer框架）后，能够显著提升在积木装配任务中的表现，证明了在无需专用架构的情况下，通过强化训练赋予MLLM复杂物理装配能力的可行性。

Abstract: We dream of AI agents that can read arbitrary designs and construct real-world objects from reusable building blocks. As a first step toward this vision, we study whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) possess the visual grounding and spatial reasoning capabilities required for brick assembly. We formulate brick assembly as a sequential decision-making problem, where each step involves two subtasks: brick selection, identifying the target brick from candidate components, and brick pose estimation, predicting where and how the selected brick should be placed. To support this study, we introduce BC-Bench (Brick Construction Benchmark), the first benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on assembly with diverse bricks. Experiments show that current state-of-the-art MLLMs remain far from reliable builders, struggling with fine-grained brick selection and failing at precise pose estimation. To bridge this gap, we propose Brick-Composer, a learning framework that equips MLLMs with assembly skills through three complementary signals: Human Design Sparks, which provide affordance-rich construction demonstrations; World Feedback, which grounds predicted actions in visual and physical consequences; and Synthetic Experience, which scales learning beyond existing object designs. Brick-Composer improves brick selection accuracy by over three times, substantially reduces pose estimation errors, and raises strict step-level assembly success from less than 1% to around 15%. After training, a Qwen-3-8B can correctly compose up to 42% of the steps for a complete object, suggesting that MLLMs can acquire assembly capabilities through targeted, physically grounded learning.

</details>


### [291] [Insurance of Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05449)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了代理智能体AI带来的复杂风险挑战，提出了基于精算和场景分析的保险评估框架，并建议通过多层次、互补的保险生态系统来覆盖其带来的新型自主性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理智能体AI从单纯的信息生成演进为具备自主规划、工具调用和执行能力的系统，产生的新型风险难以被传统的网络、职业责任或产品责任险覆盖，亟需建立新的保险框架来应对。

Method: 定义代理智能体AI的自主性连续体；分析主要风险路径（如幻觉、提示词注入、模型漂移等）；构建评估风险的精算框架（暴露评估、场景分析、依赖映射、累积风险）；提出整合多种险种的协调保险架构方案。

Result: 现有的保险产品正在通过调整来适应新兴风险；通过明确的分配机制和专属聚合额度，可以将网络险、技术专业责任险、产品责任险等整合为协调的保险架构。

Conclusion: 代理智能体AI的保险未来不在于单一品种，而在于构建一个多层级的互补生态系统，并辅以治理、透明度、遥测技术和监管明确性。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) systems are transforming the risk landscape by extending beyond information generation to autonomous planning, tool invocation, decision execution, and persistent modification of digital and physical environments. These capabilities introduce novel exposures that do not fit neatly within traditional insurance categories such as cyber, professional liability, product liability, or directors and officers coverage. This paper examines the emerging insurance market for agentic AI and develops a framework for understanding its underwriting, pricing, reinsurance, and product-design implications. We characterize agentic AI as a continuum of autonomy and delegated authority, emphasizing the distinction between informational outputs and systems capable of independently generating insured events through external actions. We analyze major risk pathways, including hallucinations, prompt-injection attacks, autonomous decision errors, model drift, dependency failures, and cyber-physical harms, and evaluate how existing insurance products are adapting to address these exposures. The paper further proposes an actuarial framework based on exposure assessment, scenario analysis, dependency mapping, and accumulation-risk management, drawing parallels to the evolution of cyber insurance. Finally, we present a coordinated insurance architecture that integrates cyber, technology errors and omissions, product liability, performance-warranty, and affirmative AI-liability coverages through explicit allocation mechanisms and dedicated AI aggregates. The analysis suggests that the future of agentic-AI insurance lies not in a single monoline product but in a layered ecosystem of complementary coverages supported by improved governance, transparency, telemetry, and regulatory clarity.

</details>


### [292] [Output Type Before Quality: A Standards-Derived XAI Admissibility Rubric for Autonomous-Driving Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05461)
*Abhinaw Priyadershi,Mandar Pitale,Jelena Frtunikj,Maria Spence*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自动驾驶可解释性AI（XAI）在安全标准下的“证据类型鸿沟”，指出主流的XAI方法（如SHAP）难以满足安全合规所需的因果证据要求，并提出了一套基于生命周期需求选择XAI方法的评估准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习驱动的自动驾驶安全标准要求提供具体的因果证据（如因果链、变量根因），但现有的XAI研究多基于技术分类而非证据需求，导致了“证据类型鸿沟”（evidence-type gap）——主流方法（如SHAP）无法直接转化为自动驾驶安全保障所需的因果证据。

Method: 通过分析 AMLAS、ISO 26262、ISO 21448 和 ISO/PAS 8800 等标准，提炼出覆盖7个生命周期阶段的19项可测试证据准则；以此评估六类XAI方法的结构适用性，并基于1,996段真实驾驶视频进行概念验证（VLA proof of concept）。

Result: 因果类XAI在识别危害、事故调查和数据管理方面具有明显优势（rubric gap 分别提升 62%、50%、50%），优于相关性或语言类方法。实验结果证实了不同XAI方法的输出类型与其依据生命周期需求预测的结构适用性相匹配。

Conclusion: 自动驾驶安全保障的XAI模型选择应由其生命周期阶段的证据需求主导，而非受方法论的流行度影响。结构性可解释性（特别是因果XAI）是在 hazard identification、incident investigation 和 data management 阶段满足安全标准的必要条件。

Abstract: Safety standards for ML-based autonomous driving specify the kind of evidence an assurance case must contain (directed cause-and-effect chains, quantified interventional effects, named root-cause variables), yet the XAI literature is organised by output type and technique family (saliency maps, feature attribution, counterfactuals, causal graphs, language traces). SHAP, the most-recommended ADS XAI method, returns a ranked feature list that no implementation effort can convert into a directed chain (Fig.1). We name this mismatch the evidence-type gap.
  From AMLAS, ISO 26262, ISO21448, ISO/PAS 8800 we derive 19 testable evidentiary criteria across 7 lifecycle stages with representative clause-cited derivations and score six XAI method classes structurally.
  Causal XAI emerges as structurally required to satisfy the derived criteria at three stages: hazard identification (+62% rubric gap), incident investigation (+50%), and data management (+50%); the verdict set is stable across thresholds T in (0%, 50%]$ and survives a worst-case single-cell flip down to T = 25%. At the remaining four stages, correlational or language-based methods are comparable or sufficient. The rubric identifies structural admissibility (necessary but not sufficient for compliance): an admissible method's specific output content may still be wrong, and validating that fidelity (the edges a fitted SCM produces, the cause a trace names) is the open assurance challenge. A single-VLA proof of concept on 1,996 real-world driving clips (79,840 rows, ten splits) is consistent with each method's observed output type matching its rubric prediction. XAI method selection for ADS safety assurance should be driven by lifecycle-stage evidence demand, not by method popularity.

</details>


### [293] [PSEBench: A Controllable and Verifiable Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs in Patient Safety Event Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05463)
*Keqi Han,Ryan Young,Annabel Strauss,Lindsey Hughes,Katharine M. Nesbitt,Nicole Schueler,Che Ngufor,Carl Yang,Yuan Xue,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对患者安全事件自动分类的需求，提出了基于“条款卡”的基准构建方法，并发布了PSEBench测试集，用于评估大模型在真实监管环境下的逻辑推理、信息追索及不确定性处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者安全事件分类通常依赖人工，极具重负荷且关键。现有LLM在该领域的应用因缺乏评价基准而受限，特别是缺乏在证据驱动推理、缺漏信息补全以及对模棱两可情况原则性弃权（abstention）方面的性能评估。

Method: 提出了一种以“条款卡（clause card）”为核心的策略基础构建方法，将监管文本转化为可审计的决策规范。利用锚点驱动的实例化（anchor-driven instantiation）和闭环验证（closed-loop verification）技术，构建了包含5,074个案例的评估集（PSEBench），并配套了一个代理式评估环境（agentic evaluation environment）。

Result: 成功构建了PSEBench基准测试集。对15种代表性LLM的评估显示：模型在处理该任务时存在一致的能力趋势，且该基准能有效识别模型在患者安全 triage 任务中存在的关键性能差距。

Conclusion: 研究证明了基于“条款卡（clause card）”的构建方法能够有效解决低资源环境下的基准测试构建问题。PSEBench为患者安全事件分类的自动化提供了可靠的评估工具，揭示了当前LLM在复杂监管逻辑推理、信息补全和不确定性处理上的能力差距。

Abstract: Patient safety event triage, determining whether a clinical event is reportable under jurisdiction-specific policy, is a high-stakes task typically performed manually by patient safety experts.
