Method: 1. 提出了 RPRO (Robotic Flow-matching Proximalized Preference Optimization) 算法：针对流匹配动作头优化的偏好优化目标，引入对比优化器与显式近端正则化器，防止奖励欺诈（reward-hacking）。2. 引入针对机器人任务的干预与回滚（intervention-and-rollback）数据采集范式。3. 开发了平滑插值（Smooth Interpolation）与批量混合程序，将稀疏的修正信号转化为密集的监督信号。

Result: 在四项长时程双臂机器人任务中，FlowPRO 的成功率均优于四个对照基准模型 (baselines)。消融实验验证了所提算法中各损失组件的贡献。

Conclusion: FlowPRO 通过集成 RPRO 算法与新型数据采集处理范式，有效解决了 VLA 模型在现实机器人部署中面临的策略鲁棒性和奖励设计难题，成功通过端到端的强化微调提升了长时程任务的成功率。

Abstract: Post-training Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models into policies that can be reliably deployed on real robots remains a major bottleneck. SFT and DAgger exploit failure signals only indirectly, and reward-based RL is bottlenecked by the difficulty of real-world reward design and of training reliable critics. We present FlowPRO, a reward-free offline reinforced fine-tuning framework for flow-matching VLAs. Algorithmically, we propose RPRO (Robotic Flow-matching Proximalized Preference Optimization), a preference-optimization objective tailored to the flow-matching action head of VLA models. RPRO pairs a contrastive optimizer with an explicit proximal regularizer that anchors the absolute magnitude of the implicit reward, thereby eliminating the reward-hacking failure mode of plain Flow-DPO. On the data side, a teleoperated intervention-and-rollback paradigm produces naturally paired positive and negative trajectories $(τ^w, τ^l)$ on a real robot from a single operator action; a Smooth Interpolation procedure, combined with batch mixing, then converts these sparse corrections into dense per-state supervision while preserving the base policy's capabilities. On four long-horizon bimanual tasks, FlowPRO attains the highest success rate, outperforming four representative baselines, and ablations confirm the contribution of each loss component.

</details>


### [405] [Learning Contact Representation for Leg Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05501)
*Emre Girgin,Cagri Kilic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅依赖关节编码器的自监督接触点检测框架，解决了足式机器人在缺乏外部力传感器情况下的里程计估算难题，并提升了对滑移等扰动的抗干扰能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人里程计估算严重依赖于对足端支撑相的准确判定，而传统的基于额外地反力传感器的检测方法存在硬件依赖性强、对滑移等扰动鲁棒性差等局限。

Method: 提出了一种自监督表示学习框架，仅利用机器人标准的关节编码器数据，通过对关节特征的建模来概率性地预测足部的支撑相（stance）和摆动相（swing）。

Result: 实验结果表明，该模型在无需人工标注和额外传感器的情况下，实现了高精度的接触检测，在与有监督方法和传统概率基线的对比中展现出更优的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的自监督接触检测框架无需额外的力传感器即可实现精准的足端状态估计，其性能优于传统的有监督学习方法及基准概率方法，且具有更好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: The estimation of odometry in legged robots depends on the assumption that the velocity of the foot with respect to the world remains zero during the stance phase. Feedback for the main body velocity is derived from the kinematic serial chain of the feet making accurate leg phase detection is a critical subproblem. A considerable number of studies employ ground reaction force sensors mounted at the tip of the foot to classify, yet these sensors may not be universally available for all legged robots. Additionally, these sensors are often unresponsive to unaccounted disturbances, such as slippage, while the foot remains in contact with the ground. In this study, we propose a self-supervised representation learning framework for contact detection that utilizes the standard sensor set of joint encoders without reliance on force sensor augmentations. We employ learned representations to model the stance and swing phases probabilistically. The experimental results obtained confirm the efficacy of the proposed self-supervised contact detector. Our framework exhibited superior performance in comparison to supervised methods which necessitate sensor set augmentation and labeling, as well as baseline probabilistic approaches. Additionally, we make our code available to the public.

</details>


### [406] [RadiusFPS: Efficient Farthest Point Sampling on CPUs and GPUs via Spherical Voxel Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06255)
*Ziyang Yu,Xiang Li,Qiong Chang,Jun Miyazaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人系统中 FPS 采样的计算瓶颈，本文提出了 RadiusFPS 算法，通过几何剪枝机制与高效 GPU 核函数设计，在保持采样质量的同时大幅提升了运行速度并降低了内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Farthest Point Sampling (FPS) 算法具有较高的计算复杂度，在处理现代高频 3D 传感器产生的大规模点云时成为实时 robotic 系统的延迟瓶颈。

Method: 提出 RadiusFPS 框架，利用球形体素构建几何边界（Geometric Bound）剪枝冗余计算，结合坐标级点跳跃测试（Coordinate-wise point-skip test）；并开发了 RadiusFPS-G，一种融合了体素选择、剪枝和距离更新的 GPU 核函数实现技术。

Result: 在室内 (S3DIS, ScanNet) 和室外 (SemanticKITTI) 数据集上，RadiusFPS-G 相较于现有 GPU 版 FPS 实现了高达 2.5 倍的加速，且在保持相当分割精度的同时，显存占用约为 QuickFPS 的一半，并在端到端推理中表现出极高的效率。

Conclusion: RadiusFPS 通过球形体素剪枝和优化的 GPU 实现，在保证与标准 FPS 相同采样结果的前提下，显著降低了计算与内存开销，为受限计算资源下的机器人感知任务提供了高效的实时采样方案。

Abstract: Point clouds are a primary sensory representation for robotic perception, underpinning LiDAR-based autonomous driving, simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM), and navigation. Within these pipelines, Farthest Point Sampling (FPS) is the most well-known downsampling operator, as its uniform coverage preserves the geometric structure on which downstream perception relies. However, the large time complexity of classical FPS scales poorly with the million-point-per-second rates of modern 3D sensors, making it a dominant latency bottleneck that conflicts with the real-time and limited onboard compute budgets of robotic systems. Therefore, we propose RadiusFPS, an FPS acceleration framework based on spherical voxel pruning that preserves the standard FPS update rule under the same initialization and tie-breaking policy. By indexing the point cloud with spherical voxels, RadiusFPS derives a conservative geometric bound that prunes redundant distance computations in each iteration, complemented by a coordinate-wise point-skip test that removes residual updates. We further introduce RadiusFPS-G, a warp-level GPU implementation that fuses voxel selection, pruning, and distance update into memory-coalesced kernels, eliminating costly global-memory round-trips. On indoor (S3DIS, ScanNet) and outdoor LiDAR (SemanticKITTI) benchmarks, RadiusFPS-G attains up to 2.5x speedup over GPU-based FPS and matches or exceeds QuickFPS among the evaluated methods while using roughly half its GPU memory, with comparable segmentation accuracy. When coupled with the learning-based FastPoint sampler, the resulting pipeline achieves the fastest End-to-End inference among all evaluated configurations. These properties make high-quality FPS-style sampling practical for latency- and memory-constrained robotic vision.

</details>


### [407] [Auditing Demonstration Curation Metrics: Action-Only Scorers Fail on the Structural Defects That Degrade Imitation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05588)
*Aarav Bedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个受控基准来评估模仿学习数据筛选指标，发现现有指标对细微扰动有效，但难以识别关键的“结构性错误”，且高筛检精度并不保证策略性能的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略的性能高度依赖于演示数据的质量，但现有用于过滤低质量数据的指标缺乏统一的基准验证，尚不清楚哪些指标真正能识别出损害策略性能的数据。

Method: 构建了一个受控测试平台，通过注入已知类型的缺陷（细微扰动和结构性错误）来审计七种现有的演示数据筛选指标，并在区分度和下游任务成功率两个维度上进行评估。

Result: 细微扰动（如动作噪声、抖动）可通过离群点检测有效识别；而结构性错误（决策偏离）对所有纯动作指标均为不可见，且部分指标表现出负面相关性。仅基于状态轨迹的指标对结构错误有一定的识别力，但即便表现最好的指标也只能回收约三分之一的性能损失。

Conclusion: 当前的动作观测（action-only）指标无法检测结构性缺陷，甚至可能出现误判。仅基于状态轨迹的指标能一定程度上检测结构错误，但修复效果有限。高检测准确度并不等同于下游任务性能的提升。

Abstract: Imitation-learning policies inherit the quality of the demonstrations they are trained on, and a growing set of curation metrics promise to score and filter low-quality demonstrations automatically. These metrics are each validated on different data with different protocols, so it is unclear which of them actually identify the demonstrations that harm a policy. We build a controlled testbed in which demonstration defects are injected with known type, and audit seven curation metrics along two axes: how well each separates defective from clean demonstrations, and whether training a behavior-cloning policy on each metric's curated subset improves task success. We study two defect regimes. Subtle perturbations (correlated action noise, tremor, truncation) are detectable by multivariate outlier scoring and, once removed, recover the full downstream gap. Structural errors, where the demonstration executes a wrong action at a key moment, are invisible to every action-only metric we test, and two of them are inverted: they score defective demonstrations as higher quality and, used for curation, tend to leave the policy at or below the uncurated baseline rather than above it. Only metrics that examine the state trajectory detect structural errors, and even the best of them recovers just a third of the downstream gap. High detection accuracy does not guarantee downstream improvement. We release the testbed and all curation implementations.

</details>


### [408] [Discrete-WAM: Unified Discrete Vision-Action Token Editing for World-Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05645)
*Ziyang Yao,Haochen Liu,Yuncheng Jiang,Zeyu Zhu,Zibin Guo,Jingru Wang,Tianle Liu,Jianwei Cui,Kuiyuan Yang,Hongwei Xie,Jingwei Zhao,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Discrete-WAM是一种将视觉状态与动作数字化为统一离散标记的自动驾驶世界模型，通过离散扩散框架支持可控的因果推理与组合式决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶方法多基于简单的状态到动作映射，缺乏对动作驱动的环境演变（因果作用）的显式建模；而现有的连续潜在世界模型难以实现反事实场景的组合式因果推理。

Method: 提出Discrete-WAM框架，将未来视觉状态和自我动作表示为对齐的离散标记（Discrete Tokens），并构建了一个共享的离散扩散生成框架，联合建模世界动态、世界动作策略及分层决策策略。

Result: 在大型自动驾驶基准测试中表现出竞争性性能，并成功支持场景的可控生成和反事实推理。

Conclusion: Discrete-WAM通过统一的离散对齐和共享扩散框架，实现了一种具备因果推理能力和组合泛化性的自动驾驶决策策略，为更可靠的自动驾驶提供了一种有前景的实现路径。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires reasoning about how ego actions shape the evolution of the surrounding world. However, most end-to-end methods rely on direct state-to-action mappings, capturing correlations without explicitly modeling action-conditioned dynamics. Conversely, continuous-latent world models often lack compositional structure for causal reasoning across counterfactual futures. We introduce Discrete-WAM, a unified latent vision-action world policy that represents future visual states and ego actions as aligned discrete tokens, enabling compositional causal reasoning across alternative futures. Built upon this unified discrete alignment, Discrete-WAM establishes a shared discrete diffusion framework with unified generative tasks, jointly formulating world modeling, world-action policy, and hierarchical decision-enabled policy, supporting compositional generalization across diverse driving scenarios. Experiments on large-scale autonomous-driving benchmarks show that Discrete-WAM achieves competitive performance while supporting controllable generation and counterfactual reasoning, offering a principled path toward more reliable decision-making.

</details>


### [409] [Safe Embodied AI for Long-horizon Tasks: A Cross-layer Analysis of Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05660)
*Dabin Kim,Daemin Park,Sangyub Lee,Jinsik Kim,Yeongtak Oh,Jongho Shin,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这篇综述系统性地梳理了长程具身机器人操作中的安全性挑战，提出了基于干预时序（规划、策略、执行）的分类框架，指出了现有研究在形式化证明、基准评测和不确定性处理方面的显著差距，并为未来安全部署机器人提供了研究导向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI在处理长程物理任务时安全性至关重要，但现有文献在规划、策略设计和运行时执行等方面较为碎片化，缺乏一个综合的框架来审视长程操作中因语义错误、任务传播、执行偏差及接触式风险带来的安全挑战。

Method: 采用文献综述法，针对长程机器人操作，按照“规划时（Planning-time）”、“策略时（Policy-time）”和“执行时（Execution-time）”的安全干预位点对文献进行分类梳理，并区分了形式化证明、统计支持与经验启发式方法论证。

Result: 分析表明，当前研究在政策级安全性支持上证据不足，针对接触丰富的长程操作缺乏强形式化支持，且缺乏统一的安全性基准测试及成熟的不确定性触发干预机制。

Conclusion: 现有的安全保障方法在长程操作领域仍存在碎片化问题。未来的研究应聚焦于跨层保障机制、更严谨的评估设计，以推动具备安全保障的具身机器人能在真实物理世界中稳健部署。

Abstract: Embodied AI systems are increasingly expected to reason and act over extended horizons in physical environments. This growing capability brings safety to the foreground, because failures in the physical world can harm people, damage objects, and disrupt workplaces. Although safe embodied AI has attracted substantial attention, the literature remains fragmented across planning, policy design, and runtime execution. Long-horizon robotic manipulation is a particularly revealing anchor domain for this problem because semantic misgrounding, subtask-level error propagation, execution drift, and contact-rich physical risk can accumulate within the same closed-loop system. This survey therefore provides a structured review of safety in long-horizon robotic manipulation from an embodied AI perspective. We organize the literature by intervention locus, covering planning-time, policy-time, and execution-time safety, and we analyze the strength of the evidence that each line of work provides, distinguishing formal guarantees, statistical support, and empirical safety heuristics. This framework clarifies the distinct roles of backbone capability papers, direct safety mechanisms, and benchmark or evaluation studies, while exposing where current safety claims are well supported and where they remain indirect. We identify persistent gaps, including limited evidence for policy-time safety, weak formal support for contact-rich long-horizon manipulation, immature uncertainty-triggered intervention, and a shortage of manipulation-specific safety benchmarks. We conclude by outlining research directions for cross-layer assurance, evaluation design, and safer deployment of long-horizon robotic agents in real-world settings.

</details>


### [410] [Preserving Full 6-DOF Actuation Under Abrupt Total Rotor Failures: Passive Fault-Tolerant Flight Control Using a Biaxial-Tilt Hexacopter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05663)
*Yipeng Yang,Yiqiao Tang,Hao Zhang,Jinqi Jiang,Jianfeng He,Rumo Chen,Xinghu Yu,Zhan Li,Huijun Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对双轴倾转六旋翼（BTO）的无源容错控制架构，无需故障检测即可在多电机失效下实现稳定的全6自由度飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多旋翼飞行器在发生突发电机故障时，力矩空间（AWS）急剧收缩，导致难以实现全6自由度恢复，因此亟需探索无需显式故障诊断即可实现故障容错的控制架构。

Method: 1. 扩展了可达力矩空间（AWS）的内切球度量，引入瞬态力矩跳变项以量化故障稳健性；2. 提出了两种无源方案：控制层采用高阶全驱动（HOFA）控制器结合线性扩张状态观测器（LESO），分配层采用基于模型参考自适应的分配方法及动量力矩估计。

Result: 通过仿真和飞行实验验证：BTO在单/多电机同时故障下均能保持稳定悬停或进行6自由度轨迹跟踪。相较于单轴倾转和共面六旋翼，BTO具备更大的失效后恢复裕度，并能在室内外复杂环境（如强风、狭窄空间穿越）中保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了双轴倾转过驱动六旋翼无人机（BTO）在不依赖显式故障检测或在线优化的情况下，能通过无源设计实现极强的容错飞行能力，且在多旋翼故障恢复裕度上优于常规构型。

Abstract: Conventional multirotors suffer from a rapid collapse of attainable wrench space (AWS) under abrupt total rotor failures, rendering full 6-DOF recovery physically impossible. This paper addresses passive fault-tolerant flight of a biaxial-tilt overactuated hexacopter (BTO) under abrupt total rotor failures that are a priori unknown to the controller. The control design and analysis focus on representative abrupt rotor-failure cases for which the post-failure system remains fully actuated, while no explicit fault detection, isolation, or fault-mode switching is assumed. First, we extend the inscribed-sphere metric of the AWS by incorporating the transient-wrench-jump term, enabling quantitative feasibility assessment under up to three simultaneous rotor failures and benchmarking against uniaxial-tilt and coplanar hexacopters. Second, we develop two computationally efficient passive schemes without relying on fault detection or online optimization. One scheme operates at the controller layer by combining a high-order fully actuated (HOFA) controller with a linear extended state observer (LESO) for lumped-disturbance rejection. The other scheme operates at the allocator layer by using model-reference adaptive control allocation with momentum-based wrench estimation to compensate for control-allocation biases. Simulations and flight experiments validate stable hovering and 6-DOF trajectory tracking under single and multiple rotor failures. Further systematic comparisons confirm that the BTO provides larger recovery margins than uniaxial-tilt and coplanar designs. Additional onboard-sensor-only experiments, including indoor tracking under wind disturbance, outdoor tracking under extreme conditions, narrow-frame traversal, and contact-based aerial writing, further validate the robustness of the proposed framework in complex operational environments.

</details>


### [411] [Dynamic Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery in Robotic Cellular Warehousing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05669)
*Cheng Ren,Ming Li,Xinping Guan,George Q. Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对仓储动态订单演化问题，本文提出两种基于令牌传递的分布式重规划算法，通过局部调整与协作优化，显著提升了动态环境下的任务处理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MAPD问题多假设任务静态，而实际仓储作业中订单SKU会动态增加，缺乏针对此类“订单内部演化”场景的有效求解机制。

Method: 提出两种事件触发式的在线重规划算法：1. 动态令牌传递（Dynamic Token Passing）：通过订单分解和基于优先级的令牌调度实现局部重规划；2. 协作令牌传递（Cooperative Token Passing）：引入空闲机器人协助机制，实现系统级协作优化。

Result: 在机器人仓储系统（RCWS）的仿真中，所提算法显著降低了任务的流转时间（order flowtime），且表现优于静态和非协作基线方案。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的两种基于令牌传递的动态重规划算法有效提升了复杂动态仓储环境下多智能体拣选任务的响应速度和系统整体效率，优于静态及非协作策略。

Abstract: Robotic Cellular Warehousing Systems (RCWS) give rise to multi-agent pickup and delivery (MAPD) processes in which robots sequentially collect multiple stock-keeping units (SKUs) for each order. Unlike classical MAPD formulations that assume static tasks, real warehouse operations often involve dynamic order evolution, where new SKUs may be appended to an order while it is being executed. Motivated by this practical requirement, this letter formulates the Dynamic Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery problem considering internal order evolution for the first time. Building on the token passing paradigm, we propose two event-triggered online replanning algorithms. The first, Dynamic Token Passing, performs localized replanning upon order updates through add-order decomposition and priority-based token scheduling while preserving collision-free execution. The second, Cooperative Token Passing, further enables idle robots to opportunistically assist newly added pickups, improving system-level efficiency. Simulation results in RCWS environments demonstrate that the proposed methods significantly reduce order flowtime compared with static and non-cooperative baselines.

</details>


### [412] [Accelerating and Scaling MPC-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Humanoid Locomotion and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05687)
*Junheng Li,Liang Wu,Sergio A. Esteban,Lizhi Yang,Ján Drgoňa,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 MPC-RL 框架，利用高效的并行 GPU MPC 求解器 π^nMPC 在训练阶段引导强化学习，解决了人形机器人控制中 MPC 开销大、RL 训练难的问题，显著提升了机器人运动与操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 MPC 与 RL 结合方案存在计算耗时长、训练成本高等问题，导致难以落地应用。本文旨在提出一种高效的“训练时 MPC 指导”框架，以兼顾物理合理性与鲁棒性。

Method: 1. 提出了一套基于质心动力学（centroidal-dynamics）的 MPC 奖励函数，用于在训练阶段引导策略学习。
2. 开发了 π^nMPC：一种并行于时域（parallel-in-horizon）、无需繁琐问题构建的 GPU 批处理求解器，有效解决了高内存占用与编译开销问题。

Result: 对比实验与硬件验证结果表明，MPC-RL 在人形机器人的运动控制（locomotion）和操作（manipulation）任务中均表现出优于基准方法的能力。

Conclusion: MPC-RL 框架通过融合 MPC 的物理约束与 RL 的鲁棒性，以及利用高效的并行 GPU 求解器 π^nMPC，实现了人形机器人行走与操控性能的显著提升，证明了该方法在实际应用中的有效性。

Abstract: In humanoid motion control, model predictive control (MPC) offers physically grounded prediction and constraint handling, while reinforcement learning (RL) enables robust whole-body skills through large-scale simulation. However, using MPC inside RL often requires time-consuming problem construction or excessive training overhead, making such frameworks difficult to justify in practice. This work studies efficient training-time MPC guidance for humanoid locomotion and manipulation, termed MPC-RL. We introduce a centroidal-dynamics MPC reward formulation that leverages guidance from MPC trajectories in training time. To make this practical in massively parallel RL, we develop $π^n$MPC, a parallel-in-horizon and construction-free batched GPU MPC solver that operates directly on time-varying dynamics to avoid high memory usage and pre-compilation. Through a variety of comparative studies and hardware validations, we have found that MPC-RL achieves superior performance in locomotion and manipulation skills. The code base is available at https://github.com/junhengl/mpc-rl.

</details>


### [413] [DexFuture: Hierarchical Future-State Visuomotor Targeting for Bimanual Dexterous Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05699)
*Runfa Blark Li,Kuang-Ting Tu,Nikola Raicevic,Dwait Bhatt,Xinshuang Liu,Keito Suzuki,Ki Myung Brian Lee,Nikolay Atanasov,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexFuture 提出了一种分层深度强化学习架构，通过将“预测未来状态轨迹”与“执行细粒度动作控制”解耦，实现了无需特权状态的高效、快速、鲁棒的双手灵巧工具操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的双手灵巧操作方法受限于对演示数据中特权状态的依赖，或者因依赖昂贵的在线动作规划而导致推理速度过慢，难以在复杂动态任务中实时应用。

Method: 提出了 DexFuture 分层框架：1. 高层：Future-State Visuomotor Target Predictor (基于 Transformer，根据历史多模态信息生成多步未来目标轨迹)；2. 低层：Target-Conditioned Structured Dexterous Policy (基于 Transformer，执行高频动作控制以跟踪目标)。这种范式将长程语义预测与精细动作控制进行了解耦。

Result: 在 OakInk2 双手工具使用基准测试中，DexFuture 的性能达到特权模型（Oracle）的 90%，远高于基线模型（7%）；同时运行频率高达 60 Hz，比基于 CEM 规划的同类系统快约 250 倍。

Conclusion: DexFuture 通过分层架构成功解决了双手灵巧工具使用的规划与控制难题，在大规模任务中实现了与特权策略相当的性能，并具备极高的推理实时性。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous tool use remains challenging for robots due to high-dimensional hand configurations and complex hand-tool-object dynamics and contact. Most existing control policies depend on future configuration references provided from demonstrations, while future action-conditioned world models require slow online planning over high-dimensional action sequences. A significant challenge is generating a dynamically consistent future reference trajectory without relying on privileged states from demonstrations or slow counterfactual planning. We propose DexFuture, a hierarchical system that couples a high-level Future-State Visuomotor Target Predictor with a low-level Target-Conditioned Structured Dexterous Policy. Conditioned on egocentric RGB, proprioceptive and geometric history, the high-level predictor constructs structured hand-tool-object visuomotor embeddings and uses a horizon-conditioned transformer to generate a multi-step future target trajectory. Then, the low-level policy tracks them with a target-conditioned per-link transformer. This hierarchy decouples coarse future reference generation from fine-grained action control, and slow long-horizon semantic prediction from high-frequency execution. On OakInk2 bimanual tool-use tasks, DexFuture achieves 90% of the privileged-oracle performance, compared to 7% for a no-reference policy. DexFuture operates at 60 Hz, approximately 250 times faster than DexWM-style Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) planning with a future action-conditioned world model.

</details>


### [414] [PiL-World: A Chunk-Wise World Model for VLA Policy-in-the-Loop Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05773)
*Chong Ma,Taiyi Su,Jian Zhu,Jianjun Zhang,Zitai Huang,Yi Xu,Hanli Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PiL-World 是一个新颖的分块式世界模型，通过在 VLA 策略推理与生成预测之间交替循环，实现了高保真的机器人闭环仿真评估，显著提升了策略离线评估的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人动作评估模型多局限于预采集轨迹的开环预测，无法支持 VLA 策略所需的闭环评估（即每一步动作都依赖上一步动作产生的观察结果）。为了填补这一空白，提出该模型以实现无需真实机器人持续介入的闭环评估。

Method: 提出了 PiL-World，一种用于策略闭环评估的分块式世界模型。该模型通过在 VLA 推理和世界模型预测之间交替运作，实现虚拟闭环；利用源于动作的视觉控制（视觉控制引导运动）和序列潜在历史来增强生成保真度，并同时预测多视图观测；通过混合学习成功与失败的演示数据，提升其对策略执行分布的拟合能力。

Result: 在三个真实的双臂操作任务上进行了评估，PiL-World 生成的模拟轨迹与真实执行高度一致；评估统计数据显示，其将真实环境与世界模型评估之间测得的成功率误差从 63.2% 降低至 12.0%。

Conclusion: PiL-World 有效实现了闭环 VLA 策略评估，极大地缩小了模拟评估与真实机器人操作成功率之间的差距，为机器人策略的离线测试提供了一种高保真度的替代方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies operate in a closed loop in real-world robot tasks: a robot observes the scene, executes an action chunk, and conditions its next decision on the resulting observation. However, most existing world models for robot action evaluation are limited to open-loop prediction along pre-collected action trajectories. This prevents them from supporting closed-loop VLA evaluation, where each action chunk must be conditioned on the observation generated by the previous execution. To address this gap, we propose PiL-World, a chunk-wise world model designed for policy-in-the-loop VLA evaluation. Given the current observation and the action trajectory rolled out by a VLA policy, PiL-World generates multi-view future observations that are consistent with the VLA rollout and match the image inputs required by the policy. By alternating between VLA inference and world-model prediction, PiL-World enables closed-loop evaluation without real robot execution at every step. To improve rollout fidelity, PiL-World conditions video generation on action-derived visual control from head-view robot motion and latent histories that encode task execution context, while jointly predicting complementary multi-view observations. Beyond successful teleoperated demonstrations, it also learns from failed execution trajectories, helping the imagined rollouts better match the distribution of real policy executions. We evaluate PiL-World on three real dual-arm manipulation tasks. PiL-World generates imagined rollouts that are highly consistent with real robot executions. More importantly, compared with the baseline, it reduces the error between VLA success rates measured in real-world rollouts and those estimated through closed-loop world-model evaluation from 63.2% to 12.0%.

</details>


### [415] [Visuotactile and Explicitly Force-Controlled Robotic Ultrasound for Abdominal Volumetric Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05848)
*Adrian Piedra,R Brooke Jeffrey,Oussama Khatib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套结合立体视觉与触觉反馈的自主机器人超声扫描系统，通过学习专家操作经验并辅以柔性力控技术，实现了对腹部复杂区域的高质量、自适应三维扫描。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决复杂人体解剖结构下，自主机器人超声扫描在探头接触力保持及适应性路径规划方面的挑战，旨在通过学习专家经验和利用多源传感反馈，提升扫描的标准化水平和诊断价值。

Method: 1. 数据采集：记录放射科医生的手法（探头运动、施加力、扫描策略）；
2. 环境感知：利用立体视觉构建腹部三维拓扑，通过触觉反馈识别肋骨边界；
3. 控制策略：基于7自由度柔性力控机械臂，结合专家策略进行闭环力反馈控制；
4. 扫描规划：实现沿肋骨下方斜向扫描和软组织垂直扫描两种路径。

Result: 物理实验表明，该系统能产生与人类专家相当的高质量图像，并能动态适应不同的患者体表地形；此外，机器人通过三维体积采集技术提高了诊断潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了整合专家经验与多模态传感（视觉、触觉）对于实现自主、自适应机器人超声扫描的有效性。该系统不仅能达到专家级的成像质量，还通过三维体积采集扩展了诊断能力，展示了感知驱动自主性与物理推理相结合在医疗机器人领域的前景。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a robotic ultrasound acquisition system that integrates stereo vision, touch-based feedback, and expert-informed strategies to perform autonomous and adaptive abdominal scans. The system records freehand motion and force data from expert radiologists, creating a framework to capture transducer motion, applied forces, and anatomical scanning strategies. This expert data is replayed to replicate characteristic scans with the robot, forming a foundation for further autonomous capabilities. Using stereo vision, the system generates three-dimensional topography maps of the patient's abdomen, which are refined through stiffness measurements at key points to delineate the rib cage boundary. These combined techniques enable the robot to execute two distinct scanning paths: an upward-angled sweep beneath the rib cage to visualize structures near the upper abdomen and a perpendicular sweep across soft tissue regions. A compliant, torque-controlled seven degree-of-freedom robotic manipulator is controlled to maintain consistent probe contact through closed-loop force control over the varied anatomical surfaces. Physical experiments demonstrate that the system achieves high-quality imaging comparable to expert scans while dynamically adapting to patient-specific topographies. Furthermore, the robotic system surpasses expert capabilities by enabling three-dimensional volume acquisition, which enhances diagnostic potential and provides volumetric data for advanced analyses. This work highlights the integration of expert knowledge into autonomous robotic systems and underscores the potential of combining perception-based autonomy with physical reasoning for enhanced diagnostic performance.

</details>


### [416] [LadderMan: Learning Humanoid Perceptive Ladder Climbing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05873)
*Siheng Zhao,Yuanhang Zhang,Ziqi Lu,Pieter Abbeel,Rocky Duan,Koushil Sreenath,Yue Wang,C. Karen Liu,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LadderMan 是一套能让仿人机器人鲁棒攀爬多样化梯子并执行任务的统一系统，通过两阶段学习流水线、视觉基础模型和双智能体操作策略实现了极具挑战的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在以人为中心的复杂环境中操作前景广阔，但梯子攀爬因立足点稀疏、全身协调复杂以及对感知和控制误差敏感，仍是极具挑战性的任务。

Method: 1. 两阶段学习流水线：利用混合运动跟踪学习多个爬升专家，并通过混合模仿学习和强化学习蒸馏为统一的基于深度的视觉运动爬升策略；2. 环境适应：利用视觉基础模型弥合深度知觉的仿真实体差距；3. 双智能体操作：训练独立的双智能体操作策略，实现梯上的稳定操作。

Result: 实验证明 LadderMan 在多种梯子几何结构上实现了鲁棒的攀爬，在真实硬件上实现了零样本迁移，并支持在梯子上执行各种操作任务。

Conclusion: LadderMan 提供了一种鲁棒且通用的解决方案，使得人形机器人能够在各种梯子几何形状上进行攀爬并执行操作，并成功实现了零样本 sim-to-real 迁移。

Abstract: Humanoid robots hold great promise for operating in human-centered environments, yet ladder climbing remains one of the most challenging tasks due to sparse footholds and handholds, complex whole-body coordination, and sensitivity to perception and control errors. We present \textbf{LadderMan}, a unified system that enables humanoid robots to robustly climb diverse ladders and perform manipulation under such constrained conditions. Our climbing policy is built on a scalable two-stage learning pipeline, where we use hybrid motion tracking to learn multiple climbing experts from a single reference motion, and distill these experts into a unified depth-based visuomotor climbing policy via hybrid imitation and reinforcement learning. To enable real-world deployment, we leverage vision foundation models to bridge the sim-to-real gap in depth perception. Building on the learned climbing policy, we further train a separate manipulation policy using a dual-agent formulation, allowing stable on-ladder manipulation via teleoperation. Experiments demonstrate that LadderMan achieves robust ladder climbing across a wide range of geometries, successfully transfers to real-world hardware in a zero-shot manner, and supports various manipulation tasks under challenging ladder constraints. Video results are available at https://ladderman-robot.github.io .

</details>


### [417] [TAGA: Terrain-aware Active Gaze Learning for Generalizable Agile Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05880)
*Peizhuo Li,Hongyi Li,Mingfeng Fan,Fangzhou Xu,Shuhao Liao,Yuxuan Ma,Zicheng Zeng,Ze Wang,Yongbin Jin,Yuhong Cao,Hongtao Wang,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAGA是一种基于主动凝视机制的人形机器人感知运动框架，通过强化学习使机器人自动聚焦于地形关键区域，在节省计算资源的同时，显著提升了在复杂大尺度地形下的运动能力与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在复杂地形下实现敏捷运动需克服宽视角感知与局部精细几何理解之间的矛盾，且需在有限的车载计算能力约束下实现高效感知。

Method: 提出TAGA（Terrain-aware Active Gaze）框架，结合视觉、本体感知与运动指令，通过强化学习训练模型自主学习“主动凝视”策略，即动态选择高度扫描图中的关键区域进行特征提取，而非处理全域感知信息。

Result: 所提方法在仿真及实机上表现鲁棒，实现了包括可靠落脚点选择、高台穿越、稀疏落脚点穿越及1.2米的大跨度缝隙穿越，并在感知干扰下保持稳定。

Conclusion: TAGA框架通过引入主动视觉机制，使人形机器人能在受限计算资源下实现复杂地形下的感知运动，验证了主动凝视行为可通过强化学习自发涌现，显著提升了机器人的运动稳定性和地形适应能力。

Abstract: Agile humanoid locomotion across diverse challenging terrain demands both wide perceptual coverage and precise local geometry understanding. Motivated by the way humans selectively look at relevant terrain during locomotion, we introduce TAGA, a Terrain-aware Active Gaze learning framework for Attention-based humanoid control. By fusing vision, proprioception, and motion commands, our framework guides the model to learn anticipatory cues and actively attend to specific areas of the height scan, selectively using these informative regions for the downstream network. This adaptively increases the information density of observations under tight onboard computational constraints, thus enabling fine-grained perceptive locomotion over larger-scale terrains. We find that such gaze behaviors can naturally emerge through reinforcement learning alone, without requiring additional supervision or explicit guidance, significantly improve training efficiency. As a result, the trained policy demonstrates robust and generalizable locomotion in simulation and on hardware, including reliable terrain-aware foothold selection, elevated-platform traversal, competitive sparse-foothold traversal, and the largest reported real-world gap traversal distance of 1.2m among perceptive humanoid locomotion systems, while maintaining stability under severe perceptual disturbances and environmental interference.

</details>


### [418] [A Novel Method with Encoder-Decoder for Cross-Sensor Adaptation in Surface Shape Sensing with Sparse Strain Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05903)
*Shuo Wang,Heng Luo,Dian Jin,Xiaoming Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对传感器阵列间性能不一致问题，通过元学习和少样本自适应技术，仅需极少数据和时间即可实现高精度形状传感，大幅降低了部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器阵列因自身差异或安装环境不同导致性能波动，现有的形状传感方法需要大量数据并为每个阵列单独重训练模型，导致高昂的数据采集、传输与计算成本。

Method: 提出了一种基于稀疏应变传感器的编码器-解码器架构，并将元学习（meta-learning）与少样本自适应（few-shot adaptation）策略集成，使模型能够快速适配不同的传感器阵列。

Result: 在仅使用少于5%新标注数据的情况下，模型在1秒内快速适配，使新传感器阵列的传感误差从23.0 mm显著降低至约4.0 mm，误差小于5.0 mm的采样点数提升了65%以上。

Conclusion: 所提出的元学习与少样本自适应方法能够有效解决传感器阵列间的性能差异问题，显著降低了跨阵列部署的成本和训练负担，在软体机器人和可穿戴设备领域具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Performance variations in sensor arrays, caused by intrinsic differences or installation conditions, can lead to inconsistent results during shape sensing. To obtain accurate results, a large amount of data is usually required, and a separate model must be retrained for each sensor array, thereby increasing the cost and time of data acquisition, transmission, and computation. To address this issue, this work proposes an encoder-decoder architecture for surface shape sensing based on sparse strain sensors and further incorporates meta-learning and few-shot adaptation strategies to enable adaptation across different groups of sensor arrays. Experimental results demonstrate that, after the cross-sensor adaptation, a newly deployed sensor array achieves a sensing error of approximately 4.0 mm relying on less than 5.0% newly labeled data and requiring an adaptation time of under 1 second, which represents a substantial improvement from 23.0 mm error without adaptation and 20-minute data collection time required to train a new model. Moreover, the number of points with errors below 5.0 mm increased by more than 65.0%. These results indicate that the proposed method can substantially reduce the cost and training burden of surface shape sensing, and it has broad potential applications in soft robotics and wearable devices.

</details>


### [419] [Learning of Robot Safety Policies via Adversarial Synthetic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05952)
*Nikolai Dorofeev,Alexey Odinokov,Rostislav Yavorskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于红蓝对抗代理的机器人安全强化学习框架，通过自动化生成复杂危险场景，实现了安全策略的高效演进，解决了传统方法难以覆盖高风险边缘工况的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机模拟或人工枚举无法有效覆盖机器人安全策略中的高风险边缘工况（edge cases），需要一种更高效、自动化的方法来生成危险场景并训练安全策略。

Method: 构建“代理博弈”框架：红队代理（Red Team）负责生成危险场景以探索潜在失效空间，蓝队代理（Blue Team）负责根据这些场景迭代优化安全策略。该方法结合了传统风险建模与现代对抗性学习。

Result: 提出了一种创新的问题定义及相应的系统架构，该方法能够高效发现并解决难以通过常规手段获取的危险场景，增强了物理AI系统的安全性。

Conclusion: 引入了一种基于红蓝对抗代理的框架，通过生成危险场景来迭代优化机器人安全策略，为实现复杂环境下的机器人安全性提供了一种可扩展的解决方案架构。

Abstract: In this work, we propose an agentic gamification framework for hazard-informed learning of robot safety policies through synthetic scenarios. We model scenario generation as an adversarial game between two agents: a Red Team that explores the space of potential failures by constructing hazardous situations, and a Blue Team that incrementally refines safety policies to prevent them. This iterative process enables efficient discovery of high-risk edge cases that are unlikely to be captured through random simulation or manual enumeration. By combining classical risk modeling with adversarial scenario generation and modern learning paradigms, this work provides a scalable pathway for embedding safety into Physical AI systems operating in complex real-world environments. The paper describes ongoing work. The contribution is a problem formulation and a proposed solution architecture.

</details>


### [420] [Towards a Data Flywheel for Embodied Intelligence in Logistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05960)
*Anlan Yu,Zaishu Chen,Zhiqing Hong,Daqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对物流具身智能，本文提出了一种数据驱动的“飞轮”框架，利用世界模型（WM-DAgger）合成鲁棒性数据，实现了从日常操作到策略优化的闭环管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能在物流领域的工业部署需要克服数据收集、组织和复用的规模化挑战，现有的感知-规划-控制流水线难以应对复杂多变的工业环境。

Method: 构建以数据为中心的物流数据飞轮（Logistics Data Flywheel）：1.将日常操作转化为可重用资产；2.利用世界模型（World Models）生成长尾包裹操纵的可靠监督信号；3.提出 WM-DAgger，通过合成分布外（OOD）恢复数据提升模仿学习的鲁棒性。

Result: 成功引入 WM-DAgger 框架，通过合成 OOD 恢复数据增强了鲁棒模仿学习，并建立了多模态数据（人类演示、视频、机器人日志）对齐与持续学习的反馈机制。

Conclusion: 通过构建物流数据飞轮，利用世界模型和数据增强技术，可以有效解决具身智能（特别是物流领域）中长尾操纵任务的数据稀缺与泛化难题，推动工业部署的规模化。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence is moving from laboratory demonstrations toward industrial deployment, with the logistics industry serving as a key application scenario. Learning-based policies offer a promising path beyond traditional perception-planning-control pipelines, but their scalability depends on how embodied data can be collected, organized, and reused. This research studies a data-centric framework for industrial embodied intelligence by constructing a logistics data flywheel. Our framework converts daily operations into reusable data assets, uses World Models to generate reliable supervision for long-tail parcel manipulation, and feeds deployment feedback back into policy improvement. As an initial result, \textit{WM-DAgger} introduces a World-Model-based data aggregation framework that synthesizes out-of-distribution recovery data for robust imitation learning. Building on this result, ongoing work explores how large-scale in-the-wild multimodal data, including labeled human demonstrations, unlabeled operational videos, and system-level robot logs, can be aligned for policy learning and transformed into feedback for continual system improvement.

</details>


### [421] [World-Language-Action Model for Unified World Modeling, Language Reasoning, and Action Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05979)
*Yi Yang,Zhihong Liu,Siqi Kou,Yiyang Chen,Yanzhe Hu,Jianbo Zhou,Boyuan Zhao,Zhijie Wei,Xiao Xia,Xueqi Li,Pengfei Liu,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WLA 是一种结合了世界模型与视觉-语言-动作模型的具身基础模型，通过自回归 Transformer 预测物理状态与动作，提升了机器人处理长视距任务的能力，并能从无标注视频中直接学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在打破现有模型在“大规模视频学习（如 WAM）”与“长视距指令执行（如 VLA）”之间的壁垒，构建一种既具备世界建模能力以理解环境，又具备语言推理能力以完成复杂任务的统一具身基础模型。

Method: 提出 WLA（世界-语言-动作）模型，采用自回归（AR）Transformer 架构。核心模块包括：1. 世界专家（World Expert），基于世界建模目标预测语义意图与物理动态；2. 动作专家（Action Expert），利用世界预测提供的物理特征进行决策；3. 采用元查询（meta-queries）机制隐式连接世界建模与动作生成，支持推理时的测试时间扩展（test-time scaling）。

Result: WLA-0 原型机（20 亿活跃参数）在 NVIDIA RTX 5090 上推理速度达 40 ms。在 RoboTwin2.0 Clean 环境下达到 92.94% 成功率，RMBench 环境下达到 56.5%，均达到当前领先水平（SOTA）。

Conclusion: WLA 模型通过结合语言推理能力与世界建模，实现了高效的多任务和长视距机器人控制。其不仅在仿真和真实场景中达到 SOTA，还展示了从无动作标注的跨实体视频中学习新任务的潜力，展现了具身智能的扩展性。

Abstract: We propose world-language-action (WLA) models as a new class of embodied foundation models. WLA takes textual instructions, images, and robot states as inputs to jointly predict textual subtasks, subgoal images, and robot actions, conjoining the \emph{world modeling interface} to learn from extensive egocentric videos as in the world-action model (WAM) and the \emph{language reasoning} capacities to solve complex long-horizon tasks as in vision-language-action (VLA) models. At the core of WLA lies an \emph{autoregressive (AR)} Transformer backbone, instead of a bidirectional diffusion Transformer as in WAMs, to predict the \emph{next state}, comprising the \emph{semantic-level} textual intention and complementary \emph{fine-grained} physical dynamics. The physical dynamics are supervised by the world modeling objective based on a dedicated World Expert, and are leveraged to ease the characterization of the state-action correlation for the Action Expert. WLA leverages meta-queries to make the world prediction \emph{implicitly} impact the action generation so that the former can be disabled during inference. The world prediction can also be activated to enable test-time scaling for improved robot control. Our WLA-0 prototype, with 2B active parameters, achieves 40 ms per inference on an NVIDIA RTX 5090. Evaluations across simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that WLA-0 achieves state-of-the-art multi-task and long-horizon learning abilities, e.g., 92.94\% success rate on RoboTwin2.0 Clean and 56.5\% success rate on RMBench. WLA-0 also holds the promise to learn novel tasks directly from \emph{cross-embodiment robot videos} without action annotations.

</details>


### [422] [Merging model-based control with multi-agent reinforcement learning for multi-agent cooperative teaming strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06011)
*Christian Llanes,Spencer W. Jensen,Samuel Coogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合多智能体强化学习（MARL）与模型预测控制（MPC）的混合框架 MA-AC-MPC，通过整合两者的优势，实现了多智能体系统在复杂任务中更安全、更具鲁棒性的协同决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决多智能体协同任务中存在的挑战：既需要 MARL 处理复杂的长程协作策略，又需要模型预测控制（MPC）确保动作的安全性与动态可行性。

Method: 提出了多智能体执行者-评论者模型预测控制（MA-AC-MPC）算法，该方法将多智能体强化学习（MARL）处理长视界协作策略学习的能力与模型预测控制（MPC）在短视界内实现动态可行性和安全性的优势相结合。

Result: 在追逃场景和异构系统（无人机与轮式机器人）协作降落实验中，MA-AC-MPC 的成功率（100%）显著优于基准方法 MA-AC-MLP（60%），并成功在实体硬件上进行了部署验证。

Conclusion: MA-AC-MPC 算法在多智能体协同任务中表现出比纯神经网络架构（MA-AC-MLP）更高的成功率、更好的动态可行性和安全性，且在异构机器人硬件实验中证明了其鲁棒性和实际部署潜力。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a framework that combines multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) with model-based control to achieve safe, dynamically feasible actions in cooperative multi-agent tasks. Multi-agent reinforcement learning provides the advantage of learning cooperative policies for multi-agent teams from discrete non-differentiable rewards in a long planning horizon. Model-predictive control is robust and offers safe, dynamically feasible actions in a fast replanning framework for short horizons. We propose an algorithm that extends actor-critic model predictive control for MARL which we refer to as multi-agent actor-critic model predictive control (MA-AC-MPC). We demonstrate the capabilities of this algorithm by applying it to a multi-agent pursuit-evasion scenario. Specifically, we compare the evader team's strategy using the MA-AC-MPC model and a multi-layer perceptron model (MA-AC-MLP). The pursuer team uses augmented proportional navigation as it is accepted as an advanced adversarial control law. We also provide an example with a heterogeneous environment where a drone and omni-wheeled rover cooperate to achieve repeatable and successful landing with 100% success rate in hardware for MA-AC-MPC compared to 60% for MA-AC-MLP. We demonstrate the robustness of the proposed MA-AC-MPC algorithm in hardware for both environments.

</details>


### [423] [RealDexUMI: A Wearable Universal Manipulation Interface for Dexterous Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06033)
*Chaoyi Xu,Yixuan Jiang,Jiahui Huan,Yuhui Fu,Haoyu Zhou,Weitian Yuan,Jiayi Yu,Wanpeng Zhang,Haoqi Yuan,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RealDexUMI提供了一种新型灵巧操作数据采集框架，通过同构遥操作手套和共享传感硬件，消除了机器人学习中数据采集与执行的差异，大幅提升了复杂 manipulation 任务的训练效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的灵巧操作数据采集方法存在局限性：重定向（retargeting）或具身转换导致操作灵活性丢失；传统的机器人专用遥操作设备成本高、扩展性差且缺乏细腻的接触感知。现有流水线难以平衡“操作精细度”与“部署可执行性”。

Method: 提出RealDexUMI系统，核心包括：1. 共享灵巧末端执行器模块（集成轻量化手、手内视觉及指尖触觉传感）；2. 掌侧同构遥操作手套，将人类手工输入直接映射到机器人关节指令；3. 实现数据采集端与部署端在观测、触觉及动作上的完全对称和零差距。

Result: 在8项真实的机器人操作任务（涵盖精细、接触密集、长周期及双臂操作）中，RealDexUMI训练出的策略平均成功率达到88.75%，具备了良好的初始位姿泛化能力，并成功在三种不同的机器人实施例上实现了迁移。

Conclusion: RealDexUMI通过物理同构的遥操作接口实现了数据采集与部署端的零差距（Zero-gap）对齐，为解决灵巧操作任务中动作映射、触觉与视觉感知失配问题提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案，且具有良好的跨实施例迁移能力。

Abstract: Learning dexterous manipulation requires demonstrations that preserve fine hand-object interactions while remaining executable at deployment. Existing pipelines either lose deployable dexterity through retargeting or embodiment conversion, or rely on robot-specific teleoperation that is costly to scale and often lacks intuitive, contact-aware control for dexterous data collection. We present RealDexUMI, a wearable universal manipulation interface built around a shared dexterous end-effector module that integrates a lightweight dexterous hand, in-hand vision, and fingertip tactile sensing. A palm-side isomorphic teleoperation glove maps human finger inputs to robot-hand joint commands, enabling real-time, retargeting-free, intuitive, and precise hand control. The shared hand and sensing modules yield zero-gap end-effector data, with matched in-hand observations, tactile signals, contacts, and hand actions between collection and deployment. Across eight real-robot tasks spanning fine-grained, contact-rich, long-horizon, and bimanual manipulation, policies trained on RealDexUMI data achieve an average success rate of 88.75%, generalize to unseen initial poses, and transfer across three embodiments. Website: https://research.beingbeyond.com/realdexumi

</details>


### [424] [Gotta Grow Fast: Design and Benchmarking of a Tip Mount for High-Speed Vine Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06040)
*Antonio Alvarez Valdivia,Robert Reeve,Ankush Dhawan,Ciera McFarland,Chad Council,Margaret McGuinness,Nathaniel Hanson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三角形滚轮尖端安装座，有效降低了藤蔓机器人生长过程中的内部摩擦，提升了其携带传感器后的生长稳定性和重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 藤蔓机器人通过尖端外翻（tip eversion）进行生长，这种特殊机制使得在尖端安装相机等传感器因材料更替、摩擦力及重量限制而极具挑战性，阻碍了其在复杂环境下的侦测与搜索任务。

Method: 1. 设计了一种三角形滚子尖端挂载机构，将滑动摩擦转换为滚动摩擦；2. 进行了迭代式故障分析以优化设计；3. 开发了专门的测试台架，通过测量尾部张力来量化不同挂载方案的生长性能。

Result: 实验表明，三角形滚轮安装座显著降低了尾部张力，在TPU涂层尼龙材料上实现了高度可重复的生长性能，其表现优于现有设计。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于三角形滚轮结构的藤蔓机器人尖端安装方案，该设计通过减少内部阻力实现了更稳定的生长，并为后续软体机器人的传感器集成提供了一个可量化的基准测试框架。

Abstract: Soft, growing vine robots extend through tip eversion, a mechanism that enables navigation through cluttered environments. However, integrating cameras and other sensors at the tip is uniquely challenging because the material forming the tip is constantly renewed as the robot grows. This continual material turnover, combined with friction between internal layers, added tip weight, and fabric constriction, complicates sensor and tool mounting. These limitations hinder the deployment of vine robots for inspection and search tasks, where rapid growth while carrying tip-mounted sensors is essential. In this work, we present a triangular roller tip mount that reduces internal resistance during growth by rolling rather than sliding against the robot body. The design was refined through iterative failure analysis, enabling, for the first time, consistent eversion on a TPU-coated ripstop nylon vine robot. To quantitatively evaluate mount performance, we introduce a custom testbed that isolates tip mounting effects by measuring tail tension during eversion. Comparative experiments across multiple mount variants, including prior designs, show that our triangular roller mount achieves the lowest tail tension and most repeatable growth performance. These results establish both a validated tip mount design and a repeatable benchmarking framework for advancing sensor and tool integration in soft growing robots. CAD for the mount and testbed is available at: https://sprout-mitll.github.io/tip_mounts/.

</details>


### [425] [Sample-efficient Low-level Motion Planning for Robotic Manipulation Tasks via Zero-shot Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06041)
*Yuanzhi He,Victor Romero-Cano,José J. Patiño,Juan David Hernández,William Sawtell,Gualtiero Colombo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对复杂机器人任务，提出了一种结合迁移学习和奖励重构的增强型iCEM算法（iCEM+TL），显著提升了复杂操控（如堆叠）的成功率与部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统iCEM算法在处理堆叠、滑动等复杂机器人操控任务时表现受限，且计算复杂度和训练时间需求日益增加。

Method: 提出了iCEM+TL框架，将简单上游任务的iCEM参数迁移至复杂下游任务；同时引入奖励重构（RR）策略，通过任务分解优化特定任务表现。

Result: 仿真实验表明成功率最高提升了23%，并在Franka Emika机械臂的堆叠任务实机测试中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架将迁移学习引入iCEM，通过跨任务参数复用及奖励重构有效提升了复杂操控任务（如堆叠、放置）的成功率，并被证明具有良好的现实部署可行性。

Abstract: As robotic systems become more sophisticated, the growing complexity of their motion planning models and the longer training times pose substantial challenges. Evolutionary algorithms such as the Sample-efficient Cross-Entropy Method (iCEM) have recently demonstrated promising potential for low-level real-time planning by leveraging efficient knowledge reuse strategies to improve performance. Although effective in many control tasks, iCEM's performance can be constrained in more complex scenarios, particularly those requiring stacking, sliding, and shelf placement. In this work, we propose a novel iCEM+TL framework that explicitly leverages Transfer Learning (TL), where key iCEM parameters are transferred from simpler upstream tasks to guide more complex downstream tasks. Additionally, we applied Reward Redesign (RR) through task decomposition for stacking objects and shelf placement to optimize task-specific performance. Results from the simulation show that our framework achieves success rate improvements of up to 23%. The framework is further validated on a real Franka Emika robot in a stacking task, demonstrating its practical feasibility for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [426] [L-SDPPO: Policy Optimization of Spiking Diffusion Policy for Intra-vehicular Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06049)
*Liwen Zhang,Dong Zhou,Guanghui Sun,Yifei Zheng,Yuhui Hu,Kaihong Ouyang,Zuoquan Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对航天器舱内机器人操作中微重力漂移挑战与扩散策略高能耗的问题，本文提出了基于脉冲神经网络与状态依赖延迟注入机制的 L-SDPPO 框架，在提升任务成功率的同时显著降低了能量消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作研究在航天器内部面临微重力环境下物体无阻尼漂移的挑战，且传统的扩散策略（DP）在采样时计算能耗过高，无法满足航天器严苛的能源限制。

Method: 1. 构建了脉冲扩散策略（SDP），通过脉冲神经网络优化扩散模型的采样过程以降低能耗；2. 结合强化学习（RL）算法对策略进行系统训练；3. 提出状态依赖延迟注入（SDLI）机制，模拟生物神经延迟以处理微重力环境下的动态时空特征感知。

Result: 在五项典型的舱内日常任务（如舱门开启、精密容器封盖）测试中，L-SDPPO 在成功率和能效比方面均优于现有最先进的机器人操作方法。

Conclusion: L-SDPPO 框架通过结合脉冲扩散策略与状态依赖延迟注入机制，为航天器内机器人操作提供了一种既能适应复杂多模态动作需求，又具备高能效和稳健性的创新方案，验证了其在微重力环境下的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Intra-vehicular robots in spacecraft help reduce astronaut workload and improve mission efficiency. Recent research focuses on using deep learning methods to achieve the acute control required for operations in these complex environments. However, objects exhibit unpredictable, unconstrained drift without gravitational damping. These factors demand robustness against complex multimodal action distributions. Diffusion policies (DP) can model these complex actions, but their iterative sampling process consumes too much energy for the limited power budgets of spacecraft. We therefore propose a low-energy intra-vehicular robotic manipulation framework, L-SDPPO, in which the Spiking Diffusion Policy (SDP) is optimized with a reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm. Furthermore, to address the insufficient perception of dynamic spatiotemporal features in microgravity, we propose the statedependent latency injection (SDLI) mechanism, which mimics biological neural delays to dynamically regulate the timing of input information. Evaluation on five representative intra-vehicular daily tasks (e.g., hatch opening and precision container capping) shows that our method consistently achieves higher success rates and lower energy consumption, compared to the state-of-the-art robotic manipulation methods. These results demonstrate our method is a viable intra-vehicular robotic manipulation method.

</details>


### [427] [A Conversational Framework for Human-Robot Collaborative Manipulation with Distributed Generative AI models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06061)
*Arash Ghasemzadeh Kakroudi,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于ROS 2的分布式人机协作框架，通过大模型（VLM/LLM）实现从自然语言到机器人操纵指令的转化，并引入了可视化的安全反馈机制，有效提升了复杂场景下机器人操纵的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人机协作中由于任务场景复杂性及自然语言指令模糊性导致的执行需求，旨在实现一个灵活、可扩展且具备高安全性的机器人操纵系统。

Method: 构建了一个基于ROS 2的分布式框架，将语言理解、视觉定位（VLM）、编排与运动执行解耦为独立节点；通过VLM实现图像空间目标检测，并结合深度信息与标定转换为机器人坐标系目标；引入Web仪表盘进行意图可视化与人工确认。

Result: 在Franka FR3平台上验证了系统的端到端任务可靠性与低延迟表现，并通过对比实验评估了不同LLM/VLM配置在多类场景下的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于分布式ROS 2的对话框架在处理人机协作操纵任务中的有效性和响应性，通过引入显式确认机制平衡了任务自动化与操作安全性。

Abstract: This paper presents a distributed conversational framework for human-robot collaborative manipulation that integrates local language and vision-language models (VLMs) with a Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2)-based execution stack. Language understanding, visual grounding, orchestration, and motion execution run as separate ROS 2 nodes, enabling flexible deployment across distributed hardware while maintaining a responsive control loop. From free-form user commands, the system generates structured action requests for pick, place, and handover. It uses a VLM to return image-space targets, which are converted into metric robot-frame goals using depth and calibration. A web dashboard exposes intermediate intent and grounding overlays (pixel, depth, and robot-frame) and requires explicit operator confirmation before any motion is executed. Experiments on a Franka FR3 platform evaluate end-to-end task reliability and latency under increasing working table scene ambiguity and compare alternative LLM/VLM configurations in the same pipeline. Code and full documentation are available at [github.com/cogrob-tuni/franka-llm](https://github.com/cogrob-tuni/franka-llm).

</details>


### [428] [3D Underwater Path Planning via Generative Flow Field Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06077)
*Zachary Cooper-Baldock,Paulo E. Santos,Russell S. A. Brinkworth,Karl Sammut*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出了一种利用cGAN替代高昂CFD计算的方法，实现了AUV在复杂舰船尾流环境下的实时高效路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV在母船推进器尾流区域进行回收时，传统的均匀流场模型精度不足，而高保真CFD计算成本过高，无法在航行器机载设备上实现实时路径规划。

Method: 提出了一个分层生成流水线，利用条件生成对抗网络（cGAN，包括PatchGAN和带有自注意力的2D3DGAN）作为RANS CFD数据的替代 surrogate 模型；将预测的$128^3$体素流场集成到三维能量加权A*路径规划框架中进行路径优化。

Result: cGAN surrogate 模型在推理速度上达到微秒级（28-146 μs），比传统CFD快几个数量级；在规划性能上，相较于简单的均匀流模型，cGAN方案显著降低了能耗并减少了穿越尾流核心的概率（回收了Ground-truth CFD 45-60%的优化潜力）。

Conclusion: 研究证明了基于cGAN的流场预测模型可作为高保真CFD的有效替代方案，在航拍机器人边缘设备上实现实时轨迹规划的同时，能够回收约45-60%的复杂水动力环境下节能和避险效益，验证了深度生成模型在水下航行器动态避障任务中的实用价值。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) launch and recovery (LAR) into the hull of an advancing host platform requires traversal of a complex, three-dimensional propeller wake whose hydrodynamic structure cannot be characterised by a uniform current model. High-fidelity Reynolds-Averaged Navier-Stokes (RANS) Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) simulations resolve this structure with sufficient accuracy for path planning, but their computational cost renders them impractical for onboard use. We address this gap by integrating two conditional generative adversarial network (cGAN) architectures -- a regularised PatchGAN and a 2D3DGAN with self-attention -- as drop-in replacements for RANS CFD data within a three-dimensional, energy-weighted A* path planning framework. Both generators are driven by a hierarchical pipeline that synthesises full $128^3$ voxel flow field volumes from scalar operating condition inputs alone, with end-to-end inference times of approximately 28-146 $μ$s, compared to hours for a single RANS computation. We benchmark all four environmental knowledge levels: uniform current, ground-truth CFD, PatchGAN, and 2D3DGAN~SA across 19,800 independently generated trajectories spanning 550 distinct flow conditions. Full CFD wake knowledge reduces energy expenditure by 5.7-12.5% and high-velocity wake-core encounters by up to 77.8% relative to uniform-current planning, with both benefits scaling with operating severity. The cGAN surrogates recover approximately 45-60% of the CFD energy benefit and high-velocity cell avoidance benefit while operating at inference speeds compatible with edge device use. These results provide the first systematic quantification of the downstream path planning value of cGAN-predicted hydrodynamic fields in a three-dimensional maritime robotics application.

</details>


### [429] [Towards Realistic 3D Sonar Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06130)
*Youssef Attia,Davide Costa,Francesco Wanderlingh,Filippo Campagnaro,Enrico Simetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 中集成物理声学传播的 3D 声纳模拟框架，以解决现有几何驱动模拟在复杂水下环境感知中的不足，并通过实验验证了其在 SLAM 任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的几何驱动声纳模拟无法处理折射、多径干扰和相位相关信号等关键声学现象，限制了水下机器人复杂 3D 感知和自主导航算法的开发。

Method: 在 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 环境中集成 GPU 加速图形引擎与基于物理声学传播原理的体积 3D 声纳模型（以 Water Linked 3D-15 为原型），并结合硬件在环（HIL）配置运行改进的 FastLIO2 SLAM 管道。

Result: 成功构建了模拟框架，通过 HIL 配置验证了传感器融合（合成声纳、DVL、IMU 和压力数据），并对比了模拟输出与海港实测数据，分析了仿真到真实的差距。

Conclusion: 提出了一个能够弥补现有模拟框架缺陷的模块化架构，并为实现声学与体积感知相结合的真实感水下模拟奠定了路线图。

Abstract: As underwater robotics research increasingly addresses complex 3D perception and autonomous navigation, the fidelity of sonar simulation has become a key factor in algorithm development. Current simulation frameworks typically rely on geometry-driven rendering, approximating 3D sonar as an underwater equivalent to LiDAR, which fails to account for fundamental acoustic phenomena such as refraction, multi-path interference, and phase-dependent signal formation. This paper proposes a modular architecture for realistic 3D sonar simulation that integrates GPU-accelerated graphics engines with physically grounded acoustic propagation principles. We implement a volumetric 3D sonar model within the NVIDIA Isaac Sim environment, modeled after the Water Linked 3D-15 sensor, and integrate it into a comprehensive underwater simulation framework. The system is validated through a hardware-in-the-loop configuration, where a modified FastLIO2 SLAM pipeline, executed on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano, performs sensor fusion using synthetic 3D sonar, DVL, IMU, and pressure data. Finally, a qualitative comparison between simulated outputs and real-world data from harbor sheet-pile inspections is provided, characterizing the remaining sim-to-real gap and establishing a roadmap toward fully acoustics-driven volumetric sensing.

</details>


### [430] [MotionDisco: Motion Discovery for Extreme Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06139)
*Ilyass Taouil,Michal Ciebelski,Shafeef Omar,Haizhou Zhao,Angela Dai,Aaron M. Johnson,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionDisco是一个无需人类演示，利用LLM引导的进化搜索与轨迹优化自动发现人形机器人复杂长周期移动操纵技能的框架，并已成功在真实硬件上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决长周期人形机器人运动操纵任务中接触交互组合空间过大、依赖人类遥操作或动作重定向数据导致开发效率低、普适性差的问题。

Method: 结合大语言模型(LLM)引导的进化搜索(exploration)与高效的序列运动动力学轨迹优化(trajectory optimization)；引入剪枝策略以应对组合爆炸问题；最后利用强化学习训练跟踪策略，将生成的轨迹迁移至实体人形机器人。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够生成多个具有挑战性的长周期全身运动轨迹，并成功在真实人形机器人上部署，是首个完全通过自动化进化搜索实现长周期移动操纵技能发现与部署的工作。

Conclusion: MotionDisco成功实现了在无需人类演示的情况下，自动化发现并部署复杂的长周期人形机器人运动操纵技能，证实了“LLM引导进化搜索+轨迹优化”范式在人形机器人控制任务中的有效性。

Abstract: We present MotionDisco, a framework that discovers contact-rich, long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation motions from scratch, without relying on teleoperation or motion retargeting from human demonstrations. This is challenging because the space of possible contact interactions grows combinatorially with the task horizon and the number of objects in the scene. MotionDisco enables rapid discovery of novel motions by coupling a large language model (LLM) guided evolutionary search over sequences of interactions with an efficient sequential kinodynamic trajectory optimizer and pruning strategy, enabling the rapid discovery of novel skills. Through extensive ablation studies, we show that our LLM-guided search discovers successful whole-body trajectories across several challenging long-horizon tasks. Finally, by training reinforcement learning tracking policies on the discovered trajectories, we transfer the motions to a real humanoid robot. This is the first work to discover and deploy long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation skills entirely through automated evolutionary search. Supplementary videos of the experiments are available at: https://youtu.be/DHiVz34QYlw.

</details>


### [431] [AffordanceVLA: A Vision-Language-Action Model Empowering Action Generation through Affordance-Aware Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06155)
*Qize Yu,Jiadi You,Yuran Wang,Jiaqi Liang,Bowen Ping,Yang Tian,Yue Chen,Minghong Cai,Zeying Gong,Ruihai Wu,Yinchuan Li,Junwei Liang,Yingcong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AffordanceVLA 通过引入结构化的“可供性推理”（Which/Where/How2Act）作为中间表示，解决了VLA模型在语义空间与控制策略之间的映射难题，显著增强了机器人的操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）与具身控制策略在结构上存在不匹配，导致机器人在精细操作映射学习中表现受限，难以实现准确的感知动作映射。

Method: 1. 提出了 AffordanceVLA 框架，包含 Which2Act（目标定位）、Where2Act（2D交互定位）和 How2Act（3D几何推理）三个模块；2. 使用 Mixture-of-Transformer (MoT) 架构；3. 采用三阶段训练策略及数据课程学习；4. 开发了自动化数据增强流程以解决密集的标注数据短缺问题。

Result: AffordanceVLA 在仿真和真实环境的多种机器人操作任务中表现优异，验证了该方法在提供空间扎根、语义条件化与动作耦合中间表示方面的有效性。

Conclusion: AffordanceVLA 框架通过引入结构化可供性预测作为中间表示，有效解决了视觉-动作（V-A）映射中的语义与空间不匹配问题，显著提升了机器人操作任务的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models leverage the rich world knowledge of pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) to enable instruction-following robotic manipulation. However, the structural mismatch between VLM semantic spaces and embodied control policies often hinders the learning of precise perception--action mappings. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{AffordanceVLA}, a unified framework that introduces structured affordance forecasting as a task-oriented intermediate representation to establish a more precise and robust perception--action mapping. Specifically, we progressively model manipulation priors through three complementary components: 1) \textbf{Which2Act} for object-centric grounding via visual latent prediction to suppress distractions; 2) \textbf{Where2Act} for 2D interaction localization via affordance map estimation; and 3) \textbf{How2Act} for 3D geometric reasoning to guide manipulation policies. These affordance cues provide spatially grounded, semantically conditioned, and action-coupled intermediate representations, thereby naturally bridging vision, language and action. We integrate these modules into a Mixture-of-Transformer (MoT) architecture with specialized experts and train the model using a three-stage training strategy with a progressive data curriculum. To overcome the scarcity of dense affordance labels in robotic datasets, we also develop a robust automated data augmentation pipeline. Extensive experiments on simulation and real-world demonstrate that AffordanceVLA achieves strong performance across diverse manipulation scenarios.

</details>


### [432] [ActiveMimic: Egocentric Video Pretraining with Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06194)
*Xingyao Lin,Guojin Zhong,Tianyi Lu,Ziyi Ye,Yichen Zhu,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActiveMimic 框架首次将人体视频中的“主动感知行为”显式建模为视点动作，有效提升了机器人在复杂操作任务中的表现，使自我中心人体视频成为机器人预训练的高效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前利用自我中心（egocentric）视频进行机器人预训练的效果普遍不如直接使用机器人数据，原因是现有方法忽略了人体在操作过程中的“主动感知行为”（即持续调整视角的相机运动）。

Method: 提出 ActiveMimic 框架：1) 从单目佩戴 RGB 视频中恢复同步的相机和手部轨迹；2) 将相机运动建模为一种“视点动作”（viewpoint action）；3) 联合学习主动感知策略与操作技能，并迁移至机器人系统。

Result: 在多项具有不同主动感知需求的任务中，ActiveMimic 的表现一致优于基准方法，并达到了与使用机器人数据预训练的模型相当的水平。

Conclusion: 主动感知（Active Perception）是解锁自我中心人体视频用于机器人预训练的关键。通过将视角运动建模为一种动作，ActiveMimic 成功缩小了人体视频与机器人数据之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Egocentric human video offers a scalable alternative to robot data for pretraining, yet models pretrained on such video consistently underperform those pretrained on robot data. We attribute this gap to a missing signal, the active perception behavior in egocentric videos, where humans continuously reposition their viewpoint during manipulation, inducing camera motion that standard pipelines treat as noise. To address this, we present ActiveMimic, a pretraining framework that recovers synchronized camera and wrist trajectories from a single body-worn RGB camera, models camera motion as a viewpoint action, and jointly learns active perception and manipulation from in-the-wild egocentric human video before adapting to a target robot. Empirically, real-world experiments across tasks with diverse active perception demands show that ActiveMimic consistently surpasses baselines pretrained on human video and matches state-of-the-art models pretrained on robot data. Further analysis provides evidence that active perception capability originates from egocentric human video pretraining rather than robot-specific fine-tuning, confirming active perception as the key to unlocking egocentric human video for robot pretraining.

</details>


### [433] [TAM: Torque Adaptation Module for Robust Motion Transfer in Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06218)
*Dongwon Son,Florian Shkurti,Jason Lee,Naman Shah,Beomjoon Kim,Dieter Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 TAM 的扭矩适配模块，通过在线修正扭矩指令，解决了机器人动力学不确定性导致的控制失效问题，实现了无需真实数据即可在多种任务与机器人硬件上进行的零样本适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对机器人技术中普遍存在的动力学不匹配问题（如 sim-to-real 差距、负载变化、硬件个体差异），现有的域随机化或系统辨识方法要么导致策略过于保守，要么需要繁琐的重采样，难以在接触丰富的动态操作任务中保持高效控制。

Method: 提出 Torque Adaptation Module (TAM)，作为一个位于低级控制器与执行器之间、独立于任务策略的模块。它利用历史本体感知（proprioceptive history）输入，通过编码器与残差扭矩预测，实时修正指令以补偿动力学差异。TAM 采用多机器人随机模拟预训练加针对性微调的训练策略。

Result: 在 Franka Panda 机器人上对视觉驱动推箱子、翻转动作及 MPC 平衡球等任务进行了验证。实验表明，TAM 在零样本执行上优于传统的在线系统辨识和 RMA 基线，具有更强的跨平台泛化与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TAM 为机器人动力学不匹配问题提供了一种通用、零样本的解决方案，通过在模拟中进行迁移学习，无需真实机器人数据即可显著增强复杂动力学任务在不同硬件平台上的鲁棒性。

Abstract: A policy tuned for one robot often behaves differently on another, whether due to the sim-to-real gap, unknown payloads, or the differing dynamics of two instances of the same robot. In contact-rich, dynamic manipulation, even small motion discrepancies can result in failure to track reference motion, since they disrupt the timing and modes of contact. Common remedies, such as domain randomization or system identification, either produce overly conservative task policies or require data that must be recollected for each robot or payload. We introduce the Torque Adaptation Module (TAM), a learned module that adapts the torque commands sent to the robot to match the behavior of an ideal robot. TAM operates between the low-level controller that tracks the policy's actions and the robot's torque interface. It includes a history encoder that embeds proprioceptive history into a latent state and a torque adaptor that computes residual torque corrections. Because TAM depends only on proprioceptive history and not on policy observations, or the action space, the same TAM weights can be reused to adapt policies with different action spaces (joint targets, end-effector targets, or direct torques). The policies themselves do not need to be trained with domain randomization of robot parameters. Instead, we offload the need for domain randomization to TAM by training it entirely in randomized simulation, using multi-robot pretraining followed by a robot-specific fine-tuning step that still requires no real-robot data. We evaluate TAM zero-shot on a real Franka Panda robot across dynamic manipulation tasks that include a vision-based box pushing policy (from RL), a flip policy (from BC), and an MPC ball-on-plate balancing. Our experiments show that TAM improves zero-shot real-robot execution compared to online system identification and RMA baselines and enables robust dynamic manipulation performance.

</details>


### [434] [CLEAR: Cognition and Latent Evaluation for Adaptive Routing in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06219)
*Yining Xing,Zehong Ke,Zhiyuan Liu,Yanbo Jiang,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CLEAR 框架，通过引入单步条件漂移和基于轻量级大语言模型的语义感知调度，解决了扩散模型在自动驾驶中推理延迟高的问题，实现了兼顾高性能与实时性的多模态规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型在多模态轨迹生成的多样性与实时推理的低延迟要求之间存在矛盾，传统扩散模型的迭代去噪机制导致推理延迟过高，不适用于安全关键型场景。

Method: 1. 视觉特征提取：使用 Drive-JEPA 作为视觉编码器。
2. 轨迹生成：将多步去噪扩散过程替换为 VAE 潜在空间中的单步条件漂移（Conditional Drift），通过调节系数平衡多样性与精度。
3. 语义感知：微调 Qwen-3.5-0.8B 模型提取场景感知隐状态。
4. 自适应决策：利用感知隐状态驱动自适应调度器（Adaptive Scheduler）动态调整采样策略，并使用跨注意力评分器（Cross-attention Scorer）优选轨迹。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1 基准测试上取得了 93.7 的 PDMS（规划多样性与意义得分），表现优于现有最优模型。

Conclusion: CLEAR 框架证明了无需复杂的几何标注或繁琐的迭代采样，即可实现高效、高保真的多模态自动驾驶规划，在 NAVSIM v1 基准测试中实现了 SOTA 水平。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models often struggle to balance multi-modal maneuver generation with real-time inference constraints. While diffusion models successfully capture diverse driving behaviors, their iterative denoising process incurs unacceptable latency for safety-critical deployment. To address this, we propose CLEAR (Cognition and Latent Evaluation for Adaptive Routing), a framework that combines ultra-fast generative planning with deep semantic reasoning. CLEAR employs Drive-JEPA as the visual encoder and replaces the multi-step denoising chain with a single-step conditional drift in a VAE latent space, introducing a conditioning coefficient to balance diversity and expert precision. Meanwhile, we fully fine-tune Qwen~3.5~0.8B on driving QA pairs to extract scene-aware hidden states. These states guide both an Adaptive Scheduler, which selects the conditioning coefficient $α$ and sample count $N$ from a discrete set of predefined schemes, and a cross-attention scorer that selects the optimal trajectory from candidates. On the NAVSIM v1 benchmark, CLEAR achieves a state-of-the-art PDMS of 93.7. Our results demonstrate that high-fidelity, multi-modal planning can be executed efficiently without dense geometric annotations or iterative sampling.

</details>


### [435] [MPCoT: Reward-Guided Multi-Path Latent Reasoning for Test-Time Scalable Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06245)
*Boyang Zhang,Lianlei Shan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对 VLA 模型在复杂控制任务中的决策能力限制，MPCoT 引入了一种在保持推理延迟不变的前提下，通过奖励引导的多路径潜在推理来增强任务决策质量的新架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型在长任务和高不确定性场景下表现脆弱，而传统的思维链（CoT）推理方法会带来额外的延迟且存在文本到动作接口的间接性。

Method: 提出 MPCoT 框架：1. 初始化 M 个潜在假设；2. 进行 K 步权重共享（weight-tied）的迭代细化；3. 通过奖励引导的路径偏好目标（训练期）对各分支进行评估与对齐；4. 在推理时执行软聚合（soft aggregation）并直接解码动作，无需额外生成推理 Token。

Result: 在 LIBERO 和 CALVIN 基准测试中，相较于基准模型，MPCoT 显著提升了长周期任务的成功率；验证了推理深度（K）和广度（M）对性能的正向影响。

Conclusion: MPCoT 通过在训练阶段引入奖励引导的路径偏好优化，在不增加推理延迟的情况下，有效提升了 VLA 模型在长期任务中的性能，证明了多路径潜在推理的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies remain brittle in long-horizon and high-uncertainty control, where one-pass action decoding provides limited inference-time deliberation. Explicit chain-of-thought can increase reasoning depth, but introduces token latency and an indirect text-to-action interface. We propose MPCoT, a reward-guided multi-path latent reasoning framework that initializes $M$ hypotheses, refines them for K weight-tied steps, and softly aggregates them before action decoding. A training-only path-preference objective evaluates candidate action branches with expert-action consistency, world-model/VLM-based progress, and success feedback to align the latent path scorer with downstream execution quality. MPCoT preserves the original 8-step action interface, generates zero reasoning tokens, and exposes configurable inference controls (K,M). Under matched protocols on LIBERO and CALVIN, MPCoT improves long-horizon performance, with ablations confirming depth-width effects, confidence-weighted aggregation, and reward-guided path supervision.

</details>


### [436] [Breaking Time: A Fully Gaussian Framework for Distributed and Continuous-Time SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06250)
*Davide Ceriola,Simone Ferrari,Luca Di Giammarino,Leonardo Brizi,Giorgio Grisetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: G-solver 是一种新型分布式连续时间 SLAM 框架，它通过结合高斯信念传播（GBP）和高斯过程（GP）先验，有效且可扩展地解决了异构异步传感器数据流的轨迹估计与多传感器融合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 SLAM 方法在处理异步、异构传感器（如滚动快门相机、激光雷达等）的非均匀数据流时存在局限性，且在大规模环境下计算复杂度和扩展性不足。为了实现更具韧性、可扩展的持续时间轨迹估计，需要一种既能融合概率先验又能实现分布式计算的统一框架。

Method: 1. 核心架构：采用 Gaussian Belief Propagation (GBP) 进行分布式消息传递，实现大规模优化；2. 轨迹表达：使用 Gaussian Process (GP) 作为运动先验，对轨迹进行平滑建模与概率化表示，支持对非均匀异步测量数据的插值；3. 处理方案：将 SLAM 问题建模为因子图形式，通过 GBP 在局部信念下进行迭代更新，无需全局同步。

Result: 1. 成功在滚动快门相机和分布式多相机数据集上实现了高质量轨迹估计；2. 在计算效率上保持了与现有最先进连续时间 SLAM 相当的实时性能；3. 验证了该方法在处理异步传感器数据时的灵活性，且无需复杂的额外工程同步。

Conclusion: G-solver 提供了一个高度可扩展、分布式的连续时间 SLAM 解决方案。利用 Gaussian Belief Propagation (GBP) 和 Gaussian Process (GP) 的结合，该系统能够高效地处理异步、非均匀采样的传感器数据，并自然地扩展到多相机协同场景，在保证稳健性的同时具备与现有主流方法相当的计算性能。

Abstract: Continuous-time SLAM provides a principled framework for fusing heterogeneous sensors while estimating smooth trajectories, and is particularly well-suited for handling heterogeneous, asynchronous sensor streams with non-uniform readout patterns, such as rolling shutter cameras, LiDAR scanners, radar sweeps, or event-based sensors. In this work, we introduce G-solver, a fully Gaussian and distributed framework that combines Gaussian Belief Propagation (GBP) with Gaussian Process (GP) motion priors for continuous-time trajectory estimation. Our GP model provides a probabilistic representation of the trajectory, enabling consistent interpolation and the use of data-driven hyperparameters, while GBP offers a scalable message-passing formulation well-suited for decentralized settings. The resulting solver naturally extends to multi-camera scenarios without specialized synchronization or engineering effort. We evaluate the approach on synthetic and real data, including rolling shutter and distributed multi-camera optimization, demonstrating accurate and stable estimation with runtimes comparable to existing continuous-time methods. An open-source implementation is released.

</details>


### [437] [Multi-Resolution Tactile Imitation Learning for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06281)
*Rickmer Krohn,Erik Helmut,Niklas Funk,Jan Peters,Vignesh Prasad,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 MiTaS 框架，通过融合不同时间分辨率的视觉与异构触觉（RGB、GelSight、Evetac）传感器，显著提升了机器人在复杂触控操作任务中的成功率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的触觉传感器种类繁多，但针对具有不同时间分辨率的异构触觉传感器的融合研究严重不足，无法充分利用多源触觉信息来解决高动态、接触复杂的操控任务。

Method: 提出 MiTaS 框架，采用模态特定的卷积提取器和基于 Transformer 的跨模态融合机制，并将融合后的特征用于条件流匹配（flow-matching）策略。

Result: 在五个接触式操控任务中，MiTaS 的平均成功率达到 80%，远高于视觉基线（31%）和视觉-触觉基线（54%）；且多模态联合训练在无需测试阶段依赖所有传感器的情况下仍能显著提升性能。

Conclusion: MiTaS 验证了多模态、多分辨率触觉感知在复杂接触式任务中的有效性，证明了不同时间分辨率传感器（RGB相机、GelSight Mini、Evetac事件传感器）的融合能显著提升模仿学习性能。

Abstract: Touch sensing is beneficial for solving a wide variety of manipulation tasks. While there exists a wide range of tactile sensors with different properties, exploiting the fusion of multiple heterogeneous tactile sensors to improve manipulation learning remains underexplored. We present Multi-Resolution Tactile Sensing (MiTaS), a representation framework that leverages multiple tactile sensors operating at different temporal resolutions in order to solve complex contact-rich manipulation tasks. We propose a novel architecture using modality-specific convolutional stems and transformer-based fusion that effectively fuses information from an RGB camera stream, a vision-based GelSight Mini sensor and a high-frequency event-based Evetac sensor. This multi-sensor representation then conditions a flow-matching policy for solving downstream tasks. Experimental results across five contact-rich manipulation tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of multi-resolution tactile features in imitation learning. MiTaS achieves an average success rate of 80 %, while vision-only (31 %) and visual-tactile (54 %) baselines cannot solve the task reliably. Co-training a visuo-tactile model with multi-tactile data boosts performance by over 10 \% in certain tasks, without having access to the Evetac sensor during policy evaluation. A detailed sensor-reading and attention analysis reveals the importance of different sensors throughout task execution, validating our multi-resolution tactile sensing approach. Project Page: http://mitas-touch.github.io.

</details>


### [438] [Attitude-Aided Linear Calibration of Triaxial Accelerometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06308)
*Yongqiang Yu,Tian Huang,Yipeng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种创新的 ALAC 校准算法，利用姿态辅助将加速度计校准转化为线性最小二乘问题，实现了高效、高精度的闭式解校准，省去了复杂迭代，特别适合低成本与在线应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统加速度计校准方法依赖昂贵的参考设备或复杂的非线性迭代优化，难以应用于低成本或需要快速在线校准的场景。

Method: 提出“姿态辅助线性加速度计校准”（ALAC）方法，通过构建组合误差矩阵（CEM）将校准模型线性化，利用约束齐次最小二乘法（CHLS）求解，仅需五组任意方向的测量值即可实现闭式解，且支持递归在线扩展。

Result: 实验证明，ALAC 在离线和在线模式下，精度和抗噪声能力均超过了现有的基准方法；在相同数据集上达到并超过了复杂的非线性迭代校准性能。

Conclusion: ALAC 提供了一种鲁棒且实用的 MEMS 加速度计校准方案，特别适用于低成本 IMU 和需要在线校准的场景。其性能在静态和动态环境下均优于传统的参考校准和迭代校准方法。

Abstract: Triaxial MEMS accelerometers are widely used for inertial sensing, navigation, and sensor fusion, but existing calibration methods often rely on costly reference setups or nonlinear iterative optimization, limiting their efficiency and applicability to low-cost or self-calibrating systems. We present attitude-aided linear accelerometer calibration (ALAC), a method that operates on any platform providing orientation information, such as turntables, robotic arms, or inertial measurement units. ALAC constructs a combined error matrix (CEM) to represent sensor errors in a unified calibration model and enables linear least-squares estimation. The bias and gravity vector are jointly estimated, implicitly accounting for platform misalignment, and matrix decomposition of the CEM recovers scale, non-orthogonality, and alignment rotation parameters. Under static gravity, calibration is formulated as a constrained homogeneous least-squares (CHLS) problem and solved in closed form using standard linear algebra. Only five arbitrarily oriented measurements are required, and a recursive extension supports online or in-field calibration. Experiments on a stationary robot-mounted accelerometer and a quasi-static public IMU trajectory show that ALAC, in both offline and online modes, outperforms reference-based and online baselines in accuracy and robustness to sensor noise. On the same dataset, it matches iterative self-calibration under filtered conditions and surpasses all evaluated baselines on raw measurements. These results demonstrate a robust and practical calibration scheme for MEMS-based inertial platforms, especially low-cost IMUs and online calibration scenarios.

</details>


### [439] [Meridian: Metric-Semantic Primitive Matching for Cross-View Geo-Localization Beyond Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06312)
*Mason Peterson,Qingyuan Li,Yixuan Jia,Fernando Cladera,Carlos Nieto-Granda,Camillo Jose Taylor,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Meridian是一种无需领域特定训练的机器人全局定位方法，通过匹配航空图像与地面RGB-D数据的语义基元，实现了在非结构化复杂自然环境下的高精度定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GNSS受限环境下，现有的基于航空影像的定位方法主要针对结构化城市环境，难以处理非结构化自然地形中常见的重复几何结构和特征稀疏问题，且对特定环境训练模型有很强依赖。

Method: 提出了Meridian方法，通过在航空图像与地面机器人RGB-D数据之间匹配高层度量-语义基元（metric-semantic primitives）。引入新型一致性度量来估计机器人子图位姿分布，并结合鲁棒的位姿图优化技术滤除异常假设，实现轨迹估计。该方法无需任何针对特定区域的训练或微调。

Result: 在自动驾驶数据集、公园、校园及野外露营地等多种环境下进行了测试，累计移动距离19公里，平均优化轨迹误差仅为2.4米。

Conclusion: Meridian通过无需针对特定区域训练的度量-语义基元匹配方法，为非结构化自然环境下的机器人全局定位提供了一种鲁棒且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Successful robot automation requires accurate global localization to support repeatability, task planning, goal specification, and safe operation. However, reliable localization in GNSS-denied environments remains an open problem. Overhead aerial imagery offers a promising solution, but existing approaches primarily target structured urban environments and have been rarely demonstrated in unstructured natural terrain. Limitations of the state-of-the-art include a reliance on models trained for specific environments, as well as difficulty handling repetitive geometries and featureless landscapes commonly found in natural outdoor areas. To overcome these challenges, we present Meridian, a method for matching high-level metric-semantic primitives across aerial images and ground robot RGB-D camera data that achieves accurate global localization and generalizes well across diverse environments, all without any training or algorithmic fine-tuning on area-specific data. We formulate novel consistency metrics to estimate a distribution over robot submap poses and to reject outlier hypotheses in a robust pose graph optimization step for accurate robot trajectory estimation. We demonstrate that our algorithm can localize a ground robot across a wide variety of environments, including an autonomous driving dataset, a park and campus area, and a wilderness camp, with an average optimized trajectory error of 2.4 m over 19 km of ground traversal.

</details>
